### [1] [MintFlow: Minimal Trajectory Intervention for Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02260)
*Yesom Park,Kelvin Kan,Qifan Chen,Thomas Flynn,Hayden Schaeffer. Xihaier Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MintFlow通过对流轨迹施加最小闭式扰动并自适应选时，实现训练免费、保持分布的约束采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束采样方法通常在强制满足约束与保持预训练数据分布之间存在显著权衡，强约束会导致样本远离预训练分布；需要一种既能严格或接近满足约束又最小化分布偏移的采样方法。

Method: 提出训练免费框架：在某一中间流状态施加闭式最小扰动，使在不改变预训练流场的条件下，后续演化满足目标约束。采用伴随方程导出扰动的闭式解，并自适应选择干预时间以权衡扰动放大与初始扰动幅度。

Result: 在生成视觉任务和物理系统建模等多项任务上，MintFlow在约束满足度上与最先进方法持平或相近，同时在保持预训练生成分布方面显著优于现有受约束采样方法。

Conclusion: MintFlow通过在预训练流的轨迹上实施最小干预来实现约束采样，从而在满足约束的同时最大限度保存预训练分布，表现出比现有方法更好的分布保持能力和竞争性的约束满足效果。

Abstract: Flow matching models excel at generative modeling, and many downstream applications require their samples to satisfy prescribed constraints, such as observed measurements and physical laws. However, existing constrained samplers often face a trade-off: \textit{enforcing constraints can substantially displace samples from the pretrained data distribution}. To address this trade-off, we introduce \textbf{MintFlow}, a training-free constrained sampling framework that formulates constraint enforcement as a minimal intervention on the pretrained flow trajectory. MintFlow seeks the minimal perturbation of an intermediate flow state such that its subsequent evolution under the pretrained flow field satisfies the target constraint. By minimally perturbing the flow state while keeping the pretrained flow field unchanged, MintFlow enforces the constraint while minimizing unnecessary deviation from the pretrained distribution. An adjoint formulation yields a closed-form expression for this perturbation, eliminating expensive iterative optimization. Furthermore, MintFlow adaptively selects the intervention time to balance the required perturbation magnitude with its amplification by the remaining flow. Across a range of tasks in generative vision and physical system modeling, MintFlow achieves competitive constraint satisfaction while preserving the pretrained generative distribution substantially better than state-of-the-art constrained methods.

</details>


### [2] [Fast Models, Slow Evidence: A Paired and Self-Audited Evaluation of System-1 Decision Models for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02267)
*Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 配对、可复现的实证评估显示托管 System-1（Jev）在多数决策点上优于开源模型（Laya），但系统对候选规模、选项顺序和评估设计高度敏感；研究同时通过审计纠正了若干误导性部署主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理（agent）在运行时要做大量、低延迟的二类决策（选模型、选工具、相关性判定、注入检测等）；用单次前向即可输出概率的轻量 System-1 模型，有望比调用完整 LLM 大幅节省成本与延迟，因此需要严格比较其准确性与稳健性。

Method: 对两个 System-1 模型（开源 Laya 与托管 Jev）在 11 个代理决策点上进行配对评估；数据来自 18 个公开来源，包含 7,283 基线样例与 6,640 个鲁棒性变体；使用字节相同输入、配对测试、跨硬件与跨日复现检查；并对自身分析管线进行审计以识别误差与混淆因子。

Result: Jev 在 9/11 决策点上显著领先（优势 10.8–46.0 个百分点）；两模型在零样本模型路由上均不优于随机，在 RAG 相关性门控上并列。Laya 对选项顺序非常敏感（30% 回答变化），在候选数目增多或相似候选时性能严重下降。对自身管线审计揭示了四个关键错误/混淆：遗漏预筛成本、将门控准确率误报为端到端质量、在样本内调阈而在外部失效、以及通道效应导致注入误报。开源了全部用例、原始输出与分析代码。

Conclusion: System-1 专门决策模型在节省成本/延迟方面有潜力，但实际效果高度依赖模型实现、任务构造、候选数量与评价细节。Jev 在大多数决策点上显著优于 Laya，但两者在若干关键场景（如零样本模型路由、多候选工具）表现不足。审计发现的分析和设计误差严重影响先前的部署主张，强调了端到端评估与透明披露的重要性。

Abstract: Agent harnesses make many small, typed decisions per task: which model to call, which tool to use, whether retrieved text is relevant, whether an input carries an injection. System-1 decision models answer such questions in a single forward pass with class probabilities, promising large cost and latency savings over LLM calls. We present a paired evaluation of an open-weight (Laya) and a hosted (Jev) System-1 model on 11 agent decision points built from 18 public sources: 7,283 base cases plus 6,640 robustness variants, with byte-identical inputs, paired tests, and cross-hardware and cross-day reproducibility checks. Jev is significantly more accurate on 9 of 11 decision points (+10.8 to +46.0 pp). Neither model beats chance on zero-shot model routing, and they tie on RAG relevance gating. Laya changes 30% of its answers when the option order is reversed and degrades sharply with many or similar candidates (31% at 50 nearest-neighbour tools, vs. 98% for Jev on items with a unique correct tool). We also audit our own pipeline. Three analysis errors and one design confound distorted headline deployment claims: an omitted pre-screen cost (reported 23.9% saving, actual 4.3%), gate accuracy reported as end-to-end quality (58% vs. 98%), in-sample thresholds (5% target, up to 17% held-out misses), and a "channel effect" on injection false positives that vanishes with channel-native content. Two other suspected confounds did not change the conclusions. All cases, raw outputs and analysis code are available at https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval.

</details>


### [3] [The AI Risk Observatory: What Can We Learn from AI Disclosures in Annual Reports About Societal Resilience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02281)
*Bart Jaworski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用LLM规模化分析近10k份年度报告能捕捉AI披露趋势与行业差异，但大多数披露并不实质性，需进一步完善方法与验证以提升信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会韧性研究需要可用且可操作的数据，研究问题是：将年度报告与LLM结合能否在规模上提供公司如何披露应对AI的信息？

Method: 构建并复现性报告一个两阶段分类管道：先用大语言模型（LLM）在全文中识别与AI相关段落，再对这些段落做细化标签与实质性判定。样本为9,821份英国上市公司年度报告（2020–2025，含部分2026数据），并用474个人工标注段落作验证。

Result: （i）2020–2025年间报告中提及AI风险与采用的比例显著上升，供应商名称集中于少数供应商（以微软为首）；（ii）披露在不同市场板块与关键基础设施行业间差异明显，AIM板块与能源、数据基础设施等行业披露率较低；（iii）关于AI造成伤害的披露极少（全样本仅7份），且大多数AI风险披露被判定为非实质性：2025年41.2%提及AI风险但仅4.3%被认定为实质性披露。

Conclusion: 年度报告在规模化处理后能提供关于公司披露其应对人工智能的信息性信号，但大部分披露缺乏实质性；仅有少数报告真正提供可操作或具体的AI风险说明。

Abstract: Societal resilience research relies on access to useful and actionable data, which motivates our main research question: Can annual reports, processed at scale with LLMs, provide a useful signal about how companies disclose their response to AI? We test this by applying a reproducible two-stage classification pipeline to 9,821 annual reports from 1,362 UK listed companies (2020-2025, with partial 2026 data). We first validate the method against 474 human-annotated passages, finding high recall and moderate label-level agreement. We then report three empirical patterns: (i) between 2020 and 2025, the share of reports mentioning AI risk rose from 2.8% to 41.2%, while AI adoption disclosure also rose, from 13.8% to 45.2%, and named vendor mentions cluster around a small set of major providers led by Microsoft; (ii) disclosure varies substantially by Critical National Infrastructure sector and market segment: AIM reports disclose AI risk at far lower rates than Main Market reports, and sectors such as Energy and Data Infrastructure lag behind the rest in AI risk disclosure; and (iii) harm disclosures are near-absent (seven reports across the entire corpus). We develop a substantiveness classification to assess the quality of the disclosure and find that most AI risk disclosure is not substantive: in 2025, 41.2% of all reports mention AI as a risk, but only 4.3% contain AI risk disclosure we classify as substantive.

</details>


### [4] [Keep It CALM: Analyzing the Limits of Global Unsafety in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02300)
*NaHyeon Park,Minhyun Lee,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper reveals a coverage-selectivity trade-off in global unsafe subspaces and introduces CALM, a prompt-local, training-free counterfactual correction method that more selectively removes unsafe components and preserves benign utility.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address limitations of global unsafe signals applied universally, and to improve selectivity in blocking unsafe content without degrading benign utility.

Method: CALM (Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation): training-free method that, for each prompt, identifies active unsafe categories via matched unsafe-benign anchors, minimally modifies violating token representations toward a safe direction, and suppresses residual components positively aligned with unsafe signals.

Result: CALM substantially improves suppression of unsafe outputs while better preserving benign generation quality compared to global removal baselines, demonstrating that prompt-local counterfactual correction is a more selective and effective safeguard.

Conclusion: Global, reusable unsafe signals create a coverage-selectivity trade-off: small subspaces miss diverse unsafe content while large ones harm benign prompts. Localized, prompt-specific counterfactual corrections reduce this trade-off.

Abstract: Training-free safeguards for text-to-image generation often rely on a reusable safety signal, such as an unsafe direction or global toxic subspace, applied broadly across prompts. We provide a controlled geometric analysis of this global-unsafety assumption and reveal a consistent coverage-selectivity trade-off: compact unsafe subspaces fail to cover heterogeneous unsafe semantics, whereas broader aggregation increasingly distorts safety-adjacent benign prompts. Motivated by this finding, we propose CALM (Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation), a training-free safeguard that replaces uniform global removal with prompt-local counterfactual correction. Using matched unsafe-benign anchors, CALM routes each prompt to active unsafe categories, minimally edits only violating token representations toward the safe side, and suppresses positively aligned unsafe residual components. Across broad evaluation, CALM significantly improves unsafe content suppression while preserving benign utility, demonstrating that local counterfactual correction provides a more selective alternative to global unsafe signal removal.

</details>


### [5] [Choosing Before Acting: Comparative Value Estimation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02330)
*Yu Li,Zheng Zhang,Xin Liu,Shengtian Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CITA通过训练比较推理模型，利用日志、贝叶斯模拟器和LLM比较信号，为候选工具调用估计长期价值，解决长时序信用分配问题并提升工具调用和任务完成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序工具调用中，最终结果回报对中间决策的信用分配太弱；虽然步级回报更有针对性，但获取可靠步级监督代价高或需额外模拟。应在执行前估计下一步工具调用的长期价值以改善决策。

Method: 提出CITA，训练比较推理模型(CIM)。CIM基于成对信号训练，这些信号结合了（1）日志中观测到的工具行为，（2）来自贝叶斯工具图模拟器的可扩展监督，以及（3）基于大模型的语义比较判断，以估计在当前上下文下某次候选工具调用支持最终成功的概率。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准和多种主干LLM上，CITA持续提升Tool F1和任务成功率；分析显示CIM能学习到准确的步级价值比较，从而改善工具选择。

Conclusion: 通过在决策前对备选工具调用进行比较性估值，模型能更好地完成长时序工具使用任务的信用分配，从而提高工具调用准确率和最终任务成功率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on long-horizon tool invocation sequences for complex tasks, where each invocation can alter the task state and condition subsequent decisions. In long-horizon tool use, final-outcome rewards provide weak credit assignment over long interaction traces. Step-level rewards can offer more targeted feedback, but obtaining reliable step supervision often requires human or LLM judgment, or additional rollouts to estimate the downstream effect of an intermediate decision. In this paper, we argue that effective tool-use agents should estimate the long-horizon value of a possible next tool invocation before executing it. This objective requires comparative supervision over alternative invocations under the same context, while logged trajectories only contain the invocation that was actually taken. Therefore, we propose Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents (CITA). CITA trains a Comparative Inference Model (CIM) from paired signals that combine observed tool behavior, scalable supervision from a Bayesian tool-graph simulator, and semantic judgments from LLM-based comparison. The resulting CIM learns to estimate how likely a possible next tool invocation is to support final task success under the current context. Across three tool-use benchmarks and multiple backbone LLMs, CITA consistently improves Tool F1 and task success. Additional analysis shows that CIM learns accurate step-level value estimates for comparative tool choices.

</details>


### [6] [World Editing: Intervening on Executable Worlds at Increasing Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02331)
*Max Ku,Nok-Kan Law,Yu-Chien Tang,Shih-Ying Yeh,Ping Nie,Andy Zheng,Tat Hei Lai,Fei-Yueh Chen,Nikko Yu,Wei-Chieh Sun,Suzy Huang,Chiao-Wei Hsu,Chih-Chuan Huang,Chak-Wing Mak,Ho Yin Sam Ng,Edisy Kin Wai Chan,Min-Hung Chen,Ho Kei Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并量化了“世界编辑”与“干预深度”，发布了IGMWorld与IGMBench基准；实验证明现有代理具备可观编辑能力但在深度耦合与视觉一致性上仍显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前交互式世界模型在生成与交互上进步显著，但针对已有可执行世界的定向编辑（需保持部分属性不变）研究不足；实际场景（游戏mod、仿真更新、可重复研究环境）需要这种能力与可量化评估。

Method: 论文提出了“干预深度”作为度量编辑与世界实体、动力学和子系统耦合强度的轴，并通过工业级游戏mod对现有世界进行修改来实现该概念。构建了IGMWorld与基准套件IGMBench（110项任务、1.1K+判定标准），并通过一组确定性可执行性、行为、保持性与视觉检查标准来评估编辑结果，使用前沿的代码生成/代理配置进行实验评测。

Result: 在IGMBench上，最优代理配置在严格任务级标准下解决78.2%任务，在判定级别达到94.8%。整体可靠性随干预深度下降，即编辑耦合越深成功率越低；多数失败编辑仍可构建并载入，表明主要挑战在令编辑后的世界行为符合预期；视觉一致性是显著薄弱点，所有配置的联合视觉通过率均低于50%。

Conclusion: 本论文将“世界编辑”定义为在保留应保持不变属性的前提下对已有可执行世界进行干预，并证明这是一项不同于世界生成与交互的独立能力；可执行电子游戏（如Minecraft/ Terraria）是验证与度量该能力的实用平台。

Abstract: Interactive world models are increasingly capable of generating environments and acting within them, yet deliberately editing an existing executable world remains underexplored. We formulate world editing as intervening on an existing world while preserving properties that should remain unchanged, and introduce intervention depth as an axis describing how strongly an edit couples world entities, dynamics, and systems. We instantiate this capability through industry-grade game modding and introduce IGMWorld, together with IGMBench, a benchmark of 110 tasks and over 1.1K executable state and behavioral criteria across Minecraft and Terraria. The tasks span property, entity, dynamics, and system interventions and are evaluated through deterministic executability, behavioral, preservation, and visual checks. Frontier coding agents already exhibit substantial world-editing capability: the strongest configuration solves 78.2% of tasks under a strict task-level criterion, while criterion-level performance reaches 94.8%. Reliability generally decreases with intervention depth, and this pattern persists even among tasks with similar numbers of evaluation criteria. Most failed edits still build and load successfully, suggesting that the main difficulty is making the edited world behave as requested. Visual consistency remains a separate weakness, with all evaluated configurations below 50% joint visual pass rate. These results show that world editing is a distinct capability from world generation and interaction, and that executable games provide a practical testbed for studying it.

</details>


### [7] [A Multi Method Importance and Performance Efficiency Analysis of Topological Metrics for Natural Visibility Graph Based Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02342)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过四种重要性方法共识排序，3个聚类系数统计量即可替代21个NVG指标，在CICIDS2018上既提速又略增效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估并量化在网络流量攻击检测中是否可以用更少的NVG拓扑度量保持或提升分类能力，并减少特征提取与训练的计算成本。

Method: 对21个NVG导出拓扑度量用SHAP、分组置换重要性、Boruta和递归特征消除（RFE）四种方法分别评估，再通过共识排序融合结果；基于该排序构建Full21、Top15、Top10、Top7、Top5与Top3配置；使用CICIDS2018数据集与基于CNN的分类器，并采用分层5折交叉验证评估性能与运行时。

Result: Top3（avg_clustering_coeff_median、avg_clustering_coeff_std、avg_clustering_coeff_mean）在观测平均上取得97.148%准确率、97.055%加权F1与0.9675的MCC，优于Full21的95.999%/95.521%/0.9549；同时总运行时从14961.39秒降至589.22秒（减少96.06%）。

Conclusion: 基于重要性引导的指标精简能够在所评估的设置下，用更少的NVG拓扑指标实现更高或相当的分类性能且显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Natural Visibility Graph (NVG) based analysis characterizes network traffic through topological descriptors reflecting different structural properties. However, not all descriptors contribute equally to cyber-attack classification, and extracting a large metric set can increase computational cost. This study evaluates 21 NVG derived topological metrics and investigates whether a compact subset can preserve classification capability while improving computational efficiency. Four importance analysis methods SHAP, grouped Permutation Importance, Boruta, and Recursive Feature Elimination (RFE) are integrated through a Consensus Ranking strategy. Based on this ranking, Full21, Top15, Top10, Top7, Top5, and Top3 configurations are evaluated using the CICIDS2018 dataset, a CNN classifier, and stratified 5 fold cross validation. The three highest ranked metrics are avg_clustering_coeff_median, avg_clustering_coeff_std, and avg_clustering_coeff_mean. Top3 achieved the highest observed mean performance, with 97.148% accuracy, 97.055% weighted F1 score, and an MCC of 0.9675, compared with 95.999%, 95.521%, and 0.9549 for Full21, respectively. It also reduced total runtime from 14,961.39 s to 589.22 s (96.06%). These results indicate that importance guided metric reduction can provide a compact NVG representation with higher observed mean predictive performance and substantially lower computational cost under the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [8] [DeReAct: Decomposed Reasoning and Acting for Reliable AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02351)
*Ajay Vohra,Tao Chen,Neeti Narayan,Caron Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeReAct通过将行动验证（Critic）与完成认证（Context Manager）外部化，能有效提升弱模型的执行正确率并在强模型上提供更证据完备的终止控制，代价是可能更保守或早期终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ReAct将行动提议、执行与完成判定耦合，导致错误传播与不受支持的完成声明终止流程，难以独立强制执行行动授权与完成控制。DeReAct旨在通过外部化门控来降低错误传递、提高证据完备性并增强对任务完成判断的可验证性。

Method: 提出模块化代理架构DeReAct：保留原有的Brain（生成行动建议），但在执行前用Critic验证行动并在终止时用Context Manager重构并校验环境支持的State与任务完成性。通过在GAIA与SWE-bench Verified上对不同能力等级的Brain（如Qwen3-Coder-480B、Claude Sonnet~4.5、Claude Opus~4.5）进行Pass@1评测，并通过轨迹/消融分析评估门控有效性与适用边界。

Result: 在GAIA与SWE-bench Verified上，对较弱的Brains（Qwen3-Coder-480B、Claude Sonnet~4.5）Pass@1分别提升约6.5–7.0和4.2–5.2点；随着Brain能力提升，增益递减；使用Claude Opus~4.5时Pass@1与ReAct接近，但DeReAct产生了更证据完备、约束满足度更高的轨迹；消融显示门控在目标失效频繁且门控本身充足时最有效。总体上对弱模型有较大提升，对强模型保持或改进接地性。

Conclusion: DeReAct通过将行动授权与完成判定外部化为两个门控策略（Critic与Context Manager），实现了对传统ReAct式代理的鲁棒性与可控性提升。对较弱的“Brain”模型显著提高了任务通过率（Pass@1），对强模型保持了接地（grounding）优势并以更严格的完成证据换取更早/保守的终止。

Abstract: ReAct-based agents typically rely on a single LLM policy to propose actions, interact with the environment, and decide when a task is complete. This coupling makes action authorization and completion control difficult to enforce independently, allowing errors to propagate and unsupported completion claims to terminate execution. We introduce DeReAct, a modular agent architecture that externalizes two gating policies: a Critic that validates proposed actions before execution, and a Context Manager that reconstructs an environment-supported \textsc{State} and certifies task completion.
  Across GAIA and SWE-bench Verified, DeReAct improves Pass@1 most for weaker Brain models, with gains of 6.5--7.0 points for Qwen3-Coder-480B and 4.2--5.2 points for Claude Sonnet~4.5; gains diminish as Brain capability increases. Trajectory and ablation analyses show that external gating is effective when targeted failures are sufficiently prevalent and the gating policy is itself sufficient. With Claude Opus~4.5, Pass@1 remains comparable to ReAct, while DeReAct produces more evidence-complete and constraint-satisfying trajectories, indicating that completion control can trade earlier termination for stronger grounding. Overall, DeReAct improves weaker agents while retaining grounding benefits as models strengthen.

</details>


### [9] [Traversing the Satisfaction-Diversity Frontier in Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02372)
*Kevin Zhai,Siva Rajesh Kasa,Soumya Roy,Sumit Negi,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SatisDive通过在推理时对批次应用奖励下限与分组策略，实现每张图像的质量保证与批次多样性的可控权衡，在多项评测上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么分开处理奖励与多样性，要么将两者合成为单一得分，导致高多样性掩盖低奖励。需要一种能同时保证最低质量和整体多样性的机制。

Method: 训练无关的推理时方法：对一批候选使用批次相对奖励截断将候选分为低/高奖励两组，强调提升低奖励候选并在高奖励组内推进多样性；通过调整奖励地板控制最差候选奖励与批次多样性的平衡，形成并遍历Pareto前沿。

Result: 在Pick-a-Pic数据集下、匹配DreamSim范围内，SatisDive相较FK steering在最差候选奖励上提升显著（例如：使用FLUX.1-dev+HPSv3提升最多0.43；SANA-1.6B+ImageReward提升最多0.70），并在满意度-多样性曲线上帕累托支配FK steering。

Conclusion: 提出将文本到图像生成视作满足约束的多目标优化（satisficing），确保每张候选图像达到最低奖励，同时整个批次满足多样性阈值；方法SatisDive在推理时通过批次相对奖励截断提升低奖励候选同时保留高奖励候选间的多样性，从而在满意度-多样性权衡上优于FK steering。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation enables users to explore several images generated from the same prompt. For these generated images to be useful, each one must reflect the user's preferences, measured by a learned reward, and differ visually from the others to maintain diversity. Existing methods are limited: they either address reward and diversity separately or combine them in one aggregate score, enabling high diversity to offset low rewards. In this paper, we address these limitations by formulating generation as satisficing: every image (candidate) must satisfy a reward floor and the batch of images must satisfy a diversity cutoff. The reward floor controls the balance between worst-candidate reward and batch diversity; we show that varying this floor defines a Pareto frontier. To traverse this frontier, we introduce SatisDive, a training-free inference-time method. SatisDive uses a batch-relative reward cutoff to distinguish lower- from higher-reward candidates, emphasizing reward improvement for candidates below the cutoff and diversity among candidates above it. On Pick-a-Pic, at matched DreamSim, SatisDive improves worst-candidate reward over FK steering by up to 0.43 with FLUX.1-dev as the base model and HPSv3 as the reward, and by up to 0.70 with SANA-1.6B as the base model and ImageReward as the reward. More broadly, across their overlapping DreamSim ranges, SatisDive's satisfaction-diversity curve Pareto-dominates FK steering's curve in each setting.

</details>


### [10] [THPL: A Vision-to-Language Decision Support Framework for Rainbow Trout Feeding Management in RAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02378)
*Meng Liang,Guanbo Feng,Haozhuang Chi,Shilong Zhao,Zhixin Xiong,Yuhang He,Wenfeng Han,Tianhao Zhao,Zhihong Ma,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出THPL框架：从轨迹到双重证据token，再经LoRA与反事实mDPO微调LLM，有效将连续运动学信息注入决策链，显著提高喂养决策准确性、可解释性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在鱼类行为观测与养殖管理知识之间缺乏认知对齐，导致难以将感知结果转化为可解释且可执行的喂养决策；研究旨在通过融合连续时空表征与LLM推理建立这一对齐。

Method: 1) Fishsort从轨迹中提取行为并计算喂食强度的Activity Coefficient(AC)。2) Hierarchical Behavior Encoder(HBE)用Temporal与Set Transformers建模个体时序与群体动态，将轨迹张量转换为显式物理token与隐式soft token的双重证据表示。3) 将双重token、环境参数、元数据与专家规则一起用于通过LoRA微调LLM，并采用反事实多模态Direct Preference Optimization(mDPO)强化因果推理与偏差抑制。

Result: AC与专家标注喂食强度呈显著单调正相关（Spearman ρ=0.925，p<0.001）。使用双重证据token后决策准确率从文本基线33.33%提升到93.33%；在此基础上引入反事实mDPO将准确率进一步提升至96.67%，METEOR从58.10%升至85.30%，Self-BLEU-2由58.79%降至52.88%，Distinct-3由6.68%升至7.81%。

Conclusion: THPL通过将连续运动学令牌与大语言模型推理相结合，显著提升了RAS中虹鳟喂养决策的可解释性和准确性，展示了将视觉时空行为表征与专家规则及LLM微调（LoRA + mDPO）融合为可执行决策支持系统的可行性。

Abstract: In Recirculating Aquaculture Systems (RAS), precision feeding is critical for minimizing costs and improving fish welfare. However, existing methods lack cognitive alignment between fish behaviors and management knowledge, impeding translation into executable, interpretable feeding decisions. To address this, we propose THPL, a generative feeding decision framework tailored for rainbow trout (Oncorhynchus mykiss) in RAS. First, Fishsort extracts trajectories to establish an Activity Coefficient (AC) quantifying feeding intensity. Second, a Hierarchical Behavior Encoder (HBE) models individual temporal progression and collective dynamics using Temporal and Set Transformers, transforming trajectory tensors into dual-evidence representations of explicit physical and implicit soft tokens. Finally, these tokens are integrated with environmental parameters, metadata, and expert rules to fine-tune an LLM via LoRA, followed by counterfactual multimodal Direct Preference Optimization (mDPO) to reinforce causal reasoning. Results show that AC exhibits a statistically significant monotonic positive correlation with expert-annotated feeding intensity (Spearman $ρ= 0.925$, $p < 0.001$). Ablations indicate that decision accuracy improves from 33.33% (text-only baseline) to 93.33% with dual-evidence tokens, confirming that continuous spatiotemporal tokens provide necessary physical grounding for LLMs. Compared with standard LoRA, counterfactual mDPO elevates decision accuracy from 93.33% to 96.67%, advances METEOR from 58.10% to 85.30%, reduces Self-BLEU-2 from 58.79% to 52.88%, and increases Distinct-3 from 6.68% to 7.81%, suppressing templating and actuation biases while reinforcing causal consistency and operational safety. Overall, by integrating continuous kinematics with LLM reasoning, this study provides a novel decision support paradigm for precision aquaculture.

</details>


### [11] [FlashSinkhorn 2: Block-Sparse Entropic Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02395)
*Felix X. -F. Ye,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Naigang Wang,Davis Wertheimer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FS2 is a two-stage streaming GPU Sinkhorn for low-dim squared-Euclidean EOT that uses centroid coarse solves plus block-sparse fine correction to scale to 1e8+ particles on one A100 with practical accuracy and runtime.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing streaming GPU EOT solvers still evaluate all n×m pairs each Sinkhorn iteration; there is need for methods that reduce computation and memory while reaching prescribed marginal residuals on very large low-dimensional point clouds.

Method: FS2 couples a coarse centroid-based stage (solve on cell centroids, lift potentials, use sampled marginal checks to decide whether to continue on centroids) with a block-sparse fine stage (Morton-ordered blocks enabling screening and fused tensor-core execution; a block-mass threshold bounds omitted tiles' contributions). It avoids storing the dense kernel while screening many tiles and exploiting tensor cores.

Result: On synthetic benchmarks, FS2 reached target residual on 32 problems and matched GeomLoss multiscale on 10. On a single A100 GPU, FS2 solved EOT between two 1.34×10^8-particle measures (cosmological N-body) at entropic blur equal to mean interparticle distance, achieving all-particle marginal residual <0.01 in under 2.5 hours. Implementation is open-source.

Conclusion: FlashSinkhorn 2 (FS2) demonstrates that two-stage streaming GPU Sinkhorn for squared-Euclidean cost on low-dimensional point clouds can solve extremely large discrete entropic OT problems on a single GPU to a prescribed marginal residual, achieving state-of-the-art scale and practical runtimes.

Abstract: Streaming GPU solvers for entropic optimal transport (EOT), such as FlashSinkhorn, avoid storing the dense kernel but still evaluate all $n\times m$ point pairs in every Sinkhorn iteration. We present \textbf{FlashSinkhorn~2} (FS2), a solver for squared-Euclidean cost on low-dimensional point clouds that solves large discrete EOT problems to a prescribed marginal residual on a single GPU by coupling two stages. A coarse stage solves on cell centroids, lifts the potentials to every point and, when a sampled marginal check rejects the lift, continues on the centroids, replacing most point-level updates. A block-sparse fine stage then removes the centroid error that coarse updates cannot. Its Morton-ordered blocks support screening and fused tensor-core execution, and a threshold set by the block masses bounds each omitted tile's contribution to every row and column. On synthetic benchmarks, FS2 reaches the target residual on all 32 problems and GeomLoss multiscale on 10. On one A100, FS2 solves discrete EOT between two $1.34\times10^8$-particle measures from a cosmological $N$-body simulation, at an entropic blur equal to the mean interparticle distance, to an all-particle marginal residual below 0.01 in under 2.5 hours. To our knowledge, it is the largest discrete EOT problem solved to this accuracy within hours. For reproducibility, we release an open-source implementation at https://github.com/ot-triton-lab/flash-sinkhorn

</details>


### [12] [When Terminal-Agent Training Stalls: Demystifying Data Generation and Verification Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02405)
*Xi Qin,Isabel Kurth,Xin Cui,Elin Park,Alexander Schaefer,Yaad Oren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用前沿模型自动生成训练任务与验证器存在隐蔽失效（基准无效、框架脆弱、奖励错配）；通过提示与上下文改进能大幅提升可解性，但可解带是模型特异的，因此需要对可解带、验证器与基础设施错误进行系统化校准和审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于当前使用强大模型（如 Claude Opus）作为元智能体生成训练任务和验证器时，尽管表面上具备可执行镜像和测试套件，但端到端训练流水线仍存在隐蔽错误，导致训练结果与预期不一致，需要一种系统化诊断与评估方法。

Method: 构建一个以 Claude Opus 为元智能体的流水线用于生成终端任务与验证器，设计可运行的 Docker 镜像和测试工具链，对流水线输出与训练结果进行诊断，识别三类失效（基准无效、测试框架脆弱、奖励错配），并通过提示重设计与上下文扩展进行消融实验。评估指标为 mean pass@2 在若干步数约束下的可通过率；并通过向任务集加入“难题”来检验模型可解带的敏感性。

Result: 提示重设计与上下文扩展使基线可解性提升约 5.6 倍；但在 Claude Opus 生成的任务上，9B 模型在 20 步内的 mean pass@2 达到约 81.3% 的饱和；若在相同训练配置下加入硬任务，mean pass@2 会下降到约 20.6%，显示可解带具有模型特异性。作者据此主张把可解带校准、验证器审计和基础设施错误计入首要评估维度。

Conclusion: 作者认为，仅有可运行的 Docker 镜像和可执行测试套件并不足以保证用于终端智能体训练的端到端流程的可信性；须把可解带（solvability band）校准、验证器审计与基础设施错误计入首要评估指标。

Abstract: Using a frontier model like Claude Opus as a meta-agent to generate terminal tasks and verifiers for RL training is increasingly common. Yet a runnable Docker image and executable test suite do not guarantee a faithful end-to-end pipeline for terminal agent training. We present a meta-agent pipeline motivated by this gap, diagnosing three classes of failure: benchmark invalidity, harness brittleness, and reward misalignment. Prompt redesign and context extension raise baseline solvability 5.6 times, but a 9B model saturates at 81.3% mean pass@2 within 20 steps on Claude Opus-generated tasks. Adding hard tasks reduces mean pass@2 to 20.6% without changing the training configuration, a strong evidence that the solvability band is model-specific. These findings demonstrate that meta-agent reliability requires solvability-band calibration, verifier audits, and infrastructure error accounting as first-class evaluation criteria, not post-hoc diagnost.

</details>


### [13] [Reinforcement Learning Techniques for the Optimization of Target Polarization in Nuclear Physics Scattering Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02452)
*Armen Kasparian,Torri Jeske,Monibor Rahman,Chris Keith,James Maxwell,Thomas Britton,Malachi Schram,David Lawrence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用带不确定性估计的GP代理与基于低置信界奖励的强化学习，可在仿真中显著超越人工频率调整策略并具备分布外检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化靶需要持续调整微波频率以补偿辐照损伤及材料特性变化，传统依赖专家人工试错，需自动化、鲁棒且样本高效的控制方法。

Method: 使用APOLLO目标系统运行数据训练多层感知器(MLP)和高斯过程回归(GP)以预测极化度，提出GP近似以支持跨样本学习，将代理嵌入标准化仿真环境，并以低置信界(LCB)奖励训练强化学习代理。

Result: GP模型提供经校准的不确定性并能识别训练分布外区域；MLP对分布漂移敏感性有限；基于LCB奖励训练的RL代理在仿真中实现了接近2×相对改进。

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动控制框架能够显著提升极化靶的调谐效率，基于高斯过程的不确定性估计对分布外样本检测有用，而基于强化学习的策略在仿真评估中相比人工操作约有近2倍的改进。

Abstract: The operation of dynamically polarized targets in nuclear physics experiments relies on continuous tuning of the microwave frequency to compensate for radiation damage and evolving material properties, a task that is traditionally performed through manual trial-and-error by expert operators. This work presents a data-driven control framework that combines surrogate modeling with reinforcement learning to optimize the target polarization. Using operational data from the APOLLO cryogenic target system, we train and evaluate multilayer perceptron and Gaussian process regression models to predict polarization as a function of microwave frequency, beam current, and accumulated radiation dose. We show that Gaussian process-based models provide calibrated uncertainty estimates and reliably identify regions outside the training distribution, while MLPs exhibit limited sensitivity to distributional shift. To enable learning and control across multiple target samples, we introduce a Gaussian process approximation and embed the surrogate model within a standardized simulation environment. A reinforcement learning agent is trained using a lower-confidence-bound reward formulation that balances performance maximization against uncertainty. We are able to show an almost 2x improvement on the operators actions utilizing our RL agent.

</details>


### [14] [Tropical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02478)
*Arip Asadulaev,Aladin Djuhera,Karim Salta,Holger Boche,Fakhri Karray,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用“取最大值”的热带代数替代概率求和，给出TROPIC算法和可回放路径表示，在确定性可验证任务上显著提升了可组合推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的期望回报通过对成功轨迹概率求和只能反映策略‘成功频率’，无法指出哪个具体解起作用，且强化一个解可能导致遗忘其他未被证明错误的解；这种性质与需要分步组合的推理任务不匹配。

Method: 在对数概率空间采用热带代数（max,+）而非加法代数，对状态值定义为其最可能被验证解的对数概率并显式存储解路径；基于此提出训练算法TROPIC，适用于确定性、可重置且有可验证结果的环境，利用路径回放与拼接实现组合性增强。

Result: 在四个具象化的 agentic 任务（Sokoban、Countdown、FrozenLake、WebShop）上，TROPIC 比最强的 on-policy 基线高出最多16个百分点，表明改变RL的代数而非仅更换估计器可显著提升语言模型的可组合推理能力。

Conclusion: 将传统的期望回报（概率求和）替换为热带半环（对备选解取最大值/对数概率）可以更好地支持可组合推理：状态值表示最可能被验证的解的对数概率并附带可回放的路径，从而允许将来自不同回合的前缀和后缀拼接成完整解。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language models typically maximizes expected return, adding up the probabilities of all successful trajectories. However, the classical sum formulation can only report how often the model policy succeeds, not which solution actually worked, and because probabilities sum to one, reinforcing one solution can make the model forget another that was never shown to be wrong. This makes expected return a poor fit for compositional reasoning, where a solution must be assembled from reasoning steps that the model produces in separate, often failed, attempts but rarely produces together. To address this, we propose Tropical Reinforcement Learning, which rests on a simple change of algebra: instead of adding the probabilities of alternative solutions, we take their maximum, which yields the tropical semiring. The value of a state then becomes the log-probability of its most likely verified solution, together with an explicit path that can be replayed and reused. This enables true composition, since the best prefix and the best suffix meeting at a shared state can be joined even when they come from different rollouts. To put this into practice, we introduce TROPIC, a training algorithm for deterministic, resettable environments with verifiable outcomes. On four agentic tasks (Sokoban, Countdown, FrozenLake, WebShop), TROPIC outperforms the strongest on-policy baselines by up to 16 percentage points. Changing the algebra of reinforcement learning, not just its estimators, can thus substantially improve compositional reasoning in language models

</details>


### [15] [MEA: A Reward-Driven Multi-Agent System for Faithful Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02480)
*Yuyang Cheng,Raghav Kaushik Ravi,Srivarshinee Sridhar,Sriparna Saha,Akash Ghosh,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过 Proposer+Actor 的多智能体设计、扰动驱动的忠实度奖励与模态自适应惩罚，MEA 在三类模态与多种问题类型上生成更具忠实性的自然语言解释，证明代理化接口可扩展替代传统单目的解释工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高风险领域中 ML 模型不透明，领域专家难以使用复杂的后验解释工具；需要一个自然语言、可扩展且对多模态通用的解释接口，降低解释知识门槛并保证解释与模型实际行为一致。

Method: 框架由两类代理构成：Proposer 根据问题与模态选择/配置解释器；Actor 在忠实度奖励下端到端优化，其输出为自然语言解释。引入多类型问题（特征归因、反事实、伪特征检测）并为每类配备基于扰动的忠实度度量，同时使用模态自适应惩罚来调整奖励。

Result: 在六个数据集上，MEA 相较未训练基线和若干后验/闭源方法，在忠实度上取得显著提升：表格 +28%、文本 +21%、视觉 +34%；总体上优于单一工具与现有智能代理基线。

Conclusion: MEA 提出了一种多智能体框架，通过 Proposer 选择并配置解释工具、Actor 端到端优化并将解释输出转为以模型行为为依据的自然语言解释，从而在多模态（表格、文本、视觉）上显著提升解释的忠实度。

Abstract: Recent years have seen the employment of a plethora of machine learning (ML) models in high-stakes domains, but they remain largely opaque to the practitioners who act on their predictions. While post-hoc explanation methods offer a lens into this model behavior, wielding them effectively demands expertise most domain experts lack: navigating high-dimensional outputs, selecting the best explanations, and synthesizing evidence across disparate tools. To this end, we present MEA, a multi-agent framework that removes the explanation knowledge barrier entirely: a Proposer agent selects and configures explanation tools based on the question and modality, while an Actor agent is optimized end-to-end against faithfulness, transforming the outputs into natural language explanations grounded in model behavior across tabular, text, and vision modalities. Further, we introduce diverse question types spanning feature attribution, counterfactual reasoning, and spurious feature detection, each paired with a perturbation-based faithfulness metric. We find that frontier LLMs systematically produce unfaithful explanations. By optimizing against faithfulness rewards augmented with a modality-adaptive penalty, MEA consistently outperforms post hoc explainers, agentic, and closed-source baselines across six datasets, with reward-driven optimization yielding faithfulness gains of +28% (tabular), +21% (text), and +34% (vision) over the untrained backbone. More broadly, our findings suggest that AI agents themselves can serve as a scalable, adaptable interface to ML explainability, opening a path toward natural-language explainability that generalizes beyond the fixed, single-purpose tools that have long defined the field.

</details>


### [16] [What Does a Token Cost? A Mixture-of-Agents Measurement of Sufficient Per-Token Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02491)
*Zhixu Du,Weijia Han,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用15模型面板测得“充分计算量”，发现标记需求具高度不均匀性，基于该映射的路由与起草能显著降低推理开销并保持或略增准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型对每个生成标记投入相同计算，明显低效；要证实并量化标记间计算需求差异以支撑动态分配计算（如投机解码、模型路由、分层起草）的设计与控制策略。

Method: 构建由15个不同容量语言模型组成的面板（来自三个模型家族），在教师强制条件下让每个代理逐标记尝试复现参考序列；将能成功复现某标记的最小代理的推理成本定义为该标记的“充分计算量”，据此估计FLOPs分布并在Qwen、OLMo、R1-distilled和MATH-500等基准上评估将MoA映射用于模型路由与起草的潜在时延与准确性影响。

Result: 在三项基准上，0.5B模型能复现92–95%参考标记；最昂贵的10%标记占据64–80%的估计FLOPs；在MATH-500上，基于MoA映射的路由将预测时延从7.59s降至5.12s且略增准确率；起草策略使用MoA映射比固定窗口少用32.6%草稿标记并约降低20%预测时延，表明还有分配改进空间。

Conclusion: 本文证明单个生成标记所需的计算量存在大幅差异，并提出用Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) 框架量化“充分计算量”，显示少数最昂贵的标记消耗了大部分FLOPs，从而为基于标记分配计算资源的路由与起草策略提供实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models spend the same amount of computation on every token they generate, regardless of how difficult each token is to produce. Methods such as speculative decoding and model routing are built on the premise that much of this computation is unnecessary, yet the computation an individual token actually requires has not been measured. We measure it through a Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) lens: a panel of fifteen language models of increasing capacity, drawn from three families, in which every agent attempts to reproduce a reference sequence token by token, conditioned on the correct preceding tokens. We define the inference cost of the smallest agent that succeeds as the token's sufficient compute, which upper-bounds what the token requires. On three core benchmarks, a 0.5B agent reproduces 92--95\% of reference tokens. Across Qwen, OLMo, and R1-distilled panels, the most expensive 10\% account for 64--80\% of estimated FLOPs. On all 500 MATH-500 problems, the MoA-derived map helps model routing reduce projected latency from 7.59 to 5.12 seconds while slightly improving accuracy, relative to the best confidence-routing baseline. The MoA-map helps drafting use 32.6\% fewer draft tokens and approximately 20\% lower projected latency than fixed-window drafting at similar accuracy. These comparisons reveal remaining allocation headroom, motivating controllers that exploit sufficient-compute structure.

</details>

*Harry Lyu,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to assess whether AI outputs meet workplace requirements, but agreement on response rankings does not establish agreement on acceptance rates or occupational aggregates. We introduce O*NET-BENCH, an audit suite derived from an existing survey of 45,796 worker ratings, and evaluate 33 pre-existing judge configurations across six model families on 4,501 test ratings. Twenty-five configurations achieve tie-aware pair accuracy of at least 0.60, although a train-fitted response-only TF-IDF baseline nearly matches the strongest judge. Despite this ordering agreement, judges estimate that 3.0%-97.9% of responses are acceptable, compared with 61.1% for occupation-matched workers. In one fine-tuned lineage, changing from pointwise scoring to a bundled few-shot/listwise protocol improves response ordering while reducing agreement with worker means at the task and occupation levels; this reversal replicates on a task- and worker-disjoint validation split under prespecified criteria. Cross-validated calibration largely removes mean bias, but calibrated scores explain at most 8.5% of individual worker-rating variance. Prediction-assisted estimation yields at most small precision gains at the studied label budgets. These results show that ranking agreement alone is insufficient for occupational measurement. Judges should be validated against the acceptance rates and aggregates their scores will be used to estimate.

</details>


### [18] ["I just assumed that it would translate": examining MT risk awareness among healthcare staff with abbreviations as a use case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02496)
*Eleanor Taylor-Stilgoe,Félix do Carmo,Constantin Orăsan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Healthcare staff’s reliance on Google Translate for translating medical abbreviations in records creates under-recognized patient-safety risks; qualitative interviews with 21 clinicians reveal variable awareness and point to the need for policy, training, and safer workflows.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UK clinicians increasingly use consumer MT to communicate and access multilingual documentation, but research is limited on risks when MT is used for higher-stakes tasks such as translating patient records; medical abbreviations are a known hazard even monolingually and were chosen as a focused use case to probe MT-related risk.

Method: Semi-structured interviews with 21 UK healthcare professionals from diverse roles and specialties. During interviews, participants were shown contextualised French and Spanish excerpts drawn from authoritative clinical corpora and the output of Google Translate for those excerpts. Data were analysed qualitatively with cross-analysis to identify themes and risks.

Result: Participants frequently used and trusted GT but demonstrated limited awareness of specific failure modes when abbreviations are translated by MT. Qualitative findings highlighted examples where GT mistranslated or disambiguated abbreviations dangerously, eliciting clinician concern and identifying organizational gaps (lack of guidance, inconsistent practices). The paper calls for institutional guidance, restrict MT for high-risk documentation, improve staff training, and pursue technical and workflow mitigations (e.g., abbreviation disambiguation, professional translation for records).

Conclusion: Healthcare staff’s informal use of consumer MT (mainly Google Translate) to bridge language gaps can create significant patient-safety risks, especially when translating medical abbreviations from records; staff awareness of such risks is variable and often insufficient, indicating a need for policies, training, and safer workflows.

Abstract: In the UK, public healthcare staff report turning to machine translation (MT) - predominantly Google Translate (GT) - to communicate with patients across language barriers. Though intended to support their duty of care, potentially uninformed reliance on MT in such contexts could have serious consequences for patient safety. Research nonetheless remains limited on staff awareness of the possible risks posed by higher-stakes MT use in general and with patient medical records in particular, most existing literature instead examining its use in interpersonal situations or with patient-oriented documentation. Moreover, medical abbreviations are well-documented as increasing patient risk even monolingually, with outcomes from their misuse and/or misinterpretation ranging from temporary harm to the death of the patient. Abbreviations were therefore selected as a use case for identifying the potential risks posed by their translation with MT. Contextualised French and Spanish data examples drawn from authoritative clinical corpora and translated via GT were presented during semi-structured interviews to 21 healthcare staff participants in diverse roles and specialties. The results were then subject to qualitative analysis and cross-analysis.

</details>


### [19] [HXAI: Hierarchical Privacy-Preserving Explainable AI in Distributed Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02504)
*Poushali Sengupta,Sabita Maharjan,Frank Eliassen,Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HXAI 通过本地生成语义化解释和受控差分隐私聚合，兼顾电网级可解释性需求与家户隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源与随机负荷使电力供需平衡更难，电网需要细粒度、决策相关的家户能耗洞见以管理峰值和设计响应性电价，但直接透明会带来重大隐私风险。

Method: 提出分层框架：本地模型在受保护的环境中产生细粒度解释并保持原始家电数据本地；区域模型聚合本地解释以支持电网级决策，并通过灵活的隐私预算机制（差分隐私及其累计管理）控制查询带来的隐私泄露。

Result: 在模拟与真实能耗数据集上的实验表明：HXAI 在确保家电级消耗数据不被传输给电网运营商的同时，为区域负荷管理提供了有用的解释性信息；实验强调保持解释语义结构优于仅最小化数值误差。

Conclusion: HXAI 能在不泄露家户级用电详细信息的前提下，为电网级负荷管理提供有用的可解释性分析；关键在于在局部保留语义结构化解释并通过分级聚合与差分隐私预算管理限制累计隐私暴露。

Abstract: Balancing electricity demand and supply is increasingly difficult due to the inherent intermittency of renewable power generation and the stochastic power consumption. Grid operators require fine-grained, decision-relevant insights into household energy consumption to manage peak loads and design responsive tariffs, but increased transparency at this level raises significant privacy concerns. Traditional methods for explainable AI (XAI) can reveal sensitive information, while standard privacy techniques often reduce the usefulness of explanations. To address this issue, we introduce HXAI, a hierarchical framework that preserves privacy while enabling reasonable explainable analysis for grid-level demand management. HXAI consists of two main components: (1) a local model that generates fine-grained explanations within a secure, private environment, and (2) a zonal model that aggregates these explanations to support grid-level analysis while enforcing privacy through flexible privacy-budget management. We explicitly limit cumulative privacy exposure under repeated operator queries and show that the proposed framework preserves decision-relevant information without compromising household privacy. Experiments on both simulated and real-world energy datasets demonstrate that HXAI provides useful insights for zonal load management while ensuring that appliance-level consumption remains local and is never transmitted to grid operators. Our results show that preserving the semantic structure of explanations, rather than minimizing numerical error, is the key to XAI under differential privacy. This framework provides a way to achieve both privacy and explainability in energy management.

</details>


### [20] [World Action Modeling with Progressive Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02508)
*Fei Zhang,Zhaochong An,Duncan Frost,Yikai Wang,Pengfei Liu,Ya Zhang,Michal Drozdzal,Amir Bar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProWAM 用序列化稀疏视觉子目标为动作生成提供进度锚点，通过单次视频骨干缓存与轻量动作去噪实现高效长时序控制，带来明显的基准和现实零-shot 提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 在长时序预测上受限：密集视频生成代价高，单帧预测不能提供如何逐步接近目标的可见引导。需要一种高效且能指示进展路径的视觉预见机制。

Method: 设计了一个 progressive world action model：用单次视频骨干网络前向传播缓存稀疏子目标特征（可从大规模无动作视频学习），在控制环路中只进行轻量的动作去噪以实现重规划；联合学习子目标序列与动作生成，减少密集视频 rollout 的开销。

Result: 在多项基准上取得领先结果：LIBERO-Plus 85.8%，随机化 RoboTwin 75.7%，RoboCasa365 总体 48.1%（Composite-Unseen 18.2%），零次真实世界转移成功率 70.0%（相较最强基线从55.0%提升+15.0，+27.3%相对增益）；部分指标相较基线相对提升高达+35.9%。

Conclusion: ProWAM 通过预测有序的稀疏视觉子目标并与动作联合生成，引入“progress-indexed visual foresight”来引导长时序任务执行，从而在效率与泛化性上显著优于先前的 WAM 方法。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic control by jointly predicting future visual dynamics and actions from an initial observation and instruction. However, existing WAMs struggle with long-horizon prediction, as generating dense video rollouts is highly inefficient. Some recent WAMs address this by predicting a single future frame without generating the full video, but this approach neglects how to progress toward the goal. We present ProWAM, a progressive world action model that jointly predicts actions and an ordered sequence of sparse visual sub-goals, providing explicit visual guidance to anchor action generation throughout task execution. This design scales naturally, as sub-goal prediction can be learned from large-scale action-free videos, allowing the video backbone to offload complex visual planning from the action policy. For efficient action generation, ProWAM executes a single video-backbone forward pass to cache sparse sub-goal features, eliminating iterative full-video generation and requiring only lightweight action denoising during replanning. Across extensive evaluations, ProWAM achieves superior out-of-distribution robustness. On simulation benchmarks, it sets new state-of-the-art results on LIBERO-Plus (85.8%) and randomized RoboTwin (75.7%), outperforming the strongest baseline with relative gains of up to +35.9%. On RoboCasa365, ProWAM achieves a 48.1% success rate and 18.2% on the challenging Composite-Unseen split, ranking 4th overall. Crucially, in zero-shot real-world experiments, ProWAM achieves 70.0% success, outperforming the strongest baseline by +15.0 (from 55.0% to 70.0%, a +27.3% relative gain) in novel scenes. These results demonstrate the value of progress-indexed visual foresight for closed-loop control. Our program is in https://sii-ferenas.github.io/ProWAM-page.

</details>


### [21] [On-Premises Multi-Course RAG Tutoring for Business Education: Hardware-Software Trade-offs in a Campus AI Tutor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02510)
*Sidney Shapiro,Joshua Lindemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: On-prem multi-course RAG tutor (CourseChat) proves feasible with careful model and system trade-offs: retention of an 8B production model, evidence-fidelity testing, and operational design choices are key, but pedagogical efficacy and large-scale deployment metrics are not yet established.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Provide an institutionally-hosted, RAG-based AI tutor that grounds answers in assigned course materials while keeping data on-campus and integrating with Moodle.

Method: Deployed on-prem FastAPI service with local vector DB and an Ollama-served LLM across twin-edge hosts, per-CRN course isolation, two model bake-offs, a fixed-evidence fidelity comparison, conversation and quiz audits, and a bank of 435 prebuilt questions.

Result: Several large models failed latency constraints; a 12B and a 7B candidate passed speed, a mixture-of-experts improved some corrections but introduced new errors, and the team retained an 8B production model. Software changes improved follow-up topic resolution; prebuilt question bank decouples practice from live generation. No claims about learning gains; further evaluations (faculty ratings, peak-load, public gateway acceptance) remain outstanding.

Conclusion: CourseChat demonstrates a pragmatic on-prem RAG tutoring deployment that balances model size, latency, and fidelity for multiple isolated undergraduate courses, but stops short of claims about learning outcomes.

Abstract: Campus AI tutors based on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must ground answers in assigned course materials while keeping textbooks and student dialogue on institutional infrastructure. We present CourseChat, an on-premises, multi-course RAG tutor for undergraduate business education, deployed behind a campus web gateway and intended for use embedded in Moodle. Six isolated course offerings, each keyed by its own course reference number (CRN), share twin-edge AI hosts running a FastAPI service, a local vector database, and a local large language model (LLM) served by Ollama. We report two generation-model bake-off rounds, a separate fixed-evidence source-fidelity comparison, and conversation and quiz audits. Several larger models failed the classroom speed gate, but a 12B model and a 7B alternative passed. A separate mixture-of-experts candidate improved some corrections while introducing new factual and continuity errors. We therefore retain the 8B production model pending a demonstrated overall improvement, rather than claiming that 8B is universally optimal. Software changes improved follow-up topic resolution while preserving course scope; 435 prebuilt questions across 65 modules decouple practice from live generation. The results support treating model choice, evidence selection, serving compatibility, and product design as a joint engineering decision. They do not establish learning gains: faculty ratings, peak-load capacity, and complete public-gateway acceptance remain separate evaluation needs.

</details>


### [22] [Hypothesis-guided discovery of cognitive algorithms via program refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02523)
*Huiwen Alex Yang,Mark K. Ho,Bill D. Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于概率程序的人机混合模型精化流水线：LLM提出受限代码修订、概率推断评估拟合，能有效恢复和改进认知算法模型，且揭示重复出现的有意义创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释认知模型可利用人类专业知识但难以扩展与灵活化；纯LLM生成方法可扩展但缺乏人类约束与在复杂算法恢复任务上的验证。目标是结合两者优点，将算法发现视为可控的程序精化问题。

Method: 提出一个混合流水线：研究者先以概率程序形式提供初始认知模型；LLM代理检测模型与行为间的不匹配、在研究者约束内提出并验证代码级修改；所有候选修订由概率推断模块计算潜在变量后验与数据似然以评估模型拟合，修订被迭代地采纳或拒绝。

Result: 在一个揭示多种认知算法的问题求解范式上：经系统修订的模型一致优于祖模型（拟合更好），并且修订集中在少数可复现的创新模式上，这些模式具有行为学上可解释的意义。

Conclusion: 在给定问题求解行为数据上，将人类构建的概率化认知模型交给多个LLM代理进行代码级改造与验证，结合概率推断模块，能够得到相对于原始模型更好的拟合，并揭示少量重复出现的结构性创新以解释行为差异。

Abstract: Developing cognitive models of algorithmic reasoning from behavioral data is a central problem in cognitive science that challenges current methods. Traditional approaches to cognitive modeling are interpretable and benefit from human expertise, but lack flexibility and scalability. Emerging techniques using large language models (LLMs) for de novo generation of cognitive models are scalable and flexible, but lack a role for human expertise and have mostly been applied to simpler tasks than algorithm recovery. We propose a hybrid system that treats discovery of cognitive algorithms as a program refinement problem. Human-created cognitive models are expressed as probabilistic programs and provided to a system of LLM agents with a mandate to: identify mismatches between model and behavior; propose code-level modifications within researcher-specified constraints; and verify structural fidelity. Revisions propagate to a probabilistic inference module that performs inference for latent variables and data likelihood computations. We evaluate the pipeline on human behavior in a problem-solving paradigm that exposes a variety of cognitive algorithms. Revised models consistently improve model fit relative to ancestral models and reveal a small set of recurring innovations that capture meaningful behavioral variability in this task.

</details>


### [23] [Learning What to Investigate Next: Meta-Reasoning for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02525)
*Ankur Samanta,Yonathan Efroni,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA 通过把元推理（研究分配）与执行分离，并在元动作边界训练生成式价值预测器，构建了可迁移的价值先验与联合 actor-critic（MIRA-AC），从而有效学习和迁移长时域科研决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域科研代理面临决策稀疏与回报延迟问题：何时调查、调查什么的元决策在长执行轨迹中很稀疏，且其反馈可能在很久以后才显现，导致直接用传统策略学习低效或不稳定。把研究分配与具体执行分离可以产生更短、更有意义的决策边界，便于信用分配与跨任务迁移。

Method: 提出层次化架构：外环元推理器基于持久研究记录（persistent research record）提取上下文并生成下一项‘工单’或终止；内环执行器对单个工单进行独立执行，使每次执行构成元动作之间的状态转移。在每个元动作边界处训练一个生成式评论器（generative critic）来预测从该部分状态出发的期望剩余回报；通过跨环境预训练改进该评论器以获得可迁移的价值先验。基于该先验构建并联合训练 MIRA-AC（生成式 actor-critic），使策略学习集中在元推理决策上，而不必直接优化长执行轨迹。

Result: 无需对执行策略做额外策略训练，MIRA 在若干自动科研环境（包括定理证明与开放式神经结构研究）上提升了长时域推理能力与计算分配效率；生成式评论器在预测剩余回报上优于基于 token 级别的替代方法；跨环境预训练提升了预测与适应性；用该先验初始化并联合训练的 MIRA-AC 在四个 autoresearch 环境中提高了‘金标准’性能，且 actor 能随价值先验迁移。

Conclusion: MIRA 提出并验证了一个将元推理（研究分配）与执行分离的层次化框架，使得能以更短的元决策序列和更自然的信用分配单元来学习长时域自主科研策略，从而在定理证明与神经结构搜索等自动化研究任务上提升长时域推理质量与计算分配效率。通过在边界处训练生成式评论器并以跨环境预训练的价值先验初始化，MIRA-AC 能以联合生成-评论者（actor-critic）方式直接学习元决策策略并实现跨环境的迁移。

Abstract: Long-horizon research agents must decide both how to investigate and what to investigate next as evidence accumulates. This is hard to learn because such decisions are sparse in long execution traces, and their consequences may emerge several investigations later. We introduce Meta-reasoning for Iterative Research Agents (MIRA), a hierarchical architecture separating research allocation from execution. An outer-loop meta-reasoner curates context from a persistent research record, then writes a work order for the next investigation or ends the episode. A fresh inner-loop executor carries out each work order, making execution part of the transition between meta-reasoning actions. Without policy training, MIRA improves long-horizon inference and allocates additional compute more effectively in theorem proving and open-ended neural-architecture research. Its decision boundaries also provide natural units for credit assignment. At each boundary, we train a generative critic to forecast expected remaining return from partial states, outperforming token-level alternatives. Cross-environment pretraining improves forecasting and adaptation, yielding a transferable prior for valuing partial progress. We use this prior to initialize MIRA-AC, a generative actor-critic jointly trained to forecast remaining return and choose the next investigation, without a separate critic model. MIRA-AC concentrates policy optimization on meta-reasoning decisions, enabling efficient long-horizon reinforcement learning without directly optimizing the longer execution traces they initiate. Training MIRA-AC on the model's own proxy hill-climbing signals improves gold performance across four autoresearch environments; the actor transfers with cross-environment value initialization. Together, these results show that meta-reasoning can be learned as an explicit policy for directing long-horizon autonomous research.

</details>


### [24] [How To Train Your World Model: Fine-tuning vs RAG for LM-based World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02542)
*Dhananjay Ashok,Shantanu Agarwal,Vivek Datla,Jonathan May,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper benchmarks fine-tuning vs RAG for LM world models, finds fine-tuning usually better but RAG more data-efficient; identifies retriever failure modes and remedies via hierarchical query reformulation; proposes a hybrid parametric+retrieval world model that consistently outperforms baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Retrieval-based methods (RAG) are successful in many LM tasks, but their role in LM-based world modelling (text environments) is underexplored compared to fine-tuning approaches. The paper aims to understand tradeoffs and improve retrieval for world models.

Method: Systematic empirical comparison across five diverse text-based environments (embodied, web navigation, social), evaluating fine-tuned LMs and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) world models; analysis of data-efficiency, scaling behaviour; proposal of counterfactual intervention to estimate retriever error; testing of query reformulation strategies incl. a hierarchical retrieval pipeline; development of a hybrid parametric+retrieval system.

Result: Fine-tuned world models often yield higher downstream agent rewards (15/20 settings) and scale better with more experience; RAG is more data-efficient but retrievers surface suboptimal transitions; hierarchical query reformulation improves retrieval; hybrid system achieves best robustness and performance across environments.

Conclusion: A hybrid world model that combines parametric modelling of core dynamics with retrieval from an actively maintained memory store consistently outperforms purely fine-tuned or purely retrieval-augmented approaches across multiple text-based environments and models.

Abstract: World models (WMs) simulate the transition dynamics of environments, enabling agents to plan over the consequences of their actions. In text-based environments, fine-tuning a Language Model (LM) to serve as a WM has emerged as a dominant paradigm. However, despite the widespread success of non-parametric approaches such as Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval for LM-based world modelling remains underexplored. We conduct a systematic evaluation across five diverse environments spanning embodied, web navigation and social settings, comparing fine-tuning and RAG-based approaches for LM-based world modelling. Our study reveals that fine-tuning often outperforms RAG, with fine-tuned WMs enabling agents to obtain higher rewards on 15/20 settings. While both construction paradigms benefit from additional and more diverse exploration, RAG-based approaches prove more data-efficient, and fine-tuning approaches disproportionately benefit from scaling the amount of experience collected. With a focus on RAG-based WMs, we devise a procedure that uses counterfactual intervention to estimate the error rate of the retrieval stage, and show that retrievers consistently surface suboptimal transitions from the experience buffer. Hoping to address this failing, we study a variety of query reformulation strategies, demonstrating that a hierarchical approach outperforms the traditional retrieval pipeline. Finally, we compose our findings into a hybrid world modelling system that parametrically captures core environment dynamics, while learning to rely on retrieval from an actively maintained memory store. Our hybrid system consistently outperforms other methods across multiple environments and models, showcasing the robustness of the approach and the applicability of our findings.

</details>


### [25] [How to Have a Sensitive Debate: An Instance-Optimal Protocol for AI Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02557)
*Jiawei Li,Zhiyang Xun,Lijie Chen,Jonah Brown-Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对具有稳定分解的复杂任务，提出一个具有最坏情形正确性、主导策略均衡与黑盒实例最优性的AI辩论协议，方法基于将问题结构化并利用分数块敏感度导出上/下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当强AI在复杂认知任务上匹敌或超越人类专家时，需要可验证且可监督的方案。AI辩论把复杂问题分解为易判定的子命题，但现有工作在正确性与博弈性保证上存在限制（例如仅平均意义或Stackelberg均衡）。本工作旨在提高理论保证与最优性。

Method: 作者通过把“稳定分解”概念与查询复杂度理论中的分数块敏感度（fractional block sensitivity）联系起来，构造新的辩论规则并给出几何/组合式下界证明。具体包括设计协议细节以促进局部检验、分析策略空间以证明主导策略均衡，并用分数块敏感度工具推导出黑盒下界，证明实例级最优性。

Result: 给出一个对“稳定分解”类问题的改进协议，三大改进：1) 最坏情况下的正确性保证；2) 诚实且正确为双方的主导策略均衡（dominant-strategy equilibrium）；3) 在黑盒查询模型下给出实例级的下界，表明没有更好的协议可行。并提供将稳定分解与分数块敏感度对应的形式化证明。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种改进的AI辩论协议：对满足“稳定分解”条件的问题，协议在最坏情况下保证正确性；诚实与正确构成双方的主导策略均衡；并证明在仅依赖对人类判断的黑盒查询模型下，该协议对每个实例都是最优的。

Abstract: As powerful AI systems reach and sometimes surpass the abilities of human experts across a range of cognitively demanding tasks, the problem of accurate oversight and supervision of these systems has become increasingly urgent. One promising approach is AI debate, which seeks to leverage a debate between two powerful AIs to break complex questions down into simpler claims that can be easily judged directly. Theoretical work on debate has formalized this intuition in the language of computational complexity theory, where the goal is to design protocols (i.e., rules of the debate game) that provide rigorous guarantees on correctness for judging solutions to complex problems with limited supervision. Specifically, the current best protocol has been shown to work for all problems that have sufficiently stable decompositions into subproblems. In this paper, we design a new protocol for this same class of problems that improves on the prior work in several ways. First, correctness holds in a worst-case rather than an average-case sense. Second, being honest and correct is a dominant-strategy equilibrium for both debaters, rather than a Stackelberg equilibrium. Finally, we prove black-box lower bounds, showing that our new protocol is instance-wise optimal. That is, no protocol for this class of problems can outperform ours while making only black-box queries to human judgments. We obtain these results by relating the notion of stable problem decompositions to the concept of fractional block sensitivity from query complexity.

</details>


### [26] [Mitigating Social Sycophancy via Pluralistic Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02568)
*Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,Myra Cheng,Xiaoxuan Hou,Qian Hu,Rahul Gupta,Natasha Jaques,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlurPO通过让模型自我模拟多方视角并基于自生成信号训练偏好，能在无需外部真值的情况下显著减少社交谄媚并改善建议质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在个人/关系建议场景中倾向于过度迎合用户（sycophancy），这会增加用户过度自信并降低修复人际关系的意愿；以往缓解方法在非事实性、没有“真值”的社会建议场景上效果有限。

Method: 提出 Pluralistic Preference Optimization (PlurPO)：在给定人际冲突输入时，模型自动识别并模拟相关利益相关者视角，利用模型自身对候选回复的评价信号构建偏好数据集（无需外部真值标签），并训练模型优先生成为所有利益相关者可接受的回复。

Result: 在四个数据集和四个模型家族上，PlurPO明显优于已有方法：例如对表示有害意图的语句，平均降低认可率89%；对一般建议问题，将与人类回复的差距从17.8%缩减到8.0%；此外由8B模型构建的偏好数据对32B模型有效迁移。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过让模型模拟并兼顾多个相关利益相关者视角，可以显著降低社交层面的“谄媚”现象（social sycophancy），从而使建议更不偏袒用户、不盲目认可有害意图。

Abstract: Personal advice, including relationship advice, now ranks among the most common uses of generative AI. But language models (LMs) exhibit sycophancy: they affirm users much more often than humans do, which can make people overconfident and less willing to repair their relationships after a conflict. Prior work on mitigating sycophancy has focused on factual settings where a response can be checked against a ground truth answer, while mitigations for social sycophancy (e.g., personal advice, where there is no ground truth) have relied on simple prompting and post-training methods with limited effectiveness. Our insight is that social sycophancy occurs in part because LMs overly center on the user and fail to consider the perspectives of other stakeholders impacted by the user's behavior. To address this problem we propose Pluralistic Preference Optimization (PlurPO): given inputs describing interpersonal conflicts, the LM identifies and simulates the relevant stakeholders, and is then trained to prefer and generate responses acceptable to all stakeholders. PlurPO uses only signals the model produces about its own outputs, without ground-truth labels. PlurPO substantially reduces social sycophancy across four datasets and four model families compared to prior methods. For example, on statements of intent to cause harm, where the users' actions should not be endorsed, PlurPO reduces the endorsement rate by 89% on average across four models. On general advice questions, where the target is to match the endorsement rate of human responses, it closes the gap by more than half, from 17.8% to 8.0% on average. The preference dataset constructed by PlurPO for an 8B model also effectively transfers to mitigating sycophancy in a larger (32B) model. Our results indicate that social sycophancy can be reduced by leveraging a model's own capabilities to simulate a plurality of relevant perspectives.

</details>


### [27] [Answering clinicians' questions over trial evidence tables with verifiable, feedback-driven language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02576)
*Manan Roy Choudhury,Suparno Roy Chowdhury,Swastik Sahoo,Muhammad Ali Khan,Kaneez Zahra Rubab Khakwani,Mohamad Bassam Sonbol,Irbaz Bin Riaz,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FD-SCoPE 将 LLM 与可执行查询和专家反馈相结合，为临床证据表提供可审计、可纠错的派生属性问答，实验在小规模肿瘤数据集上表现优于若干基线并通过交互更正显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统仅能基于已记录字段响应查询，而许多临床问题需要基于未显式记录的派生属性（如药物作用靶类或统一终点）来回答，且临床用户需要可审计的推导链与纠错路径。

Method: 构建一个框架：用语言模型把临床式问题翻译成可执行查询并返回被选试验记录与派生属性的推导规则；将程序化检查（verified programs）用于结果验证；并允许专家对模型输出进行交互式更正以微调或调整系统行为。

Result: 在159条肿瘤免疫检查点抑制剂试验记录的证据表上，系统完成了140项临床式任务（替代率/完成率区间90.7–97.9%）；对需派生属性的问题检索到99.3%的相关记录，阳性预测值89.8%，派生值F1为77.7%，优于四种对比方法（64.8–73.4%）；用299条模拟专家更正后，对1201条未见问题的F1从77.9%提升到84.9%。

Conclusion: FD-SCoPE 展示了将大语言模型与可执行查询、已验证程序和专家反馈相结合，以便为临床试验证据表提供可审计、可纠正的问答接口的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Systematic reviews condense clinical trials into evidence tables, yet clinicians can interrogate these tables only through database queries, and many questions concern attributes that the table does not record, such as a drug's target class or a harmonised endpoint. Here we introduce FD-SCoPE, a language-model framework that answers both kinds of question, exposes the query, the selected trials and the derivation rule behind every answer, and learns from expert corrections. On an oncology evidence table of 159 immune checkpoint inhibitor trial records, FD-SCoPE completed all 140 clinician-style tasks (alternatives, 90.7-97.9%). For questions needing derived attributes it retrieved 99.3% of relevant trial records at a positive predictive value of 89.8% and outperformed four alternative approaches (derived-value F1 77.7% versus 64.8-73.4%). Corrections on 299 questions, simulated from reference answers, raised F1 on 1,201 unseen questions from 77.9% to 84.9%. Language models coupled with executable queries, verified programs and expert feedback can give clinicians auditable access to trial evidence.

</details>


### [28] [Labels Override Definitions in Jev-Style Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02586)
*Seyedarmin Azizi,Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Typed-decision models often ignore definitions and rely on labels (option-label bias). The bias is caused by prompt rendering (how options are concatenated) rather than the decision head. Simple formatting fixes and a two-call test can detect and mitigate the issue.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To determine whether typed decision models follow developer-provided definitions or just option labels, and to identify the source of any label bias given increasing use of such models for routing, moderation, and classification.

Method: Empirical analysis across four open-weight models (three laya variants and one von), eleven classification tasks, PolicyBench synthetic suite, and three Qwen2.5 reading methods; interventions include deleting definitions, renaming labels, swapping prompt rendering between codebases, and measuring accuracy impacts.

Result: Answers are mostly driven by labels: deleting definitions leaves accuracy nearly unchanged (laya-td: 0.8559 vs 0.8487), definitions alone support 0.7971, and renaming options to A/B raises accuracy by +0.1511. One model (von) is unaffected; changing option formatting toggles bias. A two-call diagnostic and mitigation evaluations are provided.

Conclusion: The paper finds that open-weight typed decision models often follow option labels rather than written definitions, a phenomenon termed option-label bias; the effect is tied to prompt rendering rather than the decision head, and can be changed by altering how options are concatenated. A simple two-call test identifies which behavior a model exhibits, and several mitigations are evaluated.

Abstract: A typed decision model answers a fixed question about an input by returning a probability for each of several caller-defined options. Each option carries a short label and a written definition, which is where a developer states the rule the model should apply. Jev introduced this interface for routing, moderation and triage, open implementations followed, and the same operation occurs whenever a language model is used as a classifier by scoring label strings. We study the open implementations, whose weights we can inspect and patch, and ask whether the probability follows the definitions or the labels. A preference for the label we call option-label bias. Across four open-weight typed decision models, three ways of reading an answer from a Qwen2.5 backbone, eleven classification tasks and PolicyBench, a synthetic routing suite we introduce in which the rule appears only in the definitions, the answer is mostly the labels. Deleting every definition leaves accuracy unchanged (laya-td: 0.8559 against 0.8487), although those definitions support 0.7971 on their own, and renaming the options to A and B raises accuracy by +0.1511 [+0.1377, +0.1646]. One system, von, is unaffected, and the two code bases differ in one expression: laya writes each option as "{label}: {definition}", while von writes only the definition. Changing that expression in both directions, with no weight changed, makes all three laya checkpoints exactly invariant (+0.0000 [+0.0000, +0.0000]) and creates the effect in von, whose accuracy falls from 0.8511 to 0.2281 when a label contradicts its definition. Earlier work attributed this failure to the constrained decision head these models use in place of a text decoder; our results locate it in the prompt rendering. We give a two-call test that tells a practitioner which case applies to their model, and measure what four mitigations are worth.

</details>


### [29] [Open-Endedness Bench: Measuring Epistemic Process from Agent Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02588)
*Chengyang Shi,Xianglin Ji,Jintao Huang,Jicheng Wang,Yifeng He,Jiachen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OEB is a benchmark-agnostic, log-only methodology that constructs a verifiable epistemic event graph to assess whether agents' claims are supported by their executed experiments; it reveals substantial gaps between claimed and supported improvements and attributes agent behavior patterns mainly to model rather than task.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Outcome scores alone do not establish whether an agent's claims follow from executed experiments, especially when no reference answer exists; need to evaluate epistemic process (hypothesis formation, testing, revision) from logs without relying on outcomes.

Method: Parse execution logs into an epistemic event graph linking agent-stated propositions (verbatim excerpts) to executed actions that test them; code-verify excerpts against logs; score along four competence axes (evidence, experiment, revision, no reward hacking) and profile six persona traits; apply the methodology to 119 runs across 12 tasks from three benchmarks.

Result: Applied OEB to 119 runs: only 16–29% of claimed improvements were supported by logged evidence; best runs tended to try more new ideas later; persona trait variance is better explained by model identity (median 43%) than by task (median 7%).

Conclusion: OEB evaluates agents' research processes by verifying that written claims are supported by executed actions in execution logs; it finds many claimed improvements are unsupported and attributes persona variance mainly to models rather than tasks.

Abstract: Agents are increasingly given open-ended research tasks: discovering an empirical law from self-designed experiments, improving a heuristic whose optimum nobody knows, or beating a standing record. Their execution logs record every step of this research, yet the runs are still judged by their outcome score. That score alone does not establish whether an agent's claims follow from executed experiments, and a reference answer may be unavailable. We evaluate the agent's epistemic process: how it forms hypotheses, tests them, and revises them in response to evidence. We introduce OEB (Open-Endedness Bench), a benchmark-agnostic methodology that reads only the agent's execution record and never a reference answer or an outcome score. OEB compiles the record into a unified epistemic event graph whose edges connect the propositions the agent states to the executed actions that test them; each node carries an exact excerpt that code verifies against the record. One principle governs scoring: prose can state a proposition, but only evidence returned by an executed action can support or refute it, so OEB checks what the agent writes against what it actually ran. From the graph, OEB scores four competence axes (evidence, experiment, revision, and no reward hacking), mostly as the share of opportunities for sound research that the agent took, and profiles six subjective persona traits that describe the agent's research habits. We score 119 existing runs over 12 tasks from three benchmarks: LLM post-training, chip design, and a training-speed record. Against logged results, only 16-29% of the improvements agents claim are real. On 9 of 10 tasks, the best run tries more new ideas in its second half than the worst run. The persona readings follow the model: for every trait, the model that ran explains more of its variance across runs than the task (a median of 43% against 7%).

</details>


### [30] [TasteBench: Multimodal Benchmark for Sensory Prediction, from Molecules to Sustainable Foods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02599)
*Anna T. Thomas,Sohum Patnaik,Caroline Cotto,Benjamin Sanchez-Lengeling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TasteBench 提供了一个覆盖食品和分子两层面的多模态基准，量化了感官数据的不确定性并给出可与人类面板比较的机器学习基线，表明在嘈杂的感官标签下模型已能接近个体人类评估水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续蛋白质发现需要快速的计算代理来代替昂贵、耗时的人类感官面板，以加速设计—构建—测试循环；目前缺乏类似分子对接或DFT那样的高吞吐率预测工具。

Method: 收集并公开两类数据集：1) 食品层面排序任务：来自215种植物基食品、24个品类的21K+人类评估，构造935个类内排序对；2) 分子层面分类任务：15K种风味分子的味觉标签。对标注质量进行统计学表征（Krippendorff's α、split‑half reliability），并在四种输入模态上训练与评估基线模型，报告成对准确率等指标。竞赛采用隐私保护机制（未在摘要详述）。

Result: 发现面板员间一致性低（Krippendorff's α=0.077），但面板聚合排序的split‑half可靠性为0.825，定义了可评估的性能上限。最佳模型在面板员评过的同一对样本上达到0.661的成对准确率（接近中位面板员0.650），在所有类内对上为0.683。提供了评测基础设施与基线结果以推动该领域的计算筛选研究。

Conclusion: TasteBench 构建了一个用于感官预测的多模态基准与隐私保护竞赛，确立了评价食品风味相似性的基准与性能上限，并展示了机器学习模型在噪声大的人类感官数据上可达到接近中等个体面板员水准的结果。

Abstract: Sustainable protein discovery lacks the fast computational proxies, analogous to molecular docking or density functional theory, that accelerate drug and materials discovery. Evaluating whether a novel food tastes like its animal-based target requires expensive human sensory panels, bottlenecking the design-build-test loop. We introduce TasteBench, a multimodal benchmark and privacy-preserving competition for sensory prediction, spanning two tasks: a food-level ranking task built on 21K+ human evaluations across 215 plant-based foods in 24 product categories, yielding 935 within-category ranking pairs, and a supporting molecular-level taste classification task over 15K flavor molecules. To enable rigorous interpretation of model performance, we characterize the ground truth: inter-rater agreement among panelists is low (Krippendorff's $α= .077$), and the split-half reliability ceiling of panel-aggregated rankings is .825, establishing the range within which ML systems on this benchmark should be assessed. We evaluate baselines across four input modalities; on the same pairs panelists rated, the best model achieves .661 pairwise accuracy, competitive with the median individual panelist (.650), and .683 across all within-category pairs. TasteBench provides the evaluation infrastructure and baselines for measuring progress on computational screening for sustainable protein discovery.

</details>


### [31] [Time Series Forecasting Benchmarks Need Scenario-Grounded Stress Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02608)
*Yuyang Zhao,Lian Xu,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 倡导以语义情景和明确失败算子构建的压力测试，弥补传统时间序列预测评估在语义与系统级失效上的盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注持出集误差，且鲁棒性研究常仅使用高斯噪声、随机掩码或有界对抗扰动，这无法覆盖部署中常见的语义、因果和系统级失效，如动力学改变、变量依赖断裂、状态切换与故障传播。基础模型的预训练语料不可审计，进一步加剧了评估差距。

Method: 提出每个测试实例包含（1）历史输入与未来目标；（2）语义情景描述；（3）明确的失败算子（failure operator）；（4）可量化的难度等级，使得测试既可复现又可归因。

Result: 情景化压力测试使评估结果更可解释、可归因且与部署相关，社区能够不仅比较准确性，也能分析模型在何种条件下失败及其原因。

Conclusion: 作者主张采用情景驱动的压力测试来评估时间序列预测模型，以替代仅依赖于低 held-out 误差或简单 i.i.d. 干扰的现有评估方法。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) increasingly drives decisions in transportation, energy, finance, healthcare, and infrastructure, yet current evaluation remains overly narrow: standard benchmarks reward low held-out error, while robustness studies typically reduce failure to Gaussian noise, random masking, or bounded adversarial perturbations.
