This obscures the real failure modes of deployed forecasting systems. Input-side anomalies are not merely noisier inputs: they often reflect structured events that alter temporal dynamics, break cross-variable dependencies, induce regime shifts, or propagate from faulty sensors to downstream decisions. These semantic, causal, and system-level failures cannot be faithfully captured by i.i.d. perturbations alone. The rise of TSF foundation models makes this evaluation gap more urgent, as unauditable pretraining corpora make held-out generalization increasingly unreliable. We therefore advocate scenario-grounded stress testing. Each test instance should include historical inputs and future targets, together with a semantic scenario, an explicit failure operator, and a measurable difficulty level. This shift makes evaluation interpretable, attributable, and deployment-relevant and friendly, enabling the community to ask not only which model is accurate, but under what conditions it fails and why.

</details>


### [32] [VERSE: Verified Self-Evolving Optimizer for Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02616)
*Zekai Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Zekun Wang,Hai Wang,Yuhui Xu,Joshua Frandsen,Shancong Fu,Ashia C. Wilson,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERSE: a verified, self‑evolving optimizer for LLM agent harnesses that executes and verifies edits, builds diagnostic tools, and yields measurable gains on multilingual SWE benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Optimizers typically remain fixed while harnesses evolve; the paper asks whether an optimizer can more effectively improve an agent if it also improves its own diagnostics, edit-generation, and verification procedures—especially through execution-based checks.

Method: Design and implement VERSE, an optimizer that (1) tests draft edits by executing them, (2) replays failures and perturbs suspected steps, (3) tracks fixes and regressions across rounds, and (4) revises both executor harnesses and its own prompts, tools, hooks and notes while keeping model weights fixed. Evaluate across five executors and multiple languages under a shared protocol with disjoint train/val/test splits.

Result: In controlled studies, self-evolution without execution verification fails, but with verification achieves the best results. VERSE improves four evaluated harness optimizers on held-out SWE-rebench tasks and newer OOD tasks in five languages; best validation-selected harness reaches 42.3%/37.7% vs 39.2%/29.3% for strongest baselines. Code is released.

Conclusion: VERSE demonstrates that enabling an optimizer to verify edits via execution and to self-evolve its failure-diagnosis, testing, and tooling leads to consistent improvements in optimizing LLM agent harnesses. With execution-based verification, self-evolving optimizers outperform baselines on held-out SWE-rebench and out-of-distribution multilingual tasks.

Abstract: Harness evolution improves an LLM agent's prompts, tools, and workflow, while the optimizer's own tools and procedures often remain fixed. We study whether an optimizer can improve another agent more effectively by also improving how it diagnoses failures, develops edits, and tests their effects. Two observations guide our design. In a controlled study, optimizer self-evolution fails to improve performance without execution-based verification, but achieves the best result of that study when verification is available. Across five executors, self-evolving optimizers build their own tools for failure analysis, verification, training audits, and workflow control. Motivated by these findings, we introduce VERSE, a Verified Self-Evolving optimizer for agent harnesses. VERSE lets the optimizer test draft edits, replay failures, and perturb suspected steps before submission, while tracking fixes and regressions across rounds. Using this feedback, the optimizer revises both the executor harness and its own prompts, skills, tools, hooks, and notes, while the weights of the optimizer and executor models stay fixed. Under a shared protocol with disjoint training, validation, and test tasks, VERSE improves all four evaluated harness optimizers on held-out SWE-rebench tasks and newer out-of-distribution tasks in five languages. Its best validation-selected harness reaches 42.3% and 37.7% accuracy, respectively, against 39.2% and 29.3% for the strongest baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/wzekai/VERSE.

</details>


### [33] [CuBEs: Culturally-Situated Behavioral Evaluations and the Limitations of Culture-Blind LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02622)
*Hoda Ayad,Tanu Mitra,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce CuBEs: inject cultural context into LLM behavioral tests, build a 12-culture annotated dataset, and show that cultural situatedness significantly alters observed behaviors across 13 models—highlighting limits of culturally-agnostic testing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing situated behavioral evaluations ignore cultural context, reducing generalizability across diverse global users; need to measure how cultural background changes the manifestation and triggers of problematic LLM behaviors.

Method: Extend an automated behavioral-testing pipeline to inject cultural context into scenarios; construct a human-labeled dataset across 12 cultures capturing nuanced behavior judgments; evaluate 13 open- and closed-source LLMs on culturally-situated vs baseline scenarios.

Result: The human-labeled dataset shows significant cross-cultural differences; culturally-situated evaluations change the observed presence and axis of behaviors (e.g., political bias manifests along different cultural axes); culturally-agnostic baselines miss these shifts, demonstrating the need for culturally-aware evaluations.

Conclusion: CuBEs demonstrates that culturally-situated behavioral evaluations are necessary because culturally-agnostic tests miss important variations in LLM behaviors; one-size-fits-all evaluations are insufficient for global deployment.

Abstract: Evaluating the occurrence and triggers of large language model (LLM) behaviors - such as sycophancy, self-preference, or over-confidence - is critical for predicting real-world model deployment risks. However, existing situated behavioral evaluations typically ignore cultural context, limiting their generalizability across an increasingly global user base. To address this gap, we propose CuBEs - Culturally-situated Behavior Evaluations that probe for response patterns across diverse user cultures. We first extend an automated testing pipeline to inject cultural context into behavioral test scenarios and subsequent evaluation. We assess the cultural adaptability of this pipeline by building a human-labeled dataset that captures nuanced dimensions of behavior understanding across 12 distinct cultures. Our dataset reveals significant cross-cultural variations that one-size-fits all judgments fail to capture. Through evaluating 13 open- and closed-source LLMs, we find that introducing cultural situatedness in the evaluation scenario creates significant variation in the presence of a behavior. For example, while our baseline experiments testing for political bias capture localized Western political dimensions like the American conservative-progressive divide, non-Western culturally situated evaluations surface entirely different axes of bias such as religious and colonial political issues. Our findings demonstrate that standard, culturally-agnostic evaluations fail to capture these shifts, highlighting the necessity of culturally situated behavioral testing for global deployments.

</details>


### [34] [Lost in the Request: How Communication Variation Disrupts Retrieval and Action in Email Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02627)
*Feng Chen,Ritam Dutt,Atnaz Taheri,Alex Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者设计了多样化的沟通风格与方言变体，对RAG和两个工具型代理进行评估，发现助理对请求表达方式敏感——间接、正式和方言变体都会降低性能，原因包括检索困难与动作遗漏。作者建议基准应包含表达多样性并单独评估任务完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是强调当前基准通常只用单一规范请求来测试任务，从而忽视了助理在面对相同任务但不同表达方式（沟通风格或方言）时的健壮性。作者希望评估并揭示此类表达变化对电子邮件助理完成请求工作的影响。

Method: 作者沿五个沟通风格轴和四个基于规则的方言条件，构造了经过验证的请求变体；在三个基准上评估这些变体：一个检索增强生成（RAG）管线和两个使用工具的代理。通过对比不同变体在检索与代理执行两个阶段的表现，分析了性能下降的具体成因，并量化了各类变体对系统的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：间接请求会降低所有三种基准的性能；正式请求会降低两个代理基准的性能；冗长请求主要损害词汇检索器的检索能力；间接与方言变体在检索成功时仍会导致系统未能完成应有动作；在代理场景中，间接与正式请求更可能导致遗漏必要动作，而非执行更多不支持的动作。

Conclusion: 文章结论是：当请求的措辞或英语变体变化时，电子邮件助手的可靠性显著下降——间接请求、正式措辞和方言变体都会降低系统在不同基准上的性能；不同失败类型有不同根本原因，例如冗长请求影响检索阶段，而间接/方言变体在检索成功后仍导致模型未能完成所需动作。作者建议评估应加入表达方式的多样性，并独立衡量助手是否完成了请求工作。

Abstract: An email assistant should not complete less work simply because a user phrases the same request differently. Yet most benchmarks test each task with only one canonical request, leaving this form of robustness largely unmeasured. We test whether email assistants remain reliable when the requested information, available evidence, and expected outcome stay fixed, but the communication style or English variety changes. We construct validated variants along five communication-style axes and four rule-based dialect conditions, and evaluate them on three benchmarks: a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline and two tool-using agents. Indirect requests reduce performance on all three benchmarks, while formal requests reduce performance on both agentic benchmarks. Examining the systems more closely shows that these failures have different causes. Verbose requests mainly hurt a lexical retriever by making the relevant email harder to find. By contrast, indirect and dialect variants remain harmful even when the relevant email is retrieved. In the agentic setting, indirect and formal requests mainly cause the agents to omit required actions, not to take more unsupported actions. These results show that a successful response is not enough to establish robustness: evaluations should vary how requests are expressed and separately measure whether agents complete the requested work.

</details>


### [35] [Designing the Future of User Feedback for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02631)
*Alisa Frik,Julia Bernd,Amitis Karami,Mohammad Tahaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文证明了面向部署后用户反馈的重要性：现有方案常有可用性和信号质量问题，作者基于多阶段研究给出最佳实践并验证了一个原型工具，提升了用户参与度与产品可用性，能为产品团队提供更有价值的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regulatory and industry guidance requires post-deployment user engagement, but there is little actionable guidance on how to design feedback mechanisms that are usable for consumers and actionable for product teams; aim to create scalable feedback channels to improve trust and model performance.

Method: Multi-phase study in collaboration with eBay including a benchmark evaluation of existing industry approaches, identification of common issues through qualitative and comparative analysis, deriving best-practice recommendations, designing a prototype feedback-collection tool, and user testing of the prototype.

Result: Benchmarking revealed issues such as poor discoverability, unclear terminology, and low perceived user value. The authors produced best-practice recommendations and a prototype that seeks to offer an efficient, flexible, and positive feedback experience for users while delivering rich, usable data to product teams.

Conclusion: Post-deployment user feedback is a scalable, cost-effective way to monitor and improve generative AI; current industry feedback mechanisms have usability and signal-quality issues; carefully designed feedback tools (based on best practices) can improve user engagement and provide actionable data to product teams.

Abstract: Post-deployment feedback from users can be a cost-effective, scalable, and representative means to monitor and improve generative AI systems and features. When implemented effectively, giving such feedback can increase users' engagement with and trust in GenAI systems. Government regulations and industry guidelines call for post-deployment user engagement, but there is little guidance on designing mechanisms that are usable for consumers and provide actionable input for product teams. We conducted a multi-phase study as a collaboration between academic researchers and eBay. Our benchmark evaluation of current industry approaches identified common issues including lack of discoverability, unclear terminology, and inattention to user value. Based on these findings, we developed best-practice recommendations and designed and tested a prototype feedback-collection tool. The tool aimed to provide users with an efficient, flexible, and positive feedback-giving experience, and provide product teams with rich data on performance and potential problems in a usable format.

</details>


### [36] [Batched Speech Decisions Without Decoding: Single-Token Supervision Lets a Frozen LLM Hear Beyond the Transcript](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02638)
*Jie Jin,Ziyin Ma,Min Yin,Jinyu Chen,Haigang Song,Zhikun Pang,Xiaowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuplexJev 通过把 ASR 隐状态直接注入冻结 LLM 并以单 token 读出封闭选项，实现了对大量小决策的亚 0.1s 批量响应，兼顾速度与语音属性感知，并开源了模型与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音代理在运行时要做大量小型、封闭的决策，传统用自回归解码响应导致延迟高且成本大；同时希望系统不仅能“读”转录文本，还能“听到”说话人的性别/情绪等语音信息，以改善决策。

Method: 用一个“连接器”（两种变体：最后一层线性/投影连接器与跨注意力连接器）把 ASR-encoder 的隐藏态注入到冻结 LLM；对于每个问题，LLM 的输出映射到一个候选选项集合上的单一输出 token，直接用交叉熵训练该读出 token（决策）；对内容理解仍保留转录蒸馏；评估在 8-GPU 节点上做批量推理并发布权重与流水线。

Result: 在 8-GPU 节点上，系统对 8 个语句的约 80 个决策耗时约 0.1 s。使用最后一层连接器时，口语问答准确率接近读取转录本（90% vs. 91%）；使用跨注意力连接器能让系统利用语音属性，性别和情绪识别准确率均提升到约 90%（原为 55% 和 28%），同时 spoken QA 仅小幅下降（约从 83% 到 82%）。作者并公布了模型权重、训练配方、批量推理流水线和双语口语 QA 数据集。

Conclusion: DuplexJev 提出了一种低延迟的全双工语音决策范式：把 ASR 编码器的隐状态通过小型连接器送入冻结的大型语言模型（LLM），将每个封闭选择题直接作为单一 token 的概率分布读取，完全避免自回归解码，从而在保持接近阅读转录本精度的同时显著加速决策。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents make many small, closed decisions, which current systems answer by slow autoregressive decoding. We propose DuplexJev, which feeds ASR-encoder hidden states through a small connector into a frozen LLM and reads each question as a single-token distribution over its options. Nothing is decoded, and an 8-GPU node answers 80 decisions about eight utterances in about 0.1 s. With a last-layer connector, spoken QA stays close to reading the transcript (90% vs. 91%). DuplexJev also hears the speaker: gender and emotion accuracy both reach 90% (from 55% and 28%) with a cross-attention connector, whose spoken QA drops by only 1 point (83% to 82%). We train decisions with cross-entropy on the read-out answer token, instead of the usual transcript distillation, whose teacher never hears the voice, and keep distillation for content. Encoders and LLMs are interchangeable; we release weights, training recipe, a batched-inference pipeline for full-duplex serving and a bilingual spoken-QA set.

</details>


### [37] [Coherence-Driven Belief Formation and Population Dynamics of Contagion in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02654)
*Tathagata Banerjee,Nima Moghaddas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在LLM代理中，信念采纳呈复杂传播特征：S形采纳核和阈值依赖于声明、来源与代理特性；簇状网络促成扩散，系统显示级联窗口与滞后性，使共识难以消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社会传播模型往往假设个体的采纳规则并从中推导群体行为。本研究的动机是直接测量基于语言模型代理的采纳行为，量化采纳核并检验复杂传播特征及其在群体层面的后果，从而为理论建模与AI多主体系统安全提供实证依据。

Method: 通过模拟多个基于大语言模型的代理在不同网络拓扑下的交互，实证测量个体在不同程度同行支持下采纳某条声明的概率（构建采纳核）；系统性操纵声明的可置信度、来源可靠性与代理倾向，并比较簇状与随机网络的集体扩散动力学，分析级联与滞后性。

Result: 发现采纳核为S形（复杂传播标志）；阈值受声明可置信度、来源可信度和代理倾向三维影响，这三维高度相关，可归结为“与先验的相干性”这一单一有效维度。集体层面上，簇状网络更易发生传播，系统表现出级联窗口和自持滞后性——建立共识相对容易，而要撤销共识则困难得多。

Conclusion: LLM代理的信念采纳遵循典型复杂传播的S形采纳核，存在阈值且对声明可置信度、信息源可信度与代理倾向敏感；这三者可被近似为单一有效维度（可解释为新信念与代理先验的相干性）。在多代理网络动力学中，簇状网络比随机网络更利于扩散，系统呈现级联窗口与滞后性（hysteresis），使既成共识比消除共识更困难。

Abstract: Models of social contagion usually assume how individuals adopt beliefs and derive population behavior from it. We instead empirically measure belief adoption in language model agents, quantifying the probability an agent adopts a claim given how many peers endorse it. We find this adoption kernel to be sigmoid, a characteristic of complex contagion, with a threshold that is sensitive to three sources: the claim's plausibility, the source's reliability, and the agent's disposition. These three dimensions are well approximated by a single effective dimension which we propose can be understood as the coherence of the incoming belief with the LLM agent's prior beliefs. Further, we observe a characteristic of complex contagion in the collective dynamics of belief adoption in a system of AI agents: further spread on clustered than random networks. These systems also exhibit a bifurcating cascade window, and self-sustaining hysteretic consensus which lead to consensus being far harder to remove than to establish.

</details>


### [38] [A GHOST in Long-Horizon Agents: Governance Hazard from Overlooked Safety Constraints across Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02664)
*XinPeng Shen,Lan Zhang,Yixiao Huang,Haoran Cheng,Jiewei Lai,Leilei Chen,Haoxiang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文识别并理论化了长期交互代理会在多轮后忽略早期安全约束导致的GHOST风险，证明在弱条件下该风险几乎必然出现；提出两层防御STAR-Guard（历史约束恢复 + 确定性审计），在GPT-5.5实验中成功消除GHOST事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源自长期交互智能体在多轮上下文中容易忽略或遗忘先前明确规定的安全约束，从而在“良性”交互场景下仍导致严重执行安全失败，亟需形式化该风险并提出可操作的防御机制。

Method: 方法包括三部分：1) 设计GHOST的定义与测试套件并在GPT-5.5上做大规模经验评估以量化发生率；2) 提出并证明一个概率性理论命题（依赖不可求和的违例风险下界）说明长期交互下几乎必然触发风险；3) 设计并实现STAR-Guard（语义约束恢复器与确定性执行审计器），并通过消融/对比实验验证其对GHOST的抑制效果。

Result: 主要结果为：1）在GPT-5.5上实证GHOST发生率为11.5%；2）理论结果表明在特定概率条件（残余违例风险下界不可求和）下几乎必然进入危险区域；3）STAR-Guard在实验中有效阻断GHOST，未观测到实例发生。

Conclusion: 论文提出并命名了GHOST（Governance Hazard from Overlooked Safety Constraints across Turns）这一长期交互代理的执行安全缺陷，实验证明在良性交互条件下也可能因忘却早先给定的安全约束导致不可逆损害；在GPT-5.5上该缺陷发生率为11.5%。论文在理论上证明：如果每一安全前缀处的残余条件违例风险被一个不可求和的下界序列约束，则系统几乎必然进入危险区域。基于此提出STAR-Guard，两层防御（历史语义安全约束恢复 + 执行前确定性审计），实验中在GPT-5.5设置下未观察到GHOST事件。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents are now playing an increasingly significant role in assisting humans with complex problem-solving. However, it is exactly their extended interaction history that introduces an underexplored execution-safety concern. Under benign interaction conditions, an agent may execute an action that violates a safety constraint specified many turns earlier. We term this failure mode Governance Hazard from Overlooked Safety Constraints across Turns (GHOST), which may cause irreversible damage. Our experiments reveal that GHOST events are not isolated cases: this failure mode, occurring precisely under benign interaction conditions, yields an occurrence rate of 11.5% on GPT-5.5. Furthermore, we theoretically show that if the residual conditional violation hazard along each safe prefix is bounded below by a non-summable sequence, the execution enters the hazard region almost surely. Leveraging this theoretical insight, we further propose STAR-Guard, a two-layer defense coupling historical semantic safety constraint restoration with pre-execution audit. STAR-Guard restores applicable safety constraints to reduce unsafe proposals, while its deterministic audit layer prevents residual violations from reaching the environment. Consistent with this two-layer design, we observe no GHOST events in our experiments under the GPT-5.5 setup.

</details>


### [39] [Spend Teacher Tokens Where They Matter: Success-Referenced On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02678)
*Xiang Chen,Futao Su,Kong Wang,Jiayi Chen,TanLin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用学生自身的成功回滚作为参考并基于隐藏状态轨迹选择关键失败回滚，SR-OPD 在极低教师输入预算下实现了接近 Vanilla OPD 的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 on-policy distillation 要对学生生成的每条回滚都用教师做密集的逐标记监督，教师计算代价高昂。目标是用尽量少的教师输入代价维持蒸馏效果。

Method: 先筛选出学生对同一提示同时产生成功与失败回滚的提示；将成功回滚作为参考，计算失败回滚与参考在隐藏状态轨迹上的偏差并评估偏差的持续性，同时将预估的教师输入成本纳入优先级评分；优先为偏差显著且成本可接受的失败回滚请求教师的逐标记监督；在一轮（one-pass）和受限预算的对照实验中验证方法效果。

Result: 在三个教师-学生对与六个数学推理基准上，SR-OPD 在一轮设置下只用了 Vanilla OPD 教师输入代币的 3.46–5.02% 即保持了可比的推理性能；在与 5% 教师输入预算相匹配的受控测试中，实验证实了两个关键设计：只关注同时出现成功与失败的提示，以及用成功回滚指导失败回滚选择。

Conclusion: SR-OPD 有效在保持推理性能的前提下大幅减少教师输入成本：通过选择性地只对部分提示与回滚进行教师监督（优先监督那些与学生成功回滚在隐藏状态轨迹上持续偏离的失败回滚），在一轮设置下仅使用 Vanilla OPD 所需教师输入代币的约 3.46–5.02% 即可达到可比性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher, but providing such supervision for every rollout requires substantial teacher computation. We introduce Success-Referenced On-Policy Distillation (SR-OPD), which reduces this cost by selecting which prompts and rollouts receive teacher supervision. When the student produces both successful and failed rollouts for the same prompt, a successful rollout can serve as a natural reference for selecting failed rollouts. SR-OPD therefore focuses on such prompts and prioritizes failed rollouts whose hidden-state trajectories show sustained divergence from a successful reference, while accounting for estimated teacher-input cost. Across three teacher-student pairs and six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SR-OPD uses only 3.46-5.02% of the teacher-input tokens required by Vanilla OPD in the one-pass setting while maintaining comparable reasoning performance. Under a controlled setting matched to 5% of Vanilla OPD's teacher-input budget, further experiments support both key design choices: focusing supervision on prompts with both successful and failed rollouts, and using successful rollouts to guide failure selection. These results indicate that a student's own successful behavior can serve as a useful reference for allocating teacher supervision under a fixed teacher-input budget.

</details>


### [40] [DataWeave: Deploying Human-LLM Analytics for Exploratory Structured Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02679)
*Raquib Bin Yousuf,Harith Laxman,Vitaliy Shkremetko,Eunice Son,Shambhavi Verma,Brian O'Leary,Venketesh Subramony,Sylvain Nazef,Jacquelyn Elias,Ron Coddington,Chris Contakes,Michael Riley,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataWeave通过把LLM作为可监督的交互伙伴并引入模式绑定与分析规划，改善了记者对复杂更新频繁的结构化数据库（如IPEDS）的探索性分析体验，并提炼了可推广的设计与部署教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻数据调查需要在大量异构、经常变动的结构化数据上发现趋势与问责线索；传统做法慢且脆弱，纯粹将LLM当作自动答题机会导致模式不匹配、语义误读和隐式假设等常见失误。

Method: 设计并实现一个以对话为中心的系统，包含schema grounding（模式绑定）、分析规划器、人机迭代纠错环节以及SQL等可执行查询生成和执行模块；通过与专业记者的案例研究（分析IPEDS）验证设计，并基于部署反馈迭代系统架构与工作流。

Result: 与专业记者的案例研究表明，DataWeave在实际新闻工作流中能减少因schema漂移与语义误解引发的错误，支持可检查与可导正的交互式探索；研究还总结了若干设计原则与部署经验，用于构建可信的人—大模型协作系统。

Conclusion: DataWeave将大模型定位为可检查、可纠正的交互式分析伙伴，通过对模式绑定、分析规划与可执行查询生成的结合，提升了记者在大型结构化数据集上开展探索性分析的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Data journalism, the practice of using data analysis to surface newsworthy stories, depends increasingly on the ability of reporters and investigative journalists to uncover trends, disparities, and accountability narratives. In practice, exploring large structured datasets remains slow and brittle: journalists must navigate hundreds of variables across many datasets over years, understand data coding conventions, and write non-trivial analysis code while hypotheses evolve. Although LLMs are often touted as "ask in English, get SQL/answers," real newsroom workflows expose recurring failures, e.g., schema mismatches and drift, misread domain semantics and units, and silent assumptions. We present DataWeave, a system that addresses these needs by combining conversational interaction, schema grounding, analytical planning, and executable query generation to support exploratory analysis over structured data. Rather than treating LLMs as autonomous answer engines, DataWeave frames them as interactive partners whose outputs can be inspected, corrected, and steered as hypotheses shift. We present a case study with professional journalists using our system to analyze the U.S. Department of Education's Integrated Postsecondary Education Data System (IPEDS), a high-stakes public dataset with substantial domain semantics and frequent schema updates. We also report how deployment experience and iterative refinement shaped the current DataWeave architecture and its analytical workflow. Our findings distill design principles and deployment lessons for trustworthy human-LLM collaboration in structured data analysis.

</details>


### [41] [Large language models exhibit unreliable updating of clinical judgment as patient evidence evolves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02684)
*Min Zeng,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for clinical reasoning, but whether they appropriately revise judgments as patient evidence evolves remains unclear. We evaluated longitudinal belief updating using matched intensive-care trajectories from electronic health records. Across diverse LLMs, conditioning on a preceding judgment more often increased than reduced prediction error when estimates changed, replicated for a second endpoint. Controlled interventions revealed two failure modes. First, with preceding assessment fixed, models responded more strongly to worsening than matched improving respiratory evidence; this asymmetry persisted after headroom normalization at moderate and strong evidence levels. Second, with current evidence fixed, increasing prior risk from 10% to 90% shifted estimates by 26.2 percentage points, demonstrating causal influence of prior model beliefs. Prompting did not restore reliable updating. Evidence-Validated Longitudinal Update (EVLU) identified fewer, more reliable revisions, revealing a reliability-coverage trade-off. These findings establish longitudinal belief updating as a distinct dimension of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [42] [Decoupling Memory from Context: Structured Memory for Token-Efficient Test-Time Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02687)
*Yehya Farhat,Michael Desmond,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper reframes memory updates as context-optimization, proposes GraphMemory (graph-structured reusable strategies + bounded retrieval), and empirically shows large token savings with similar performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs in practical domains need continual adaptation and domain knowledge without expensive weight updates; naive appending of experience into context incurs token costs, context-window limits and performance degradation; a principled memory approach that enables efficient, persistent experience is needed.

Method: Introduce a unified formulation that views memory update as an optimization update over the model's context; design GraphMemory, a lightweight graph representation that accumulates, refines and connects reusable strategies; on each query retrieve only a relevant subgraph so retrieved context size is bounded regardless of total memory growth.

Result: Empirical results show GraphMemory achieves comparable downstream task performance while using approximately 81–85% fewer memory-construction tokens compared to baseline memory approaches, demonstrating storage and inference efficiency under bounded retrieval.

Conclusion: GraphMemory provides an efficient, graph-based agent memory that interprets memory updates as context-optimization steps and supports bounded retrieval of relevant subgraphs, yielding competitive downstream performance with drastically fewer memory-construction tokens than baselines.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise, scientific, and medical applications, where agents must incorporate domain-specific knowledge and adapt from experience. Context engineering offers a practical alternative to weight updates by improving model behavior through instructions, strategies, and evidence supplied at inference time. However, adapting context online typically requires a costly trial-and-error process, while queries are often processed independently, preventing useful experience from carrying forward. Memory systems address this limitation by retaining information across interactions, but approaches that continually append information to a shared context face increasing token costs, context-window limits, and performance degradation as the context expands. We introduce a unified formulation of context optimization and show that an agent memory system update can be interpreted as an optimization update procedure over the model's context. This perspective attempts to provide a principled framework for studying memory design and its efficiency. We then propose GraphMemory, a lightweight graph-based memory that accumulates, refines, organizes, and connects reusable strategies. For each query, GraphMemory retrieves only the relevant subgraph, enabling online context adaptation without exposing the model to the entire memory. Under bounded retrieval, the amount of retrieved memory remains constant as the number of processed examples grows. Experiments show that GraphMemory achieves competitive downstream performance while using approximately 81-85% fewer memory-construction tokens than our baselines.

</details>


### [43] [Learning to Revise Reasoning with Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02703)
*Yuxiang Zhang,Ding Cao,Shuting Cui,Lei Wang,Weijieying Ren,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Seg-OPD：基于不确定性选段并用教师重写作为段级监督，结合逐词OPD训练学生学会修订中间推理步骤，从而提升推理质量，实验证明在多个任务上优于基线（约5.22%相对提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐词的在策略蒸馏无法显式教会模型如何修订中间推理步骤，且在学生生成退化前缀时教师的逐词监督可能强化错误模式，需引入段级的、明确的修订示例以改善后续推理。

Method: 作者提出通过不确定性度量选取学生推理片段，使用教师生成对应的重写（redraft），在训练中同时保留密集的逐词OPD监督并加入使学生偏好教师重写的段级偏好监督，从而学会修订错误或欠佳的中间推理片段。

Result: 在数学推理和竞赛编程任务上，Seg-OPD 在推理修订成功率和最终推理准确率上均优于基线方法，跨模型与任务平均相对提升约5.22%，并实现比现有最先进方法更好的表现。

Conclusion: Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation (Seg-OPD) 可以通过将学生的某些片段替换为教师的重写并将其作为明确的修订监督来提升模型的中间推理步骤，从而提高下游推理准确率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves large language model reasoning by training students on their own rollouts with dense token-wise supervision from the teacher. However, token-wise OPD does not explicitly provide a coherent alternative reasoning step showing how the student's step could be revised to improve subsequent reasoning. Furthermore, this paradigm can become less effective when the student produces a degenerate reasoning prefix, as subsequent teacher supervision remains conditioned on that prefix and may reinforce poor reasoning patterns. In this work, we focus on learning reasoning revision with segment-wise OPD to rework intermediate reasoning steps and better support subsequent reasoning. Through controlled reasoning interventions, we find that replacing student segments with teacher redrafts improves subsequent reasoning accuracy. Therefore, we address the problem of turning teacher redrafts into explicit supervision for learning to revise reasoning. We propose Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation (Seg-OPD), which selects student segments based on an uncertainty metric and obtains corresponding teacher redrafts. Seg-OPD trains the student to prefer teacher redrafts over their paired student segments while retaining dense token-wise OPD supervision. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and competitive programming tasks show that Seg-OPD-trained students achieve higher revision success rates than baselines. Seg-OPD consistently outperforms the compared state-of-the-art baselines in reasoning accuracy with an average relative improvement of 5.22% across diverse models and tasks. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Seg-OPD.

</details>


### [44] [Label-Efficient Time Series Classification at Scale: A Dual-Stream OSSE-LSTM with Counterfactual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02704)
*Nguyen Ho,Bach Tung Tran,Trung Ky Nguyen,Zhenchang Xia,Bolong Zheng,Long Van Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在小样本单变量时间序列分类问题中，OSSE-LSTM结合多尺度CNN和BiLSTM并配以反事实归因，实现了稳健、高精度且具可解释性的原型式度量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际生产环境中标签稀缺且标注成本高，核心问题是限定每类K个标注时分类器的可用性；同时需保证小样本决策的可解释性。

Method: 构建一个双流的情景式度量学习框架：一条流为Omni-Scale CNN（结合Squeeze-and-Excitation）用于多尺度motif提取，另一条为双向LSTM捕获全局时间语境；两流独立归一化后融合为原型导向嵌入；引入Counterfactual Integrated Gradients（C-IG）对目标类与对立类间的原型边际进行归因，并将归因图作为软掩码用于不更新编码器的测试时原型精化。

Result: 在19个单变量UCR基准上，OSSE-LSTM在所有支持样例数K下均取得最高平均准确率和最多单数据集胜出次数；准确率在不同K间仅在0.36个百分点内波动（96.36–96.72%），最弱配置仍优于任何基线在任一K下的最好结果（93.99%）。

Conclusion: 提出的Dual-Stream OSSE-LSTM在小样本条件下能稳定、显著地提升单变量时间序列分类性能，并通过原型导向嵌入和对比式归因增强可解释性和测试时精化能力。

Abstract: Time series are produced continuously at enormous scale by industrial equipment, wearables, power grids, and clinical monitors, yet annotation remains manual, expensive, and expert-dependent. The binding constraint in large-scale time series analytics is therefore not data volume but label volume, and the question facing a practitioner is concrete: how many examples per class must be labeled before a classifier becomes usable? We study this question directly, in a regime where the label space is fixed and known in advance and the decision rule must be constructed from only K labeled examples per class. We propose Dual-Stream OSSE-LSTM, an episodic metric-learning framework that pairs an Omni-Scale CNN with Squeeze-and-Excitation recalibration, for multi-scale motif extraction without per-dataset kernel tuning, with a Bidirectional LSTM for global temporal context. The two streams are independently normalized and fused into a prototype-oriented embedding. Because decisions taken from a few labels must also be explainable, we introduce Counterfactual Integrated Gradients (C-IG), which attributes the prototype margin between target and opposing classes rather than an isolated classifier logit, and reuses the resulting maps as soft masks for test-time prototype refinement without updating the encoder. On 19 univariate UCR datasets, OSSE-LSTM attains the highest average accuracy and per-dataset win count at every support size, and its accuracy remains within a 0.36-point band (96.36-96.72%) across that range. Its weakest configuration still exceeding the best result any compared baseline achieves at any K (93.99%).

</details>


### [45] [Ego2World: Compiling Egocentric Cooking Videos into Executable Worlds for Belief-State Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02715)
*Qinchuan Cheng,Zhantao Gong,Pengzhan Sun,Angela Yao,Shijie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ego2World compiles annotated egocentric activities into executable, partially-observable planning environments with separable world/belief, exposing gaps between planned actions and task attainment and showing that persistent belief reduces observation needs but not completion in a tested LLM agent.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current egocentric-video benchmarks do not let researchers execute and verify arbitrary agent actions under partial observability; need a reusable testbed to study how planning and memory choices affect execution success, observation demands, and task attainment.

Method: Construct a compiler that maps video-annotated source steps and objects into symbolic action schemas, persistent world-state representation, explicit task pre/post-conditions, and agent belief tracking; provide execution tracing and condition checking; evaluate six planners on 105 tasks and run a paired study with Qwen-Plus comparing persistent belief vs. no persistence.

Result: On 105 tasks, planners often accepted operations that did not achieve goals; execution traces classify interrupted, partially attained, and executed-but-unfinished cases. In a paired Qwen-Plus experiment, persistent belief increased action validity by 4.15 percentage points and cut visual-query attempts by 90.27% while increasing token use and not improving completion rate.

Conclusion: Ego2World enables execution-level evaluation of agents by compiling annotated egocentric cooking videos into symbolic, partially-observable planning environments with persistent world state and separable agent belief, revealing that many accepted actions still fail to achieve goals and that memory persistence reduces perception queries but not task completion in a tested LLM agent.

Abstract: Egocentric videos capture how people carry out everyday activities, yet testing an agent requires evaluating the consequences of actions it chooses itself. We introduce Ego2World, a benchmark that turns annotated cooking activities into executable planning environments under partial observation. Its compiler links source steps and objects to symbolic action rules, persistent world states, and explicit task conditions, so researchers can execute an agent's proposed actions and check their outcomes. World state and agent belief are maintained separately, enabling controlled studies of planning and information reuse across continuing tasks. Evaluating six planners on 105 tasks shows that accepted operations often leave task goals unmet. Execution traces and condition checks distinguish interrupted runs, partial attainment, and completed execution without goal attainment. In a separate paired Qwen-Plus study, persistent belief improves action validity by 4.15 percentage points and reduces visual-query attempts by 90.27%, with higher token use and no detected completion gain. Ego2World provides a reusable testbed for tracing how planning and memory choices affect execution, observation demand, and task attainment, connecting recorded human activity to the development and evaluation of interactive agents.

</details>


### [46] [On the Chain-of-Thought Monitorability of Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02741)
*Han Wang,Ishwar B Balappanawar,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loop depth can hurt CoT monitorability in some stress-test cases, but looped architectures are not inherently less monitorable than comparable non-looped models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand how looped (recurrently applied shared-layer) transformer architectures affect the ability to monitor internal chain-of-thought reasoning, given LoopLMs’ increasing effective depth without increasing model size and the importance of CoT monitoring for detecting undesirable behavior.

Method: Systematic evaluation across two settings: (1) varying loop depth within a LoopLM family to isolate recurrent computation effects; (2) comparing LoopLMs with non-looped LMs matched by parameter size, transformer-layer count, or effective depth. Evaluations run across eight MonitorBench tasks under standard and stress-test conditions, measuring CoT monitorability (likely via verification pass rates and cue-answer alignment) and analyzing generated tokens qualitatively.

Result: Task-dependent reductions in CoT monitorability are observed under stress tests on specific Logic/Science/Engineering Cue Answer tasks; other tasks show weaker or different trends. Declines are not fully explained by task difficulty, verification pass rate, or output length; qualitative analysis suggests changes in cue usage/attribution by deeper-loop models. No systematic monitorability disadvantage for LoopLMs compared to matched non-looped models.

Conclusion: Deeper loop depth can reduce chain-of-thought (CoT) monitorability on some tasks under stress tests, but looped transformer architecture by itself does not necessarily make models less monitorable compared to non-looped models matched by size, layer count, or effective depth.

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring provides a promising approach for detecting undesirable model behavior. Looped language models (LoopLMs) repeatedly apply shared transformer layers, increasing effective computational depth and enabling additional latent computation without increasing model size. However, the effect of looped architectures on CoT monitorability remains largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic evaluation of CoT monitorability in LoopLMs. We study two complementary settings: (1) varying the loop depth within the same LoopLM family to isolate the effect of additional recurrent computation, and (2) comparing LoopLMs with non-looped language models matched by parameter size, transformer-layer count, or effective depth to study whether LoopLMs are less monitorable. Across eight tasks from MonitorBench and both standard and stress-test settings, we observe task-dependent reductions in CoT monitorability under stress tests on specific Logic/Science/Engineering \texttt{Cue Answer} tasks, while other tasks exhibit weaker or qualitatively different trends. Our diagnosis suggests that these declines are not fully explained by task difficulty, verification pass rate, or generated token length; qualitative examples further suggest changes in how deeper-loop models explicitly use or attribute provided cues. Our cross-model comparison finds no evidence that LoopLMs are systematically less monitorable than non-looped language models matched on size or depth. Overall, our results suggest that deeper loop depth can reduce CoT monitorability in some tasks under stress tests, but looped transformer architecture alone does not necessarily imply lower monitorability.

</details>


### [47] [Dynamic LLM Routers are Often Misguided](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02762)
*Sam Wang,Julia White,Sahibzada Allahyar,Dhruv Atreja,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 商业动态路由器未达预期：常被简单的两模型随机路由超越，问题源于评估目标与现实模型特性。替代评估与简化路由可缓解部分问题，但潜在收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过为每个查询选择最便宜且能正确回答的模型来显著降低推理成本。

Method: 对六个商业路由器在14种设置与覆盖八类任务的多样化基准上进行实证评估，分析路由决策与成本-准确率目标之间的关系，归纳出四种普遍模式，并提出替代评估方法与一个两模型路由器的原型实现。

Result: 发现标准目标会激励导致性能下降的行为（难度盲点、长度反转、语义匹配、名册次优）；现实中两项支持大规模名册的假设——模型粒度与模型专门化——并不成立。提出的两模型路由器可避免四种问题，但相对于随机路由增益有限，因为精心挑选的名册本身就几乎没有可路由的空间。

Conclusion: 动态LLM路由器普遍未能超越在相同成本下随机在两款精心挑选模型之间切换的基线；部分路由器性能落后超过10个百分点。

Abstract: Dynamic LLM routers promise to cut inference costs by sending each query to the cheapest model that can answer it correctly. We analyze six commercial routers across 14 settings on a diverse benchmark spanning eight task categories, finding that none of them outperforms a router that randomly selects between two well-chosen models at matched cost. Some underperform by more than 10 percentage points. We trace this gap to four patterns prevalent across routers: difficulty blindness, length reversal, semantic matching, and roster suboptimality. We show that the first three are what the standard objective rewards: cost-accuracy Pareto efficiency on realized costs favors escalating moderately hard queries over the hardest ones, shorter queries over longer ones, and routing by a query's source over its difficulty. We also argue that the two assumptions that would justify large rosters, model granularity and model specialization, do not hold empirically. We propose an alternative evaluation methodology that does not reward these patterns, and as a proof of concept, we design a simple two-model router that avoids all four. Nevertheless, its gain over random routing is limited, because a well-chosen roster leaves little to route.

</details>


### [48] [Law And Order: Tax Law Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02792)
*Sophia Simeng Han,Yoshiki Takashima,Anjiang Wei,Zhaoyu Li,Michael Genesereth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将LLM生成与符号验证和局部修复结合，可实现对税法表单的高精度自动形式化，显著优于纯LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本（尤其是纳税表格与填写说明）包含复杂、可计算的结构（算术、分支、递归、表格推理），需要可扩展、可验证的方法将其自动翻译为精确的符号表示以便执行与审计。

Method: 提出Law&Order神经-符号框架：用LLM生成表格的符号程序草案；建立结构对应（将法律组件映射到符号组件）和指称对应（符号组件实现法律指定的计算）；通过基于单元的符号验证发现错误，并利用人工编写的OpenTaxSolver返回进行迭代局部修复。

Result: 在未见过的TaxCalcBench基准上评估：Law&Order在51份持出返回上实现单元级与表单级100%准确率；最先进LLM单独运行时的准确率为66%，表明符号验证+修复显著提升可靠性。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将大语言模型生成与基于符号的单元级验证和局部错误修复结合，可以可靠地将税法表格与说明自动形式化为可执行程序；在51份未见过的测试表单上实现了单元与表单100%准确率，而仅依赖最先进LLM的准确率为66%。

Abstract: Legal systems are increasingly implemented through software, yet scalable methods for translating legal texts into accurate symbolic representations remain underdeveloped. We study this problem through tax law, where forms and filing instructions define large computational structures involving arithmetic, branching, recursion, and tabular reasoning. We propose Law&Order, a neuro-symbolic framework for automatically formalizing tax forms and instructions into executable symbolic programs. Our approach establishes two forms of correspondence between law and logic: structural correspondence, which aligns legal and symbolic components such as cells and schedules, and denotational correspondence, which requires symbolic components to implement the computations specified by their legal counterparts. We combine large language model synthesis with cell-level verification and iterative localized error repair using human-written OpenTaxSolver tax returns. We then evaluate the resulting formalizations on independently authored, held-out TaxCalcBench returns, that are never exposed during generation or repair. Although the most advanced LLM achieves only 66% accuracy, Law&Order achieves 100% cell-level and form-level accuracy on 51 held-out returns, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining LLM-based synthesis with symbolic verification for scalable and verifiable large-scale legal autoformalization compared with using an LLM alone.

</details>


### [49] [PAPER2LLM++: Continual Self-Evolution of LLMs from Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02793)
*Hongji Pu,Yilun Zhao,Wenpeng Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种让LLM从学术论文中持续自我改进的框架：抽取发现、验证限制、生成学习信号并通过验证后提交更新，从而把研究发现闭环到模型演化中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于LLM局限性的研究成果与模型演化脱节，模型不能自动从关于其自身失败的新研究中学习；目标是闭合人类发现与模型演化之间的循环，使模型能持续吸收研究带来的改进。

Method: 对每篇新论文：抽取基于证据的发现、检测当前模型是否仍存在该限制、将必要发现转化为候选学习信号；通过try-evaluate-commit流程，仅在改进目标行为且不显著遗忘先前改进或降低通用能力时才集成更新。

Result: 在按时间顺序的研究发现流上，PAPER2LLM++能使模型逐步吸收新发现，同时保留早期收益，表明该框架在避免遗忘并提升目标行为方面是可行的。

Conclusion: 论文提出PAPER2LLM++框架，能把研究论文作为连续的证据和监督源，使LLM从关于自身缺陷的研究中持续自我进化，在实验证明可在保持先前改进的同时，逐步并入新的研究发现。

Abstract: Research on LLMs continually uncovers model limitations, their causes, and potential solutions. Yet these human discoveries remain largely disconnected from model evolution: an LLM does not automatically learn from new research about its own failures. We introduce PAPER2LLM++, a framework for continual self-evolution of LLMs from research papers. Rather than treating papers merely as knowledge to retrieve, PAPER2LLM++ uses the growing literature as a stream of evidence and supervision for model improvement. For each incoming paper, it extracts evidence-grounded findings, tests whether the reported limitation persists in the current model, and, when needed, converts the findings into candidate learning signals. A try-evaluate-commit procedure integrates an update only when it improves the targeted behavior without substantially forgetting prior improvements or degrading general capabilities. Across a sequential stream of research-discovered LLM failures, we show that models can progressively incorporate new findings while retaining earlier gains. PAPER2LLM++ thus takes a step toward closing the loop between human discovery and model evolution, enabling models to continually learn from research about their own limitations and improvements.

</details>


### [50] [Modeling Shared and Individual Structure for Cross-Subject Continuous Affect Regression from EEG-fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02796)
*Xuan Wang,Bing Wang,Shuai Chang,Hao Yuan,Xinbo Qi,Xinyue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在相同视频刺激下，把情感轨迹拆成“共享刺激响应＋个体无标签尺度校准（由α带同步性给出）”可以实现有效的零样本跨被试逐秒价-唤醒估计，误差远优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中常无法获取每个新被试的标注，因而需要无需标签即可对新被试进行连续情感估计。利用相同刺激下存在的共享情感响应，同时用可从无标签EEG提取的个体标记进行校准，以实现零样本跨被试泛化。

Method: 使用同步的EEG–fNIRS数据集（相同视频刺激下不同被试），将真实情感轨迹分解为共享的刺激响应轨迹和被试特异的尺度调整。为每个测试被试从无标签EEG中计算α带跨通道同步性作为标度标记，并以此对共享轨迹在量表中点周围进行重标定（缩放）。验证方法包括：留一被试相关性检验、与非线性替代函数的比较、重复留四被试的消融实验、和上界（ceiling）分析。与EEGNet及ASAC-Net的同一独立被试划分下基线对比。

Result: 对未见被试的总体MAE在两个评估批次分别为25.96和22.80；分维度：valence 21.94 / 19.6，arousal 29.98 / 26.0。相较于同一被试独立划分下报告的基线（原始量表分数为60.6 与55.0），性能明显更优。系统性负结果搜索未发现能胜过α-同步性标记的信号。

Conclusion: 本文证明在“零样本跨被试”情形下，通过将情感轨迹分解为“刺激驱动的共享结构”与“每被试的标度校准”两部分，并用无标签的EEG标记（α带跨通道同步性）估计每个被试的尺度因子，可以在未见标签的被试上实现连续、逐秒的价-唤醒预测，且误差显著低于已有主流基线。

Abstract: Continuous, second-by-second valence-arousal estimation from physiological signals is typically studied in a subject-dependent setting, where the model sees labeled data from the same person it is later evaluated on. We study the harder zero-shot cross-subject variant on a synchronized EEG-fNIRS dataset: predict raw-scale ([1, 255]) valence and arousal trajectories for subjects whose labels the model never observes, given only their unlabeled EEG/fNIRS recordings while watching the same video stimuli as a disjoint set of training subjects. We decompose the affect trajectory into a structure shared across subjects who watch the same stimuli and an individual structure estimated for each test subject from a label-free EEG marker (alpha-band cross-channel synchrony), which rescales the shared trajectory around the scale midpoint. We validate the per-subject calibration mechanism on four independent axes: leave-one-subject-out correlation between the marker and each subject's true optimal gain, a functional-form comparison against non-linear alternatives, a repeated leave-4-out component ablation isolating each part of the pipeline's contribution, and a ceiling analysis bounding the remaining headroom for per-subject scaling. On held-out subjects, the model reaches an overall MAE of 25.96 / 22.80 across two evaluation batches (valence 21.94 / 19.6, arousal 29.98 / 26.0), well below EEGNet and ASAC-Net baselines reported for the same subject-independent split (raw scale score 60.6 and 55.0 respectively). We further report a systematic negative-result search across model architectures, feature representations, and prediction targets that found no signal able to improve on the single alpha-synchrony marker.

</details>


### [51] [BitNest: Bit-Nested Speculative Decoding for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02800)
*Chence Yang,Ningxi Cheng,Arash Akbari,Qitao Tan,Qingchan Zhu,Ci Zhang,Changdi Yang,Yanzhi Wang,Wei Niu,Jinhui Wang,Jin Lu,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BitNest通过在同一权重中先用低精度草稿再做残差精化的‘位嵌套’策略，显著减少额外存储、保持生成质量并在边缘7B–8B模型上实现约1.5×速率提升，推测接受率约95%.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码需要额外草稿模型或权重表示，在资源受限设备上引入额外内存开销；自我推测虽减少了额外存储，但在草稿质量、目标质量与存储效率之间存在不可避免的折中，因而需要一种能在单一权重表示下兼顾速度、质量和内存的方案。

Method: 提出了“位嵌套”（bit-nested）推测解码框架：先构建一个强鲁棒的低精度基础表示（draft），再通过残差精化（residual refinement）恢复高精度目标，从而使草稿和目标共享同一权重；同时将逐级精度设计扩展到KV cache，用于长上下文推理。

Result: 在多个7B–8B边缘友好LLM与多样化任务上，BitNest平均推测接受率95.2%，在保持高精度模型质量的同时，比FP16自回归解码实现1.48–1.61×的端到端加速；在所有代表性自我推测基线支持的LLaMA模型上，BitNest也保持或优于基线的解码加速。

Conclusion: BitNest 通过将低精度草稿嵌入到高精度目标表示内，实现了在单一物理权重表示下高效的自我推测解码，从而在边缘设备上显著降低内存开销并保持高质量的生成。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a lightweight draft to propose multiple tokens for parallel verification. However, existing methods often require an additional draft model or weight representation, introducing non-negligible memory overhead on resource-constrained devices. Self-speculative approaches reduce this overhead, yet still face trade-offs between draft quality, target quality, and storage efficiency. We propose BitNest, a bit-nested speculative decoding framework that embeds a low-precision draft directly into the higher-precision target representation. Instead of deriving a draft from a predefined target, BitNest first constructs a strong low-precision base and then recovers the higher-precision target through residual refinement, enabling both models to share a single physical weight representation. BitNest further extends this progressive-precision design to the KV cache for long-context inference. Across multiple 7B--8B edge-friendly LLMs and diverse workloads, BitNest achieves an average speculative acceptance rate of 95.2% while closely preserving higher-precision model quality, and delivers 1.48--1.61x end-to-end speedup over FP16 autoregressive decoding. On the LLaMA models supported by all representative self-speculative baselines, BitNest also achieves consistently competitive or higher decoding speedup.

</details>


### [52] [VIGOR: Zero-Shot Visual Generalization via Latent-Space Consistency in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02801)
*Mingyu Park,Samyeul Noh,Hyun Myung,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A method (VIGOR) that enforces latent-space consistency across augmentations to make MBRL robust to unseen visual distractions, yielding better zero-shot generalization while keeping sample efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBRL is sample-efficient but sensitive to unseen visual distractions because encoder perturbations produce out-of-distribution latents whose errors compound through recursive latent rollouts during planning. The work aims to close this gap by making latent dynamics robust to augmentations.

Method: VIGOR combines three coupled components: (i) asymmetric weak-to-strong augmentation that creates paired latent views, (ii) dynamics-level consistency that enforces invariant transition predictions via direct regression in latent space, and (iii) encoder-level stabilization to prevent encoder drift under cross-augmentation supervision; integrates with learned latent dynamics and planning in MBRL.

Result: On DeepMind Control Suite and Robosuite, VIGOR outperforms state-of-the-art model-free and model-based baselines, improving over the second-best baseline by 3.4% on DMC and 43.6% on Robosuite. Ablations indicate robustness is augmentation-agnostic, implying latent-space consistency—not specific augmentations—drives generalization.

Conclusion: VIGOR (visual generalization via latent-space consistency) enables zero-shot generalization of model-based reinforcement learning policies to unseen visual distractions by enforcing consistency in latent dynamics and stabilizing the encoder, preserving sample efficiency.

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) achieves strong sample efficiency by planning within learned latent dynamics, yet its performance degrades substantially under unseen visual distractions such as background variations, lighting changes, or camera shifts. Unlike model-free RL, where encoder perturbations affect only single-step predictions, MBRL suffers from a two-level vulnerability: visual distractions first push encoder outputs out of distribution, and these errors then compound through recursive latent rollouts over the planning horizon. We propose visual generalization via latent-space consistency in model-based RL (VIGOR), a framework that enables zero-shot generalization to unseen visual distractions while retaining the sample efficiency of its MBRL backbone. VIGOR integrates three interdependent components: (i) asymmetric weak-to-strong augmentation, which pairs weak-only and weak-to-strong latent views within a single batch; (ii) dynamics-level consistency, which enforces augmentation-invariant transition predictions through direct latent regression; and (iii) encoder-level stabilization, which prevents encoder drift under the cross-augmentation supervision imposed by dynamics-level consistency. Evaluations on the DeepMind Control Suite (DMC) and Robosuite show that VIGOR outperforms state-of-the-art model-free and model-based baselines, surpassing the second-best baseline by 3.4% on DMC and 43.6% on Robosuite. Ablations further show that VIGOR's robustness is augmentation-agnostic: replacing the default augmentation with alternatives from distinct perturbation families preserves strong generalization, confirming that latent-space consistency, not the augmentation choice, drives robustness.

</details>


### [53] [ROUTEAUDIT: Interaction-Aware Identification for Budgeted Multi-Verifier Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02808)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ROUTEAUDIT：一个对 verifier 路由进行合同式鉴定并返回可证书化归因的框架，能在策略相关的环境变化下给出稳健比较，且在实证测试中表现出高精度的归因恢复与显著的策略改进检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多验证器（multi-verifier）系统中，端点的质量-成本差距常用于比较，但当验证器目录、可用性、记账、过滤或评分随策略而变时，这一比较会混淆归因。需要一个合同化、可证书化且对策略独立的鉴定框架来正确归因与评估策略改进。

Method: 将 verifier 路由视为带有六要素（请求支持、验证器目录、可用性、资源记账、在线过滤、后处理评分）的合同问题；引入 contract lattice 平均坐标增量并报告路径敏感性；引入 policy-independent response tape 以配对序列对比；对不完全匹配使用基于观测结果的请求级界；通过协议提前提交已付观察与账本事件并在 oracle join 之前返回比较的归因证书。实验包括静态匹配、学习与 RL 策略比较，以及 factorial/bridge-order/stochastic-provider 稳健性测试。

Result: 在两个 held-out 原始尾部缓存中，匹配静态 SF+SA 等于 cascade，将表观增益归于 verifier 集合边；在 1,319 个 held-out 请求上，learned 和 RLVR 分别达到质量 0.9522 与 0.9553，而匹配静态为 0.9484；RLVR 与静态的配对差异 +0.0068，请求配对区间 [0.0015,0.0122]，训练种子×请求的分层区间 [0.0006,0.0131]；受控归因恢复的路线平均绝对误差 0.0011，端点重构误差 0.0004。

Conclusion: ROUTEAUDIT 提出一个合同（contract）条件下的鉴定框架，将 verifier 路由问题形式化为 contract-conditioned identification，并通过三种可测对象（contract lattice、policy-independent response tape、request-level bounds）实现可证书化的归因与鲁棒比较。实证在多项 held-out 集合上显示该方法能精确恢复归因并对学习型与 RL 策略进行可靠比较。

Abstract: Adaptive multi-verifier systems are commonly compared through endpoint quality-cost gaps, even when the verifier catalog, availability, accounting, information filtration, or scorer changes with the policy. We formulate verifier routing as a contract-conditioned identification problem. The contract records request support, verifier catalog, realized availability, resource accounting, online filtration, and post-trace scoring; a matched route contrast changes only the policy coordinate. ROUTEAUDIT adds three measurable objects to this contract. A contract lattice averages coordinate increments over every admissible bridge order and reports the resulting attribution together with its path sensitivity. A policy-independent response tape identifies paired sequential contrasts when adaptive policies reveal different observations. For incomplete matching, request-level bounds use whichever potential outcome remains observed and give a sharp finite-population interval. The protocol commits paid observations and ledger events before the oracle join and returns an attribution certificate for each comparison. On two held-out raw-tail caches, matched static SF+SA equals the cascade, assigning the apparent gains of 0.1797 and 0.1250 over full static to the verifier-set edge. On 1,319 held-out task requests, the learned and RLVR studies report quality 0.9522 and 0.9553 versus 0.9484 for matched static; the RLVR-static paired difference is +0.0068 with a request-paired interval $[0.0015,0.0122]$ and a training-seed-by-request hierarchical interval $[0.0006,0.0131]$. Controlled attribution recovery yields route mean absolute error 0.0011 and endpoint reconstruction error 0.0004. Factorial, bridge-order, and stochastic-provider studies evaluate the certificate interface; RLVR supplies a learned-policy stress test under the same identification contract.

</details>


### [54] [iS-KV: Online Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Block-Incremental SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02815)
*Yiren Zhao,Guanghui Song,Tianrui Qin,Kejiang Ye,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iS-KV 通过‘近端精确 + 远端增量低秩折叠并同步坐标’的策略，实现在线 KV-cache 低秩压缩，在高压缩比下保持接近原性能并优于令牌驱逐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归推理中 KV cache 随生成长度线性增长；现有通过删除令牌来控制增长的方法会不可逆地丢失可能需重访的历史状态；SVD 低秩压缩可保留所有位置但更紧凑，但将其从固定 prompt 扩展到在线解码面临基底演化与坐标漂移问题。

Method: 提出增量低秩压缩框架：保持最近窗口的 KV 精确；将更早状态折叠（fold）到有界秩的低秩基底中；当基底随新令牌更新时，增量地同步并变换历史坐标以保持表示一致性，避免因基底变更产生的漂移（类似增量 SVD/子空间对齐）。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 上，iS-KV 在 4.06× 持久 KV 压缩下取得 82.6% 的准确率（原模型 83.6%）；在 Qwen3-8B 上，在 5.64× 压缩下取得 89.2% 的准确率；在相同内存预算下优于基于令牌驱逐的基线。

Conclusion: iS-KV 能在在线自回归解码中以有界秩表示压缩历史 KV-cache，同时通过保持近期窗口精确与同步历史坐标，避免基底更新导致的表示漂移，从而在显著压缩下仅有极小的性能损失。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning substantially increases KV-cache memory during autoregressive decoding, as every generated token introduces new key and value states and causes the cache to grow linearly with decoding length. Existing KV-cache compression methods typically control this growth through token eviction, but irreversible deletion can remove historical states that later reasoning may need to revisit. SVD-based low-rank compression provides an alternative by retaining all positions with a more compact representation. However, extending it from a fixed prompt cache to online decoding is non-trivial. Through our investigation, we find that if the basis is updated for new tokens while old tokens keep their coordinates in the old basis, the stored history drifts substantially. Based on this observation, we propose iS-KV, an online low-rank KV-cache compression method for long-horizon reasoning. iS-KV keeps a recent window exact while incrementally folding older states into bounded-rank representations. As the low-rank basis evolves, it synchronizes historical coordinates with the updated basis to maintain representation consistency. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, iS-KV achieves 82.6% accuracy at 4.06-fold persistent-KV compression, close to the original model's 83.6%. On Qwen3-8B, it achieves 89.2% accuracy at 5.64-fold compression. Under matched memory budgets, iS-KV consistently outperforms token-eviction baselines.

</details>


### [55] [MetaRubric: Learning to Reward for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02824)
*Yuxuan Fan,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MetaRubric：通过构造反事实并交替进行证据感知的策略优化与响应驱动的量表修订，减少空洞信用并在医学问答基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rubric-based 强化学习通过对多个子要求赋予部分信用来处理开放性任务，但存在‘Vacuous Credit’故障：评审在响应缺失所需信息的情况下仍给出高分，导致错误奖励持续存在并可能逆转 GRPO 的优势。为此需要一种证据感知且能随策略输出动态修订量表的方法。

Method: 构建每个提示的反事实对（修改一个任务相关事实）；在策略优化阶段：仅当响应包含满足某条量表准则所需的充分证据时才分配该准则的分数；在每轮策略优化后：用当前策略生成的响应来引导对原始与反事实准则的修订，同时保持在各自事实下原始量表的语义解释；在阶段边界根据观察到的策略错误调整准则权重。与静态评审（static-judge GRPO）对比，采用多个模型骨干进行评测。

Result: 在多个骨干模型上，MetaRubric 在 PubMedQA 上比静态评审 GRPO 提高了 6.00–20.40 个百分点的准确率，并在 HealthBench-Hard 及两个多模态医疗基准上获得进一步提升，显示出对医学问答场景中空洞信用问题的实用效果。

Conclusion: MetaRubric 能显著减少 Rubric-based RL 中的 Vacuous Credit 问题，通过在证据感知的策略优化与基于响应的量表适配之间交替进行，从而保证只有在响应中包含足够证据时才给予部分分数，保持量表在不同事实设定下的语义一致性，并在阶段边界调整准则权重以修正策略错误。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning extends reward-driven optimization to open-ended tasks by assigning partial credit to individual response requirements. However, rubric judges can assign a high criterion score even when the information or action it requires is absent from the response, a failure mode we term Vacuous Credit. Such awards persist after the required information is removed and can reverse the sign of a response's GRPO advantage. To address this problem, we introduce MetaRubric, which alternates evidence-aware policy optimization with response-guided rubric adaptation. We construct counterfactual counterparts by changing one task-relevant fact in each prompt. During policy optimization, credit is assigned only when the response contains sufficient evidence to satisfy the required rubric criterion. After each policy-optimization stage, current policy responses guide revisions to original and counterfactual criteria while preserving the meaning of the original prompt's initial rubric as interpreted under each prompt's facts. We also adapt criterion weights at stage boundaries to better address observed policy errors. Across multiple backbones, MetaRubric improves PubMedQA accuracy by 6.00--20.40 percentage points over static-judge GRPO, with further gains on HealthBench-Hard and two multimodal medical benchmarks.

</details>


### [56] [Scaling Trajectories for Complex Tasks through Recursive Self-Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02826)
*Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Chengsong Huang,Fuxiao Liu,Haitao Mi,Jordan Boyd-Graber,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSR uses a single model to discover and recursively rewrite harness-assisted solutions into general-harness training trajectories, substantially boosting downstream task performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Specialized test harnesses produce valuable successful trajectories but rely on interventions unavailable at deployment; the goal is to preserve and reuse the learning signal from those harnesses by reconstructing their solutions as training data that a model can follow under a general, deployable harness.

Method: Use a single base model (Qwen-3.8-27B) in three roles—planner (extracts procedures into runbooks), critic (screens for verifier and solution leakage and directs recursive revision), and executor (runs qualified runbooks in fresh sandboxes). Run a recursive loop to discover, filter, and rewrite successful trajectories from multiple specialized harnesses into training trajectories for a general harness.

Result: Across ≈3K terminal tasks, three harnesses jointly solved 759 tasks (34.3% more than the best single harness). RSR expanded 2,001 source trajectories into 11,094 rewritten ones. Fine-tuning on these trajectories improved pass@3 from 57.0%→74.2% on Terminal-Bench 2, 1.5%→9.1% on Terminal-Bench 4, 39.0%→63.0% on Terminal-Bench Hard, 3.0%→6.0% on Software Terminal-Bench, and process reward on Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench from 0.21→0.29.

Conclusion: Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR) can convert harness-assisted successful trajectories into reusable supervised training data under a general harness, yielding significant downstream performance improvements over the base model and direct trajectory SFT.

Abstract: Successful trajectories on difficult tasks provide valuable supervision for model improvement, but specialized harnesses introduce interventions that may be unavailable during deployment. We propose Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR), a framework that uses one base model, Qwen-3.8-27B, to discover successful solutions under diverse harnesses and reconstruct them as training trajectories under a general harness. A planner extracts procedures into runbooks, a critic screens for verifier and solution leakage and guides recursive revision, and an executor follows qualified runbooks in fresh sandboxes. Across approximately 3K self-curated terminal tasks, three harnesses jointly solve 759 tasks, 34.3% more than the strongest individual harness in the recorded pool. RSR expands 2,001 successful source trajectories into 11,094 rewritten trajectories for supervised finetuning. Training on these trajectories outperforms both the base model and direct trajectory SFT. Compared with the base model, pass@3 increases from 57.0% to 74.2% on Terminal-Bench 2, from 1.5% to 9.1% on Terminal-Bench 4, from 39.0% to 63.0% on our self-curated Terminal-Bench Hard, and from 3.0% to 6.0% on our Software Terminal-Bench. Process reward on Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench rises from 0.21 to 0.29. These results show how diverse harness-assisted experiences can be reconstructed into reusable capabilities for a model operating under a general harness.

</details>


### [57] [MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02827)
*Carsten Maple,Cagatay Yucel,Isaac Holeman,Chris Knotz,Peter Mattson,James Goel,Jonathan Petit,Sean McGregor,James Ezick,Abhishek Kumar,Alicia Parrish,Murali Emani,Kashyap Iyer,Faiza Khan Khattak,Washington Mbonu,Daniel Machlab,Eileen Long,Shaona Ghosh,Jibin Varghese,Roman Lutz,Andrew Gruen,Bennett Hillenbrand,Prabal Gupta,Mohammed Serrhini,Dhivya Nagasubramanian,Aakash Gupta,Jun,Lu,Kurt Bollacker,Chang Liu,Jonathan Petit,Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Brent Miller,Apurv Verma,Roman Eng,Armstrong Foundjem,Mohammed Serrhini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并发布了一个可重复的单轮越狱评测基准，发现越狱提示显著提高不安全响应率，量化为平均7.57%的韧性下降，并讨论评估者与测量误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统会拒绝危险请求，越狱提示能绕开这些防护。需要一个统一、可重复的方法来比较系统在面对越狱攻击时的安全性，从而为改进模型防护提供基准与可比度量。

Method: 构建MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0：通过选择代表性系统与攻击、使用264个种子提示覆盖11类危害，分别在基线与对抗条件下对8个开源权重模型进行单轮文本越狱测试；用人类标注与自动化评估器（AILuminate Assessment Standard v1.4）评估响应，并以“韧性差距”（baseline vs adversarial 的安全性能变化）量化鲁棒性，同时评估评估者可靠性与误差来源。

Result: 评估8个开权模型在264个种子提示和多类攻击下的表现：整体不安全响应率从11.08%（基线）上升到18.65%（越狱）；平均韧性差距7.57%；可访问性更强的系统平均韧性差距更大；不同攻击类别和危害之间的攻击效果差异显著；并分析了评估者可靠性与测量误差来源。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一个端到端的基准化方法来衡量大型语言模型对单轮文本“越狱”攻击的鲁棒性，结果显示在对抗条件下不安全响应率普遍上升（基线11.08%→越狱18.65%），平均“韧性差距”为7.57%。该基准框架可重复、可扩展，并包含标注校准与风险披露流程。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are designed to refuse hazardous requests. A jailbreak is a prompt crafted to bypass those safeguards and elicit outputs that the system would normally refuse to provide. The MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 provides an end-to-end methodology for evaluating the robustness of large language models to single-turn, text-based jailbreak attacks. It combines criteria-driven system and attack selection, paired baseline and adversarial evaluation, human annotation, automated evaluator calibration, scoring, grading, and risk-calibrated disclosure within a single benchmarking pipeline. The benchmark evaluates eight open-weight systems using 264 seed prompts spanning eleven hazard categories and representative attacks drawn from the MLCommons Jailbreak Taxonomy. Responses are assessed using the AILuminate Assessment Standard v1.4, and robustness is measured through the Resilience Gap: the change in safety performance between baseline and adversarial conditions. Across all evaluated systems and attacks, the unsafe-response rate increased from 11.08% under baseline conditions to 18.65% under jailbreak conditions, producing an average Resilience Gap of 7.57%. Accessible systems showed a larger mean gap, while attack effectiveness varied substantially across attack categories and hazards. The benchmark also examines evaluator reliability and sources of measurement error. Beyond reporting results, Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 establishes a reproducible methodological foundation for comparative jailbreak evaluation and for future expansion across systems, attacks, hazards, and evaluation methods.

</details>


### [58] [FSPO: Policy-Consistent Risk and Pareto-Feasible Control for Budgeted LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02828)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FSPO is a unified feedback‑state controller for budgeted adaptive LLM RL post‑training that jointly fixes risk‑to‑go misestimation, calibration drift after selective action, and infeasible multi‑resource planning; it yields measurable improvements in accuracy, calibration, and feasibility over strong baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adaptive post‑training for LLMs changes many actuators (temperature, group size, clipping, KL regularization, verifier allocation, update budget) online, creating three coupled problems: future‑risk models trained on logged behavior may not reflect the deployed controller; score calibration degrades after selective action choice; and independent per‑resource minima do not guarantee feasible multi‑resource continuations. A unified solution is needed to ensure safe, calibrated, and feasible adaptive control.

Method: FSPO comprises three core components: (1) a policy‑consistent risk‑to‑go model trained with Bellman targets that follow the frozen controller used at decision time plus a long‑horizon utility model; (2) Decision‑Conditioned Trajectory Calibration (DCTC) that recalibrates risk on cross‑fitted trajectories generated by provisional controllers; (3) Pareto Resource Continuation Certificate (PRCC) that admits actions only if a non‑dominated cumulative reservation over remaining horizon is feasible. These are used within a feedback‑state controller that dynamically adapts multiple training actuators online.

Result: Under a matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO achieves 66.11% held‑out and 59.43% OOD accuracy vs. 64.47% and 57.03% for the best baseline (PB2). Paired seeds show +2.42 and +3.19 percentage point gains vs. contextual bandit. Under high behavior‑deployment mismatch, policy‑consistent risk reduced selected‑decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC reduced it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance. PRCC eliminated false‑feasible admissions on an 18‑action catalog (0.197→0.000). Full ablation reduced trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083.

Conclusion: FSPO jointly addresses three coupled failure modes in adaptive LLM reinforcement‑learning post‑training—risk misestimation under deployment mismatch, selective‑action miscalibration, and infeasible multi‑resource continuations—by combining a policy‑consistent risk‑to‑go model, decision‑conditioned trajectory calibration, and a Pareto resource continuation certificate. Empirically, FSPO outperforms strong baselines on held‑out and OOD accuracy and substantially reduces calibration and feasibility failures.

Abstract: Adaptive LLM reinforcement-learning post-training changes multiple training actuators online, including rollout temperature, group size, clipping, KL regularization, verifier allocation, and update budget. Three coupled issues remain unresolved. A future-risk model trained from behavior trajectories need not estimate the risk induced by the controller that will be deployed; a score calibrated on logged state-action pairs can become miscalibrated after selective action choice; and independent per-resource minimum costs do not in general certify a feasible multi-resource continuation. We introduce FSPO, a feedback-state controller for budgeted LLM RL post-training that addresses these issues jointly. FSPO learns a policy-consistent risk-to-go model whose Bellman target follows the same frozen controller used for future decisions, together with a long-horizon utility model. Decision-conditioned trajectory calibration (DCTC) calibrates risk on cross-fitted trajectories generated by actions selected by provisional controllers. A Pareto resource continuation certificate (PRCC) admits an action only when a non-dominated cumulative reservation remains feasible over the residual horizon. Under a matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO reaches 66.11% held-out and 59.43% OOD accuracy, compared with 64.47% and 57.03% for PB2, the strongest evaluated adaptive baseline. Three paired training seeds give gains of +2.42 and +3.19 percentage points over the contextual bandit on held-out and OOD evaluation. Under high behavior-deployment mismatch, policy-consistent risk lowers selected-decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC lowers it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance; PRCC removes false-feasible admissions on an 18-action catalog ($0.197\rightarrow0.000$); and enabling all three components reduces trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083 in a factorial ablation.

</details>


### [59] [AMBER: Multi-View Adaptive Budget Allocation for Listwise Vision-Language Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02831)
*Wenteng Chen,Jiachen Zhu,Rong Shan,Tianyi Xu,Yuxiang Chen,Congmin Zheng,Teng Wang,Junjie Wu,Weiwen Liu,Changwang Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMBER 是一个基于连续 Elo 更新与信息量最大化的在线预算化多视图重排序框架，通过两级动态分配 VLM 调用，在有限预算下显著提高多模态检索的重排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在多模态检索中作为强大的列表式重排序器，但推理成本高昂，现有多次调用策略多为固定计划，会在信息量低的候选对和简单查询上浪费宝贵的 VLM 调用；需要一个能在线、全局优化有限计算资源的自适应策略。

Method: 提出一种名为 AMBER 的在线预算化多视图重排序框架：将局部的列表式 VLM 输出视为局部锦标赛，使用连续的 Elo 更新维护轻量级全局排序状态；在候选层基于分数不确定性动态构造需要比较的视图；在查询层基于期望信息增益调度查询顺序以最大化总体效用。证明每次 Elo 更新等价于对 Bradley-Terry 对数似然的随机梯度上升，并为查询级分配给出次模信息论解释。

Result: 在 CIRR、CIRCO 和 PhotoBench 等数据集上的实验证明：在可比的 VLM 调用预算下，AMBER 在整体性能上优于比较的多次调用重排序方法；在更低预算下仍保持较好效果；并已公开代码。

Conclusion: AMBER 在给定 VLM 调用预算下，通过在线、两级（候选视图和查询）动态分配计算资源、并用连续 Elo 更新维持全局排序状态，有效提升多次调用下的多模态重排序性能，实验证明其在相同比较预算下优于其他多次调用策略，并在低预算场景下仍保持有效。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful listwise rerankers for multimodal retrieval, but high inference costs restrict them to evaluating small local candidate views. Existing multi-call strategies rely on fixed schedules, wasting expensive VLM calls on uninformative candidate pairs and easy queries. To address this, we propose Adaptive Multi-view Budgeted Elo Reranking (AMBER), an online, budgeted multi-view reranking framework that dynamically optimizes global resource allocation. AMBER treats fragmented listwise VLM outputs as local tournaments, using continuous Elo updates to maintain a lightweight global ranking state. Building on this, it allocates computation at two levels: dynamically constructing candidate views with high score ambiguity, and scheduling queries to maximize expected information gain. We show that each Elo update corresponds to a stochastic gradient ascent step on the Bradley-Terry log-likelihood, and provide a submodular information-theoretic motivation for the query-level allocation strategy. Experiments on CIRR, CIRCO, and PhotoBench demonstrate that AMBER achieves the strongest overall performance among the compared multi-call VLM reranking methods under comparable VLM-call budgets, while remaining effective in lower-budget settings. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wnlfc/AMBER.

</details>


### [60] [DNAlign: Dynamic Null-Space Safe Alignment for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02844)
*Jisheng Dang,Yushuo Zhao,Dewei Liu,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Tiantian Rao,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DNAlign 通过投影到有害相关子空间并用价值函数优化控制信号，实现了轻量、安全且不显著损害模型实用性的 LLM 对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法要么计算开销大（如 RLHF、在线微调），要么破坏模型原有知识导致良性任务表现下降，存在明显的安全-效用权衡。提出 DNAlign 旨在在不大幅修改模型权重或性能的前提下实现安全控制。

Method: 将 LLM 视为动态系统，引入可控干扰（control signals）引导生成向安全方向；用一个投影模块将干扰限制到由中性隐藏状态导出的“有害相关子空间”，以保护通用知识；并用基于人工偏好的价值函数自适应优化控制信号。

Result: 在多种 LLM backbone 上的大规模评估表明，DNAlign 在降低有害输出方面持续有效，同时保持流畅性、连贯性与事实性，生成多样性不受明显削弱，总体优于已有对齐基线。

Conclusion: DNAlign 提供了一种轻量级且可部署的对齐框架，通过控制理论优化与零空间投影相结合，有效降低有害生成同时尽量保持模型的流畅性和事实性，缓解了安全-效用的权衡问题。

Abstract: Ensuring the safe and reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs) remains a fundamental challenge. Existing safety alignment approaches either incur high computational cost or unintentionally disrupt the model's core knowledge, leading to degraded fluency and factual accuracy on benign tasks. This reveals a persistent trade-off between safety and utility. We propose DNAlign, a lightweight alignment framework that integrates control-theoretic optimization with null-space projection. By treating the LLM as a dynamic system, the proposed framework introduces controllable perturbations to steer generation toward safe behavior. A key component is the projection module, which restricts these perturbations to the harmful-related subspace derived from neutral hidden states, thereby preserving general knowledge and response quality. A value function trained on human preference data adaptively optimizes the control signals to align with human safety preferences. Extensive evaluations across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that our framework consistently reduces harmful outputs while maintaining fluency, coherence, and factual utility. It achieves superior overall performance compared to prior alignment baselines without sacrificing generation diversity. These results indicate that the proposed framework provides an effective and practically deployable solution for safe LLM alignment. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DNAlign.

</details>


### [61] [Bounded Reachability & Jailbreak Detection via Contraction-Constrained State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02853)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明SSM安全头能否被IBP证书化取决于||A||_∞<1的收缩性；通过收缩正则获得实证提升与跨基准转移，且线性探针已能很好地分离有害意图，S4的价值在于可证书化而非更优判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前轻量级安全头能在经验上检测有害输入，但其对输入嵌入扰动的形式化鲁棒性未被研究；目标是找到能提供确定性鲁棒性保证的SSM安全头条件并验证其实践可行性。
