Method: 理论分析证明线性时不变分类器在嵌入空间有界扰动下的可证书化充要条件为||A||_∞<1；在训练中加入hinge惩罚以强制收缩性；在毒性评论和jailbreak检测基准上进行实证评估，并与对比模型（均值池化后的Logistic回归）比较。

Result: 当满足收缩条件时，稳态可达输出区间有界，认证可行；训练施加收缩正则后，毒性检测的已认证比例从41%提升到59%，在收缩范数阈值处出现理论预测的相变；S4安全头在JailbreakBench上零样本迁移至AdvBench和HarmBench时DR分别为0.994和0.988；同时发现线性分类器在均值池化嵌入上已能很好分离有害意图，表明S4的主要贡献在于提供形式化证书而非更强判别。

Conclusion: 证书化可行性由状态转移矩阵A的l_∞范数决定：当||A||_∞<1时（收缩条件），基于SSM的安全头可被IBP精确证书化；否则，输出区间随序列长度呈指数增长，使实用证书不可能。

Abstract: Safety heads are lightweight classifiers attached to pretrained language models for flagging harmful inputs before generation. Their empirical detection performance has been studied, but their formal robustness properties remain largely unexplored. We ask when a State Space Model (SSM)-based safety head can be certified to produce the same prediction for all inputs within a bounded embedding-space perturbation. We prove that the answer turns on a single condition: the $l_\infty$ norm of the state transition matrix must satisfy $\norm{A}_\infty<1$ (the \emph{contraction condition}), which enables exact interval bound propagation (IBP) certification for linear time-invariant classifiers. When the contraction condition holds, the reachable output interval has bounded steady-state width and examples can be certified as robustly classified. When it fails, the interval grows exponentially with sequence length and certification is impossible at any practical perturbation radius. We enforce contraction with a hinge penalty and show on toxic comment data that certified fraction improves from 41\% to 59\%, with a sharp empirical phase transition at $\norm{A}_\infty=1$ matching the theory. Applying a contraction-regularized S4 head to jailbreak detection on JailbreakBench, we achieve a zero-shot transfer to AdvBench (DR=0.994) and HarmBench (DR=0.988). A logistic regression on mean-pooled Mamba-130M embeddings matches or exceeds the S4 head on every detection metric, confirming that harmful intent is already linearly separable in the embedding space. The S4 safety head's contribution is not superior discrimination but the formal certification that no probe-based approach provides.

</details>


### [62] [Harness-Aware Distillation for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02858)
*Moonseok Choi,Taehong Moon,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HAD focuses distillation on teacher contributions beyond a fixed harness via contrastive action preferences and a harness-consistent validity filter, yielding better long-horizon agent behavior without rewards or labels.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: When distilling agents, the harness (context, tools, feedback manager) remains unchanged, so the distilled student only needs the teacher-specific abilities not supplied by the harness. Standard imitation treats the harness as input and thus fails to emphasize those teacher-specific additions.

Method: HAD augments on-policy distillation with two components: (1) an action-preference signal that contrasts teacher actions with and without harness information, scored after the student’s own reasoning; (2) a validity check that discards preference pairs whose preferred action contradicts harness records. The method focuses learning on teacher-specific behaviors the harness cannot provide, and requires no task rewards or success labels.

Result: Across multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks and models, HAD outperforms on-policy distillation baselines with the same fixed harness, reduces unproductive loops, improves error recovery, and appears to store learnable feedback in weights while reading state info from the harness.

Conclusion: Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD) improves student agents by distilling only the teacher’s contributions beyond the fixed harness, leading to better long-horizon performance and error recovery than standard on-policy distillation baselines.

Abstract: Language model agents are deployed with a harness, the software around the model that manages its context, tools, and feedback. When such an agent is distilled into a smaller one, the harness stays in place, so the student mainly needs the teacher-specific abilities that the harness cannot provide, such as acting correctly on harness information. Standard distillation, however, imitates the teacher's full outputs and treats the harness as part of the input. We propose Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD), which focuses distillation on what the teacher adds beyond the harness. HAD complements on-policy distillation with two components: an action preference that contrasts the same teacher's actions with and without the harness information, scored after the student's own reasoning, and a validity check that drops preference pairs whose preferred action contradicts the harness records. We show that the contrast gives the student information that imitating the teacher alone cannot provide, and HAD needs no task rewards, success labels, or future information. Across multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks and models, HAD outperforms on-policy distillation baselines with the same fixed harness. Our analysis shows that HAD enters fewer unproductive loops and recovers from errors more often than the baselines, and suggests that it adaptively keeps learnable feedback in its weights while reading state information from the harness.

</details>


### [63] [TACD: Distilling Efficient Text-to-Motion Models via Terminal Amplification Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02867)
*Wei-Jin Huang,Yuan-Ming Li,Kun-Yu Lin,Wang Luo,Yinlin Zhu,Yue Yu,Shenghao Ye,Junbin Yuan,Fa-Ting Hong,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TACD 是一种在不依赖真实动作数据的前提下，通过绑定教师查询与学生步长来约束终点损失、以改善少步文本到动作生成质量并显著提升速度与内存效率的 on-policy 蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是缓解文本到动作模型在部署时的慢推理与高内存占用问题，训练出少步（few-step）且组件紧凑的学生模型，同时避免使用真实动作标注数据。识别到的具体动机包括：教师-学生蒸馏在少步设置下质量下降（主要因终点误差被放大），以及需要一种 on-policy、与学生轨迹一致的监督方式。

Method: 基于分段的 on-policy flow distillation，在学生生成轨迹上监督教师的干净运动预测；识别并修正速度匹配在固定监督网格处对去噪终点误差过度加权的失败模式；通过将教师查询时间点与学生当前步长对应（终点匹配形式对扩散教师），在不改变推理流程的情况下限制有效损失权重。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 和 KIT-ML 上实验：对 HY-Motion 的八步学生相较于不加约束的蒸馏实现 FID 下降 58%；对扩散教师，终点匹配的 TACD 使四步学生在 FID 与文本-动作检索上匹配或优于 50 步教师；在 HY-Motion 与 Kimodo 上，八步学生实现 7.7-11.9x 的端到端加速和 3.8-6.7x 的峰值 GPU 内存降低。

Conclusion: TACD 提出了一种在无真实动作数据下、面向文本到动作生成的 on-policy 蒸馏方法，通过将教师最新查询与学生步长绑定来约束终点处干净运动空间的损失权重，从而显著提升少步生成质量并加速/减小模型。

Abstract: Recent text-to-motion models have improved motion quality and instruction following, yet many-step denoising and large model components make deployment slow and memory-intensive. We present Terminal-Amplification-Controlled Distillation (TACD), an on-policy approach for training efficient motion generators from text prompts and pretrained teachers, without real-motion training data. Building on segmented on-policy flow distillation, we supervise clean-motion predictions along student-generated trajectories. We identify a failure mode in which velocity matching on a fixed supervision grid repeatedly overweights errors near the denoising endpoint, degrading few-step generation. TACD ties the latest teacher query to the student's step size, bounding the effective loss weights in clean-motion space without changing inference. Experiments on HumanML3D and KIT-ML demonstrate improved few-step generation, including a 58% reduction in eight-step HY-Motion student FID relative to distillation without this bound. For diffusion teachers, the endpoint-matching form of TACD yields four-step students with lower FID and matched or improved text-motion retrieval relative to their 50-step teachers on HumanML3D. On HY-Motion and Kimodo, eight-step students with compact components achieve 7.7-11.9x end-to-end speedups and reduce peak GPU memory by 3.8-6.7x relative to their teachers. Project page: https://vkgo.github.io/TACD/

</details>


### [64] [PsyEvo: A Personalized Counseling Agent That Self-Evolves at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02885)
*Yuting Yan,Shihao Xu,Junhao Yu,Mingcong Zuo,Lu Chen,Nan Xiang,Haiyang Geng,Dongjie Tao,Minghao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PsyEvo是一个可在测试时实现个体化与策略在线改进的LLM咨询框架，包含HBSP、LiPO、SOCA三部分，在模拟评估中有小幅但稳健的性能提升，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线训练LLM难以在测试时适配个体客户与从持续治疗交互中学习的问题，提供可扩展的心理对话支持。

Method: 提出分层贝叶斯技能策略(HBSP)维护每位客户的技能后验以个性化干预；跨会话列表式偏好优化(LiPO)利用跨客户偏好证据更新共享应答适配器；状态条件序数归因(SOCA)通过一致性校验比较和序数投影生成候选偏好和轨迹归因，结合共享在线队列适应进行模拟客户评估。

Result: 在模拟客户评估下，PsyEvo在PsychEval任务获得7.684 Overall，完整框架优于去掉任一组件的变体；在共享配置下移除任一组件会使平均Overall下降0.138–0.171。

Conclusion: PsyEvo通过HBSP、LiPO、SOCA在模拟客户共享在线适应场景下实现了对话式心理支持系统的个性化与在线策略改进，实验在PsychEval上取得7.684 Overall，并且每个组件都有正面贡献。

Abstract: Mental health disorders affect a substantial proportion of the global population, yet a persistent shortage of trained practitioners leaves the majority without adequate care. Large language model (LLM)-based counselors present a promising direction for delivering scalable conversational psychological support. Offline model training alone leaves limited room to adapt to individual clients or to learn from ongoing therapeutic interaction at test time. We introduce PsyEvo, an LLM-based counseling framework that enables both client-specific personalization and response-policy improvement at test time through three components: Hierarchical Bayesian Skill Policy (HBSP) personalizes what intervention to apply by maintaining a per-client skill posterior updated from session feedback; Inter-session Listwise Preference Optimization (LiPO) improves how the selected skill is expressed by updating a shared response adapter from cross-client preference evidence; and State-conditioned Ordinal Credit Assignment (SOCA) supplies candidate preferences and trajectory credit to the two components through consistency-checked comparisons and ordinal projection. In simulated-client evaluation with shared online cohort adaptation, PsyEvo obtains 7.684 Overall on PsychEval and exceeds every component variant in each of three matched runs. Removing individual components lowers mean overall score by 0.138--0.171 under the shared configuration, supporting conditional contributions within the complete scaffold. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lingxi-mental-health/PsyEvo

</details>


### [65] [Interpreting at Write Time: A Policy Ablation for Multi-Goal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02897)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为多目标服务时，按目标写摘要最有效；全局折衷策略表现欠佳；在写入时解释目标能提升特定目标的表现，但缺乏通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期助手无法保存全部历史，只能摘要；摘要会永久替代原始流且损失不可逆。面对多重“standing goals”，需要设计如何在写入阶段选择保留何内容以服务未来可能的查询目标。

Method: 将读步骤固定，仅比较三种写策略（中立写、单一面向所有目标写、按目标写），在多种事件流和模型下评估生成摘要的重叠、相关性、完整性与准确性等指标；通过模拟不同目标集合和预算约束来度量策略表现差异。

Result: 不同目标导致写入策略分化：按目标写的摘要彼此重叠较少，但针对各自目标表现最好；单一全局摘要在有限预算下甚至不如中立摘要；将目标在写入阶段纳入解释提高了该目标的性能但牺牲了对其他目标的通用性。

Conclusion: 在为多重长期目标服务的记忆摘要问题上，按目标写摘要（per-goal summaries）在相关性、完整性与准确性上整体优于为所有目标写单一汇总或中立汇总；在写入时考虑目标（interpret at write）对该目标有明显收益，但不利于未考虑的其他目标。

Abstract: A long-running assistant cannot keep everything it has seen, so it summarises. Summarising is not neutral: what is kept is chosen against some notion of what the record is for, and that choice is made once, before anyone knows which of the user's standing goals will ask. Goals rarely disagree about what happened. They disagree about which parts of it were worth the space. Once the history is too long to re-read, the summary replaces the stream, and whatever it left out is gone. We ask what a memory should summarise for when it serves several standing goals at once. Three policies answer differently: summarise with no goal in view, write one summary covering every goal, or write one summary per goal and read them together. We compare them across several models and event streams, holding the read step fixed so that only the write differs. The goals do pull apart: summaries written for different goals overlap each other less than a summary overlaps a rewrite of itself. Per-goal summaries win on relevance, completeness and accuracy, and the all-goal summary loses even to the neutral one written at a fraction of its budget. Interpreting at write pays off, but only for the goal that later asks.

</details>


### [66] [LUMOS: Tracing Parametric Knowledge from Training Data to Behavioral Outputs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02902)
*Seoyeon Ye,Gayoung Kim,Jiyoung Hong,Sookyung Kim,Hyunsoo Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUMOS uses a model with a transparent training set to trace knowledge from exposure to output, showing that many facts are encoded but not reliably retrieved; self-reflection and chain-of-thought fail to recover unseen exposures, highlighting the need to include training-data axis in LLM evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing evaluations of LLM knowledge are output-centric and cannot distinguish genuine generalization from memorization because they lack ground-truth about what the model was exposed to. By using a transparent training corpus, the paper seeks to resolve whether correct answers come from memorized exposure or true generalization.

Method: Introduce LUMOS, a diagnostic framework that leverages a model with a fully transparent training corpus (OLMo 2) to trace causal chain from training-data exposure to internal representations and behavioral outputs. Measure separability of rare facts in internal representations, behavioral expression rates, and self-reflection calibration, comparing trained vs unseen content and scale effects.

Result: Findings: (1) Internal representations encode rare facts with high separability (~84%), but behavioral recall is lower (~54%); (2) Retrieval gap decreases with model scale; (3) Self-reflection reliably detects correctness on trained content (~83%) but performs at chance on unseen content (~49%); (4) Chain-of-thought prompting increases confidence signals but not calibration for unseen facts. Overall, training-data grounding changes speculative claims into verifiable statements.

Conclusion: Incorporating the training-data exposure axis enables verifiable attribution of model knowledge: many correct outputs reflect internalized representations from training but are not reliably retrieved behaviorally; self-reflection and chain-of-thought prompting do not recover unseen training gaps and may inflate confidence without improving calibration.

Abstract: Current analyses of LLMs' parametric knowledge are largely output-centric, drawing conclusions about what a model knows without verifying what it was actually trained on. This leaves fundamental questions, such as whether a correct response reflects genuine generalization or rote memorization, grounded in speculation rather than evidence. To resolve these ambiguities, we introduce LUMOS, a diagnostic framework that traces knowledge along the causal chain from training-data exposure to behavioral output, leveraging OLMo 2 with its fully transparent training corpus. By grounding analysis in verified exposure, we reveal that models internally encode rare facts with high separability (84%) yet fail to express them behaviorally (54%), though this retrieval gap narrows with scale. Furthermore, when models are asked to self-reflect on their own answers, they perform reliably on trained content (83%) but drop to random-baseline levels (49%) on unseen content. This collapse persists even under chain-of-thought prompting, which inflates confidence signals rather than improving calibration. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that incorporating the training-data axis into LLM evaluation transforms speculative diagnoses into verifiable claims, and we advocate that this axis should be a standard component of knowledge assessment in LLMs.

</details>


### [67] [Frequency Is Not Sensitivity Identifying Safety-Sensitive Experts in Sparse MoE LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02910)
*Md Nurul Absar Siddiky,Liuwan Zhu,Yingfei Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在不重训练的限制下，用路由器梯度敏感度来选择要抑制的专家，比基于激活频率的方法更能有效削弱MoE模型的拒绝/safety行为，且在多架构与预算下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不重训练模型的情况下抑制部分被路由到的专家会削弱模型的安全拒绝能力。传统用激活频率衡量“重要专家”，但频率反映使用频率而非对损失/行为的影响。提出用路由器梯度敏感度来衡量某专家被选中对序列损失的影响，以更直接识别安全相关的关键专家。

Method: 对比两种专家重要性信号——激活频率与路由器梯度敏感度——在五种MoE架构上的表现。为每种模型用500条良性和500条恶意提示对专家排序，并在100条保留的恶意提示上测量“拒绝”率，分别在两类抑制预算下运行：按专家数量平均分配和按名义恶意路由流量（1%–5%）分配。此外与十次随机抑制均值比较，并在一个消除层内专家数差异的匹配实验中重复评估；进行探索性跨模型相关分析以把恶意/良性集中度差与梯度峰值效应相关联。

Result: 在两个预算设定下，梯度排序在24/25个条件中比激活率引起更大的拒绝下降；与十次随机平均相比在25/25个条件中均更好。最显著的例子是OLMoE：拒绝从34降到9（相对下降73.53%），且未观察到输出降级。即便在每层专家计数匹配后，梯度方法仍在23/25条件中优于激活率（两次并列）。跨模型探索性分析给出恶意/良性集中度差与梯度峰值效应的正相关（rho=0.90，双侧精确p=0.083，n=5）。总体结论支持在所测预算下采用梯度选择。

Conclusion: 在所测条件下，基于路由器梯度敏感度（router-gradient sensitivity）的专家抑制比基于激活频率的抑制更能削弱稀疏Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型的安全拒绝行为，且在多数架构和预算设定下效果稳定显著。

Abstract: Suppressing a small set of routed experts can weaken the safety behavior of a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model without retraining. Which experts to suppress is therefore a security question, and the usual answer is activation frequency, but frequency measures use, not influence. We test an alternative: router-gradient sensitivity, the sensitivity of the sequence loss to the gate weights that select an expert. Across five MoE architectures, we rank experts by each signal on 500 benign and 500 malicious prompts and measure refusal on 100 held-out malicious prompts under two budgets: equal expert counts and equal nominal malicious routing traffic (1%-5%). Under each of the two budgets, router-gradient selection reduces refusals more than activation in 24 of 25 conditions, and more than a ten-trial random mean in all 25. The largest effect is in OLMoE, where refusals fall from 34 to 9 of 100 prompts (73.53% relative) with no degraded outputs, indicating substantive compliance rather than broken generation. After matching expert counts in every layer, gradient selection still produces greater refusal reduction than activation in 23 of 25 conditions, with two ties. An exploratory cross-model analysis links larger malicious-versus-benign concentration gaps to greater peak gradient effects (rho = 0.90; exact two-sided p = 0.083, n = 5). Together, the results support gradient selection under the tested budgets.

</details>


### [68] [HASTE: Evolving Agent Harnesses Against Emerging Attacks Using Sparse Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02920)
*Xiqiao Xiong,Moxin Li,Zhixin Ma,Ouxiang Li,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE通过安全规范与攻击用例的对抗性演化闭环，从稀疏威胁证据中自动增强代理保护套件，有效降低攻击成功率且保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中威胁信息常稀疏（短描述或少量示例），人工及时更新保护套件跟不上攻击演化，因而需要一种能从稀疏证据自动演化保护机制的方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架：一组智能体负责从短文本或少量示例中生成安全规范（指导保护逻辑），另一组负责根据当前保护逻辑生成攻击用例进行探测；系统将评估结果回馈给两端，迭代更新，推动保护套件覆盖超出初始证据的威胁。

Result: 在多种基础模型、攻击类型和证据形式上，HASTE稳定降低了攻击成功率且对良性任务的性能影响很小；代码已开源。

Conclusion: HASTE通过在安全规范生成与攻击用例生成之间进行对抗性多智能体协同，能够从稀疏威胁证据中自动进化代理保护套件（harness），在减少攻击成功率的同时保持良性任务效用。

Abstract: Agent harnesses play a critical role in defenses by enforcing safety constraints to prevent unsafe actions. However, rapidly emerging attacks outpace manual harness adaptation, motivating automated harness evolution. Yet the signals available for harness evolution are often sparse, such as brief descriptions or a few attack examples in threat reports and preprints. To address this limitation, we introduce HASTE, a multi-agent framework that evolves agent harnesses from sparse threat evidence through an adversarial interplay between safety-specification generation and attack-case generation. Safety specifications guide harness updates toward addressing identified safety vulnerabilities, while attack cases probe for remaining safety vulnerabilities after each update. By feeding evaluation outcomes back into both processes, HASTE enables harness evolution against emerging attacks beyond the initially observed evidence. Experimental results across multiple backbone models, attack types, and evidence forms show that HASTE consistently reduces attack success rates while preserving benign-task utility. The code is available at https://github.com/xxiqiao/HASTE.

</details>


### [69] [Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Agent Safety False Alarm Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02925)
*Xichen Yan,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Guangyi Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 设计了面向监控器选择偏差的PU排序框架，通过信任感知监督、可靠性门控蒸馏与共识引导结构化精炼，在不需要报警标签且不更改原监控器的前提下，大幅提升误报检出效率与排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中安全监控器保守设定会产生大量误报，但报警评分中真假报警交织，手工核验成本高。通常只存在少量被监控器接受且已验证安全的轨迹（正例），而报警仍未标注，天然构成正-未标记（PU）排序问题；关键难点是监控器导致的选择偏差（observed positives与目标恢复的正例分布不匹配）。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) Trust-aware PU Supervision：将被监控器接受的安全参考（正样本）调整到报警（未标记）域，减少对可能的误报施加过强的负压；2) Reliability-gated Rank Distillation：从多个PU参考模型中提取一致的排序偏好并蒸馏为单一学生模型；随后通过Consensus-guided Structural Refinement利用分层安全参考支持、报警间关系和预测一致性进一步优化排序。整个流程不修改底层监控器、也不需要报警的安全标签。

Result: 在多种主流安全监控器上，方法实现宏观AUPRC=0.6444，较8个评估的PU基线高出5.27–16.98个百分点；与最强基线PULDA相比，在5%复审预算下可恢复多33.3%的误报。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能在无需报警标签的情况下显著提高对误报（false alarms）的检出率，并在主流安全监控器上实现了稳健且可量化的性能提升。

Abstract: Safety monitors help safeguard language-model agents interacting with external tools and environments, but conservative monitoring can generate many false alarms, consuming extensive review resources and weakening trust in alerts. Because false and genuine alarms often remain interleaved in native monitor scores, obtaining a reliable cutoff still requires substantial manual verification. In practice, a small set of verified-safe non-alarmed trajectories may be available while alarms remain unlabeled, naturally casting false-alarm auditing as a positive-unlabeled (PU) ranking problem. The key challenge is monitor-induced selection, since observed safe references are accepted by the monitor, while the hidden safe alarms of interest are precisely those it incorrectly flags, making the observed positives poorly representative of the positives to be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage framework in which Trust-aware PU Supervision adapts safe references toward the alarm domain and protects plausible false alarms from excessive negative pressure, while Reliability-gated Rank Distillation consolidates consistent ordering preferences from multiple PU reference models into a single student. Consensus-guided Structural Refinement then improves the student ranking using hierarchical safe-reference support, alarm relations, and predicted reference consensus. The framework requires no alarm safety labels for fitting and leaves the underlying monitor unchanged. Across mainstream safety monitors, our method achieves a macro AUPRC of $0.6444$, outperforming eight evaluated PU baselines by 5.27--16.98 absolute percentage points; compared with PULDA, the strongest evaluated PU baseline, it recovers 33.3% more false alarms at a 5% review budget.

</details>


### [70] [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932)
*Yulong Ming,Jie Xu,Zihan Wu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE 通过记录编译成功/失败和比较代理与程序执行成本，结合基于预算的在线算法，在不确定的编译成本与未来重用率下做出编译决策，既有理论竞争比保证又在仿真中显著降低了 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经常重复执行的 GUI 操作编译为程序能显著减少每次执行的 token 消耗，但编译本身成本存在不确定性（可能需修复甚至失败），且未来重用次数未知（任务停止或 GUI 漂移使程序失效），因此需要一种在不确定性下评估“回本”并在线决策的机制。

Method: 设计了一个测量协议来记录成功/失败的编译成本，并在匹配任务输入上比较代理（agent）与已编译程序的执行成本以估计单次节省和回收次数（payback）；在此基础上实施一个在线编译算法，基于观测到的任务到达和过去的编译结果将预期节省与包含失败尝试的编译成本比较，并使用基于累积预算的检查机制来限制执行与编译行为。

Result: 在假设的动作-成本条件下，系统保证每次到达后总成本至多为始终使用代理执行成本的 1+ε 倍。实验（含模拟历史任务到达）显示：成功编译尝试的估计回本次数（不含源代理运行）为 2–16 次；在 ε=0.25 时，与 ReAct、AutoRPA（改编）和 ToolPro（改编）相比，平均分别减少 token 成本 17.3%、24.9% 和 17.3%。

Conclusion: PACE 提供了一种基于经验的在线决策机制，在不确定的编译成本和未来重用率下判断何时将重复执行的 GUI 步骤编译为程序，从而在保证代价上界的同时降低总体 token 成本。

Abstract: Compiling GUI procedures that agents execute repeatedly into programs can reduce their token costs. However, measuring payback and deciding when to compile have two challenges. First, compilation costs are uncertain because attempts can require repair and still fail to produce a usable program. Second, future reuse is unknown because tasks may stop arriving or GUI drift may stop the program from working. To address these challenges, we propose PACE (Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience), a system with a measurement protocol and an online compilation algorithm. The measurement protocol records successful and failed compilation costs, and compares agent and program execution costs on matched task inputs to estimate per-use savings and payback counts. Using these measurements, the online algorithm compares estimated future savings with compilation costs, including failed attempts, based on past task arrivals and compilation outcomes. It checks execution and compilation charges against a cumulative budget determined by observed task arrivals before allowing either action. Under stated action-cost assumptions, total cost after each arrival is at most $1+ε$ times the cost of running every task with the agent. For successful compilation attempts, estimated payback counts excluding source agent runs are 2-16 uses. In simulations using recorded task arrivals, PACE reduces token costs by 17.3% compared with ReAct, 24.9% with the AutoRPA adaptation, and 17.3% with the ToolPro adaptation on average ($ε=0.25$).

</details>


### [71] [Continual Graph Memory for Mathematical Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02945)
*Junyi Zhang,Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Jiachen Lu,Zhi Zhang,Xinjie He,Hyunsik Chae,Ethan Ji,Alexander K Taylor,Vigyan Sahai,Yiwen Kou,Kai-Wei Chang,Raghu Meka,Nanyun Peng,Amit Sahai,Terence Tao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将所有探索痕迹显式编码为图记忆并支持依赖感知检索与证据驱动策展的长期数学研究代理Ansatz，展示了可持续、可恢复的长时程证明搜索能力，并在若干公开问题上取得实际解答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿数学问题需要大量并行智能体长期探索，产生海量中间结果。如何组织这些中间成果并在新的证明搜索中有效复用先前探索的知识是提升自动化数学研究效率的关键挑战。

Method: 构建统一的图记忆表示所有中间探索结果（事实、计划、反例）及其关系；采用依赖感知检索获取精确局部上下文；以证据敏感的策展器更新研究前沿并蒸馏经验；通过有范围的回溯（scoped recall）将先前陈述和消极发现用于局部再证明；多智能体并行探索并在图上记录轨迹以实现跨问题复用。

Result: 在实验中，Ansatz在“First Proof Second Batch”全部十个任务上实现闭合，另外自动给出Jamison毛毛虫猜想与Erdős问题289、348、488的解答，并在数个开放问题上获得部分进展；并通过四个组成研究验证模块作用。

Conclusion: Ansatz利用可持续演化的图式记忆（Continual Graph Memory）来组织和复用长时程证明搜索过程中的中间成果，从而能够维持并恢复长期数学搜索，最终在若干公开研究问题上取得解决或部分进展。

Abstract: Using frontier agent harnesses to tackle mathematical research problems has emerged as an effective means of advancing mathematics. However, solving frontier problems in mathematics may require a massive number of agents working in parallel for extended periods to construct proofs, thereby generating an enormous volume of intermediate proof results. Organizing these intermediate results throughout a long-horizon proof-search process and reusing knowledge gained from prior explorations remain major challenges. We present Ansatz, a mathematical research agent built around Continual Graph Memory, a graph-based, evolvable, cross-problem mathematical research memory system that explicitly organizes the entire proof search process and reuses information from exploration trajectories of previous problems. Specifically, we develop a unified graph memory that represents all intermediate exploration results, including facts, plans, and counterexamples, together with edges that explicitly represent the relationships among them; dependency-aware retrieval supplies precisely targeted local context; an evidence-sensitive curator updates the research frontier and distills lessons from prior attempts; and scoped recall surfaces earlier statements and negative findings for local re-proving rather than uncritical reuse. Experiments cover runs across all ten First Proof Second Batch problems, together with four component studies. Ansatz reports closure on all ten research tasks, demonstrating its ability to sustain and resume long-horizon mathematical search. Beyond these problems, Ansatz also produces solutions to the Jamison caterpillar conjecture and Erdős Problems 289, 348, and 488 without human intervention, and makes partial progress on several open problems, illustrating its strong ability to solve open mathematical research problems.

</details>


### [72] [Reasoning with Evidence, Not Merely Rationales: Verifiable Preference Proofs for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02968)
*Yu Hou,Nathaniel Kang,Pengkai Wang,Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可验证的LLM偏好推理框架（PROVE-REC）：先生成有证据链接的偏好证明，再仅基于该证明预测，配合掩蔽与删除验证，显著提升推荐准确性与解释可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能从交互历史与评论中推断用户偏好，但生成的推荐理由可能并非基于实际使用的信息或对最终排名无显著影响，导致可解释性与可验证性不足（称为 grounding-influence gap）。

Method: 两阶段架构：Pass A 从完整历史生成紧凑的偏好证明（正向与避免性声明，并关联选择的历史条目作为证据）；Pass B 仅使用该证明及关联证据进行下一项预测，防止模型绕过证明直接利用完整历史。并引入两类验证：通过掩蔽所选证据与对照条目比较来验证证据-声明的扎根性；通过删除某声明并测量目标项排名边际下降来验证声明对推荐的影响力；额外的排序保持目标用于保留历史中的有用信息。

Result: 在多种真实数据集上的全面实验证明，PROVE-REC 稳定超越强基线（顺序、生成式与LLM增强方法），最高提升可达7.45%。消融实验表明两阶段设计与验证目标有效；生成的声明在证据扎根性与对推荐的影响力上均优于对比方法，同时保持排序质量。

Conclusion: PROVE-REC通过将完整历史压缩为带证据链接的偏好证明，并用仅基于该证明的第二通预测，显著缩小了LLM推荐中的“证据-声明”与“声明-推荐”之间的差距，从而提升了推荐准确性与可验证性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can infer user preferences from interaction histories and reviews, yet the rationales they generate may not reflect the information actually used for recommendation. A preference claim may be weakly supported by its selected evidence, or may have little effect on the final ranking. We refer to these two failures as the grounding-influence gap. We introduce PROVE-REC, a general framework for verifiable preference reasoning in LLM-based recommendation. Pass A converts the complete pre-target history into a compact preference proof consisting of positive and avoidance claims linked to selected evidence entries. Pass B predicts the next item using only the proof and its selected evidence, preventing the recommender from bypassing the reasoning path. To verify evidence-to-proof grounding, we compare the effect of masking selected evidence with masking a comparable control entry. To verify proof-to-recommendation influence, we remove a preference claim and measure the resulting decrease in the target item's ranking margin. A ranking-preservation objective further retains useful information from the complete history. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that PROVE-REC consistently outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with improvements of up to 7.45%. Controlled ablations confirm the effectiveness of the two-pass architecture and verification objectives. Moreover, PROVE-REC produces claims that are more strongly grounded in historical evidence and more influential to recommendation while preserving ranking quality.

</details>


### [73] [CreateScore: Domain-Theory-Informed Bayesian Routing for LLM-Based CV Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02972)
*Rupsa Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreateScore 用一个手工 DAG+贝叶斯条件化把简历证据转成不确定性，按阈值把大部分决策交由本地 8B 处理以节省成本；节省显著但不确定性不能当作错误检测器，尚不可作为自治招聘系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在成本受限的场景下，避免对每个候选人与每个评分标准都调用高成本的高能力模型，同时保留类似“人工复核”的上升机制以控制错误。

Method: 手工构建的因域理论驱动 DAG，使用 Dirichlet-多项式 CPT 进行标准贝叶斯条件化；基于后验不确定性对每个评分标准做阈值路由（阈值在训练折上校准，目标：70% 本地解决率）；本地由 8B 模型判定，超限则将原始输入发给 120B 参考模型。

Result: 在 200 个合成数据科学简历（139 训练、61 测试、5 个评分标准）上：77.7% 的标准被本地解决；相较于始终使用 120B，路由减少了 65.2% 的令牌消耗；相较于 8B 单独使用，完全分数一致率从 32.8% 提升到 42.6%（95% CI 31.0-55.1%），但测试集规模导致增益在 n=61 时统计上不可区分。关键缺陷是后验不确定性不能有效预测 8B 的错误（AUROC 0.47）。并记录了早期评估被本地标签静默替换推翻的案例，提出级联评估的防护措施。

Conclusion: CreateScore 使用手工指定的有向无环图和 Dirichlet-多项式 条件概率表，将简历证据映射为后验不确定性：低不确定性由本地 8B 模型决策，高不确定性上升到 120B 参考模型。该方法能显著降低令牌使用并提高相对于 8B 的精确分数一致性，但不显著优于始终使用 120B 的参考条件；更加重要的是，不确定性信号未能有效识别 8B 的错误（AUROC≈0.47）。CreateScore 被定位为可审计的成本节约机制，而非能有针对性发现错误或自治的招聘系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support rubric-based screening of CVs, but applying a high-capability model to every candidate and criterion is costly. We present CreateScore, a domain-theory-informed Bayesian network for criterion-level LLM routing. A hand-specified directed acyclic graph with Dirichlet-multinomial conditional probability tables converts CV evidence into posterior uncertainty; low-uncertainty decisions are resolved by a local 8B model and uncertain ones are escalated to a 120B reference model. The graph is causally motivated, but the system performs standard Bayesian conditioning, not causal inference. The escalation threshold is calibrated on a training fold (target: 70% resolved locally) and then frozen. On 200 synthetic Data Science CVs (139 training and 61 test candidates, five criteria), 77.7% of criterion decisions were resolved locally (237 of 305). Relative to a reference condition in which the 120B model adjudicated every criterion, routed escalation reduced token use by 65.2% and raised exact score agreement from 32.8% (8B alone) to 42.6% (95% CI 31.0-55.1%); at n = 61 the gain was not statistically distinguishable. The uncertainty signal did not, however, identify the decisions on which the 8B model erred: disagreement with the reference was 16.2% among escalated and 19.4% among locally resolved decisions (AUROC 0.47, 95% CI 0.39-0.56), no better than random selection. We also document how an earlier evaluation was invalidated when truncated reasoning-model outputs were silently replaced by local labels, and we recommend safeguards for cascade evaluation. CreateScore is supported as an auditable cost-reduction mechanism, not yet as a targeted error detector, and is not an autonomous hiring system.

</details>


### [74] [Reliable Self-Evolution with Imperfect Proxy Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02975)
*Kangjun Noh,Soyu Kim,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CISE 通过基于条件保序的候选特定奖励区间与在线密度比估计，为自进化搜索提供保守且有置信保证的筛选策略，从而在验证预算有限的情形下优先产出更精确的候选集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一些科学发现任务中高保真评估代价极高，必须依赖低成本但有偏的代理奖励；这些代理会产生假阳性，污染输出与后续搜索指导，因而需要统计校正的奖励区间以提高可靠性。

Method: 使用条件合成推断（conditional conformal inference）构建候选特定的奖励区间，结合迭代式在线密度比估计（iteration-wise online density-ratio estimation）来应对分布漂移；将区间化的保守奖励用于进化搜索的反馈，并仅当所有目标属性的置信区间完全位于可行域时才返回候选。

Result: 在材料科学的三项自进化搜索任务上，CISE 返回的所有候选在高保真评估下均为真阳性；对比基线虽返回更多候选但包含假阳性。论文并在特定独立性与协变量漂移假设下给出固定迭代覆盖率证明。仓库已公开。

Conclusion: CISE 提出了一种基于置信区间的保守奖励机制，能在自进化搜索中显著降低代理奖励产生的假阳性并只返回在高保真评估下均为真阳性的候选。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based self-evolving search is a promising approach to scientific discovery. However, high-fidelity evaluation of every candidate is prohibitively expensive in some domains. Self-evolving systems in such settings therefore rely on low-cost but imperfect proxy rewards, which may assign high scores to infeasible candidates. These false positives may contaminate both the final output and the feedback used to guide subsequent generations. This motivates statistically calibrated reward intervals for more reliable self-evolving search. We propose Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE), which constructs candidate-specific reward intervals using conditional conformal inference and iteration-wise online density-ratio estimation. CISE uses conservative interval-based rewards for evolutionary feedback and returns candidates only when all required property intervals lie entirely within their respective feasible regions. We derive fixed-iteration coverage results under explicit assumptions of independence and covariate shift. We evaluate CISE on three self-evolving search tasks in materials science. In our experiments, all candidates returned by CISE are true positives under high-fidelity evaluation, whereas the baselines return more candidates but include false positives. These results highlight the value of a smaller, more precise shortlist when downstream validation budgets are limited. Our repository is available at https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git.

</details>


### [75] [Relevant Evidence Decoding for Audio-Visual Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02976)
*Hyunjae Ra,Aecheon Jung,Jungin Park,Sungeun Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RED是一种训练-free、基于PMI的证据选择与增强方法，能够按需放大问题相关模态信息，显著降低跨模态幻觉，兼具可解释性但带来少量延时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV-LLM在多模态推理时容易出现一种模态错误影响另一模态预测的跨模态幻觉；不同问题依赖不同感知证据（仅音频、仅视频或二者交互），直接联合推理反而可能削弱正确单模态证据的信心。

Method: 提出Relevant Evidence Decoding (RED)：用点互信息（PMI）衡量音频和视频对答案的增量预测支持，将联合贡献分解为音频、视频及相互作用三部分；先做问题-only推理判断需要的证据类型，再用对应的PMI分量增强原始音视频预测。

Result: 在三个音视频幻觉基准（CMM、AVHBench、SVHalluc）和三种AV-LLM上，RED比标准解码分别最多提升约7%、6.3%、3.8%的准确率；时间开销为标准解码的平均1.5倍（到第一个token）。

Conclusion: RED能在不需额外训练的前提下，通过识别并放大对问题有用的模态证据，显著减少音视频大模型的跨模态幻觉，提高多项基准上的准确率。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AV-LLMs) remain prone to cross-modal hallucinations, where one modality incorrectly affects predictions about another. Although contrastive decoding reduces hallucinations in vision-language models, its direct extension to AV-LLMs overlooks a key challenge: different questions require different perceptual evidence, including audio, video, or their interaction. Notably, we observe that joint audio-visual inference can weaken the prediction even when a model can recover the correct answer from a single informative modality. For example, when asked which instrument is heard, a model may correctly predict violin from the audio alone. Once a video showing a guitar is added, its confidence in violin may drop. In this paper, we introduce Relevant Evidence Decoding (RED), a training-free method that identifies question-relevant evidence and selectively strengthens its contribution. RED uses pointwise mutual information to quantify the predictive support provided by audio and video beyond the question alone. It decomposes their joint contribution into audio, video, and residual interaction components. A question-only inference pass determines the required evidence type, after which the model augments the original audio-visual prediction with the corresponding PMI contribution. Across three audio-visual hallucination benchmarks and three AV-LLMs, RED improves accuracy over standard decoding by up to 7% on CMM, 6.3% on AVHBench, and 3.8% on SVHalluc, with an average relative time to first token of 1.5x standard decoding.

</details>


### [76] [RASPER: Reward-Aligned Summarization of Clinical Notes for EHR Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02979)
*Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Chen Chen,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出通过强化学习以下游预测损失为奖励的任务对齐摘要器（RASPER），并用结构化码生成软提示进行背景接入，在MIMIC数据集上显著提升再入院与用药推荐的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子病历中的非结构化出院记录携带补充性、个体化的证据，但原始文本冗长噪声大，且通用摘要常为流畅性优化而非保留决策性证据，导致下游预测性能下降。需要一种面向预测任务的摘要方法。

Method: 提出一个可调节的基于大模型的摘要器，用强化学习优化摘要策略；使用下游预测器的损失作为奖励信号；以纵向编码器将结构化病历编码为软提示，向摘要器提供患者的临床背景，从而鼓励摘要保留对预测有增量价值的信息而非重复结构化信息。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上的再入院预测与用药推荐任务中，RASPER在多个强基线之上持续提升性能，表明对下游任务对齐的摘要能有效挖掘笔记中的补充证据。

Conclusion: RASPER通过将摘要器的训练目标对齐到下游临床预测任务的损失，能够在保留与结构化代码互补的病历证据的同时，显著提升多模态（结构化代码+文本摘要）预测性能。

Abstract: Unstructured discharge notes in Electronic Health Records (EHRs) often carry signal complementary to structured medical codes, holding patient-specific evidence that standardized cohort-level codes alone cannot capture. However, this evidence in notes is frequently buried in lengthy, noisy text that is not intentionally written with any specific clinical prediction in mind. Summarization is an obvious mitigation, but generic summaries, tuned for fluency rather than the outcome, routinely omit decisive evidence while retaining plausible but uninformative detail. To this end, we propose RASPER, a Reward-Aligned Summarizer for Prediction in EHR, that optimizes note summarization directly against the downstream clinical task. RASPER employs a tunable LLM-based summarizer to extract task-relevant evidence from discharge notes and trains it via reinforcement learning from prediction feedback, using a reward derived from the downstream predictor's loss. To ground the summarizer, a longitudinal encoder converts structured codes into soft prompts that incorporate each patient's clinical context into note summarization. By rewarding the quality of the resulting multimodal prediction, RASPER encourages the summarizer to retain patient-specific evidence that complements, rather than duplicates, information captured by structured codes. RASPER consistently outperforms strong baselines on both readmission prediction and medication recommendation across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV.

</details>


### [77] [Safeguarding Mutual Correction in Source-Free Domain Adaptation via Cut Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02981)
*Seongjun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于 cut 统计量的可靠性门控机制（SafeCut），在无源域适配中实现安全的双向跨模型监督，从而缓解确认偏差并取得最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模型 SFDA 易陷入确认偏差且难以纠正自身系统性错误；虽然引入视觉-语言模型作为外部知识有助，但现有方法多为单向监督，未利用两模型互补的失败模态。如何在无标签设置下安全地实现互相纠正是主要动机。

Method: 提出 SafeCut：用 cut 统计量作为无标签的预测可靠性度量，按样本评估两个模型（源预训练模型与视觉-语言模型）相对可靠性，并基于该可靠性动态调节监督的方向与强度，选择性放大正确纠正并抑制错误传播；并给出理论证明保证净正向纠错信号。

Result: 在多种 SFDA 基准上，SafeCut 达到或优于现有最先进水平。实验展示了可靠性门控能有效放大真正的纠正并降低误纠正，且理论分析支持方法的净正向改进。

Conclusion: SafeCut 表明在无源域适配（SFDA）中，通过基于 cut 统计量的无标签可靠性度量对跨模型监督进行门控，可以安全地实现双向互相纠正，从而显著降低单模型自我确认偏差并提升适配效果。

Abstract: Source-Free Domain Adaptation (SFDA) aims to adapt a source-pretrained model to an unlabeled target domain without access to the original source domain. While early single-model approaches rely on self-refinement, they are inherently susceptible to confirmation bias and struggle to correct their own systematic errors. To overcome this limitation, recent methods introduce Vision-Language (ViL) models as external knowledge sources. However, these approaches operate in a largely unidirectional paradigm, using the ViL model primarily to supervise the source-pretrained model. This overlooks a key structural property: the two models exhibit distinct failure modes -- where one produces an incorrect prediction, the other may produce a correct one, creating a natural opportunity for mutual correction within the target domain. Yet, without ground-truth labels, identifying which model is correct on any given sample is non-trivial, and naively exchanging predictions risks propagating errors across models. To address this challenge, we propose SafeCut, a novel approach that leverages the cut statistic as a label-free measure of prediction reliability to gate cross-model supervision. Our approach dynamically controls both the direction and strength of supervision based on relative reliability, selectively amplifying true corrections while suppressing miscorrections on a per-sample basis. We further provide theoretical justification showing that this reliability-gated mechanism guarantees a net-positive correction signal. Extensive experiments across diverse SFDA benchmarks demonstrate that SafeCut achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of safeguarding mutual correction in SFDA via cut statistics.

</details>


### [78] [PLCWorld: Benchmarking LLM-Generated PLC Programs in Closed-Loop Plant Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02982)
*Yunji Kim,Yunseok Lee,Hyunwoo Seo,Jaerim Choi,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向PLC的闭环仿真基准PLCWorld，用以评估LLM生成的PLC程序在任务完成性与安全性上的表现，包含详尽验证并公开数据与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM生成的PLC代码仅依赖静态或片段式检查，无法观测命令对设备和工件状态的闭环影响，难以准确判断任务满足性与安全性。

Method: 构建基于工程控制关系的闭环仿真平台，提供100个合成任务与473个已登记的任务-条件对，按照控制依赖范围定义难度；采用参考程序、替代程序、定向反例、从业者审查、规范求值器一致性检查、以及与独立ST运行时比较来验证平台和评估器的正确性。

Result: PLCWorld可重现性强：参考与替代程序在其适用情形下通过验证；542个定向反例在至少一个注册条件下触发对应评估规则。实测表明GPT-5.5在简单案例上任务成功率高（82.70%），但在困难案例上显著下降（25.10%）；进一步比较了六个LLM与四种生成—验证工作流在完成度、安全性与生成成本上的差异。

Conclusion: 本文提出了PLCWorld，一个用于评估LLM生成PLC程序的闭环执行基准与仿真环境，能够将ST程序执行与受控对象的反馈耦合，从而以任务成功与安全违规两条线量化评估。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) coordinate industrial equipment by reading sensor inputs and issuing control commands. Evaluating whether large language model (LLM)-generated PLC programs satisfy task requirements and safety constraints requires observing how their commands affect device and workpiece states. We introduce PLCWorld, a common closed-loop execution environment and benchmark that couples Structured Text (ST) execution with simulated plant responses and sensor feedback. Grounded in control relations identified in industrial PLC programs and engineering documentation, PLCWorld contains 100 synthetic tasks and 473 registered task-condition pairs across Motion Control and Material Handling, with difficulty defined by control-dependency scope. A common protocol reports Task Success and Safety Violation separately. Validation combines practitioner review, reference and alternative programs, targeted counterexamples, specification-evaluator alignment checks, and comparisons with independent ST runtimes. Reference and alternative programs satisfy their applicable cases, while all 542 targeted counterexamples activate their designated evaluator rules under at least one registered condition. Execution Gap relates submission-profile acceptance to subsequent task failure or observed Safety Violation. Across the constructed task groups, direct GPT-5.5 achieves 82.70% Task Success on Easy cases but 25.10% on Hard cases. Evaluations of six LLMs and four adapted generation-and-verification workflows further expose differences between completion, safety, and generation cost. Our code, simulation environment, benchmark tasks, and baseline implementations are publicly available at https://yunji0516.github.io/PLCWorld/.

</details>


### [79] [Personalized Automatic Speech Recognition for a Dysarthric and Tracheostomic Speaker using Artificial Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03017)
*David Nadrchal,Monorama Swain,Florian Schmid,Gerhard Widmer,Paul Primus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A personalized, multi-stage fine-tuned Whisper Base system trained on a novel 33-hour Czech dataset (with simulated augmentation) cuts CER by ~50% for a tracheostomized, severely dysarthric speaker and shows practical promise in real-time dialogue scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Speaker has permanent tracheal stoma and severe dysarthria making speech unintelligible to untrained listeners; existing ASR struggles and there is a lack of public data for such pathology; goal is to create a practical, near real-time personalized ASR and release data to support research.

Method: Collected 33 hours of annotated speech using an "artificial conversation" protocol; proposed a multi-stage fine-tuning pipeline based on Whisper Base: (1) fine-tune on standard Czech speech, (2) fine-tune on acoustically simulated tracheostomic speech, and (3) fine-tune on the target speaker's data. Evaluated across three near real-time scenarios: scripted conversations, question answering, and spontaneous dialogue.

Result: Achieved 50% relative reduction in Character Error Rate compared to Whisper Base baseline; outperformed average recognition accuracy of the speaker's assistants on isolated utterances; demonstrated qualitative usefulness and positive speaker feedback.

Conclusion: Personalized ASR enables useful recognition for a Czech speaker with a permanent tracheal stoma and severe dysarthria; the proposed system achieves substantial improvement over Whisper Base and yields positive user feedback.

Abstract: This work presents an automatic speech recognition (ASR) system personalized for a Czech speaker with a permanent tracheal stoma and severe dysarthria rendering their speech unintelligible to untrained listeners. We release a public dataset containing 33 annotated hours of the speaker's speech, collected using a novel "artificial conversation" protocol designed for high engagement and dialogue realism. We propose a multi-stage training pipeline based on Whisper Base: fine-tuning on standard Czech speech, acoustically simulated tracheostomic speech, and the speaker's data. We evaluate the system across three near real-time scenarios: scripted conversations, question answering, and spontaneous dialogue, achieving a 50\% relative reduction in Character Error Rate compared to Whisper Base baseline and surpassing the average recognition accuracy of their assistants in acoustic recognition of isolated utterances. We demonstrate that even for severely impeded speech, a helpful ASR is achievable, as evidenced by the quantitative results and the feedback from the speaker.

</details>


### [80] [DyadMem: A Long-Term Memory Benchmark of How Agents Work with Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03020)
*Yifei Tao,Xinyu Zhong,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Fanyi Wang,Tengda Guo,Wentao Qiu,Ying Wang,Liujian Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DyadMem是一个针对关系性代理记忆（URAM）的全流程、多会话基准与标注集，揭示了现有模型在记忆捕获/回忆/删除上的弱点，并证明URAM定义与细粒度评测能有效改进模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要监督可复用的用户事实或偏好，忽视了随共享历史演化的‘关系性’代理记忆；且多以最终答案的QA为唯一评测，无法细粒度、端到端地评估记忆的捕获、更新、回忆与删除过程。

Method: 构建并标注DyadMem数据集：沿多会话轨迹并行标注用户侧事实记忆与关系性代理记忆（URAM），划分6类记忆；提供会话级的Capture与Update金标注、查询级Recall支持；设计两种QA评测设置（Gold-Memory与Full-Pipeline）；在16个开源与4个专有模型上进行基准测试与消融验证以评估URAM效用。

Result: 数据集规模为3,065个episode、50,961个session与61,210个QA实例。实验结果显示：在Gold-Memory条件下模型表现普遍较好，但在Full-Pipeline条件下性能显著下降，表明记忆提取与管理环节为瓶颈；统计揭示低Capture召回、不完整的Recall与危险的删除行为；URAM的引入在20个模型上均有正向影响。

Conclusion: DyadMem提出了User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory（URAM）的新定义，并发布了一个详细标注的多会话全流程记忆基准，展示了在金记忆（Gold-Memory）与完整流水线（Full-Pipeline）评测之间存在显著性能差距，且当前最先进的LLM在记忆捕获、回忆与删除安全性上仍有明显不足。

Abstract: Long-term agents must remember not only what is true about a user, but also how a particular agent should work with that user as their shared history evolves. Existing benchmarks primarily supervise user facts and preferences or experience reusable across users, leaving this relationship-specific agent memory implicit. Additionally, most prior works measure the model solely with final-answer QA over long interaction histories, making the assessment still incomplete and unreliable. To this end, we introduce DyadMem with the proposed new definition User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM). DyadMem jointly annotates user-side memory and URAM along the same multi-session trajectories, resulting in 6 memory categories. To summarize, it includes 3,065 episodes, 50,961 sessions, and 61,210 QA instances, with extensive session-level Capture and Update gold annotations, query-level Recall support, and two QA settings: Gold-Memory and Full-Pipeline. Across 16 open-weight and 4 proprietary models, Gold-Memory QA is consistently strong, yet Full-Pipeline QA drops sharply. Such a gap explicitly supports our fine-grained evaluation design. Additionally, several quantitative results further reveal low Capture recall, incomplete Recall, and unsafe-deletion issues arising from even the frontier LLMs. We further conduct a rigorous experiment to validate the effectiveness of our URAM and observe the positive effects for all 20 models. In summary, DyadMem is a dual-domain, full-pipeline memory benchmark with extensive annotation efforts for advancing the domain's development.

</details>


### [81] [Verifiable, Articulable, and Tacit Components of Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03025)
*Alexander Spangher,Sheldon Huang,Andreas Haupt,Noah D. Goodman,Diyi Yang,Daniel E. Ho,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大规模偏好数据集（CreativePreferences，2.8M 文本、317M 判断）揭示人类偏好含大量不可或缺的“默会”成分。用可执行验证器、示范式 rubric 和稠密训练模型对标签建模，发现稠密模型与人类偏好更一致，但存在可表述性与可验证性缺口（并随评判人数增加而扩大）。提出检索可表述/可验证指标与用捕获-再捕获估计未发现指标的测量方法，并给出何时自动化、何时保留人工判断的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many important quality judgments (e.g., story “grippingness”, newsworthiness, elegance) are partly tacit and resist precise articulation, yet modern AI alignment and improvement methods rely on explicit rubrics and verifiers. The work aims to quantify how much of human preference is articulable or verifiable versus tacit, and to derive implications for learning and deployment.

Method: Collected and released CreativePreferences: 2.8M texts with 317M human preference judgments across seven creative/evaluative domains and 42 benchmark tasks. Modeled labels using three classes of artifacts: executable verifiers (V), rubric banks (A), and densely trained models (VAT). Developed a measurement pipeline that (1) discovers articulable/verifiable metrics, (2) identifies spurious variables, and (3) estimates undiscovered metrics’ contribution via a capture–recapture statistical approach. Measured gaps by comparing VAT performance to combinations of V and A and computed upper/lower bounds for articulability and verifiability.

Result: Empirically demonstrated robust articulability and verifiability gaps in all analyzed domains (including math/software and news/peer review). Found largest articulability gaps in peer review and creative writing. Gaps widen with increasing numbers of judges, consistent with collective tacit knowledge. Showed that VAT models match human aggregate preferences better than articulated/verifiable proxies, and that formalizing preferences changes what is optimized (Goodhart effect). Presented practical recommendations for prompting, learning strategy, and when to keep humans in the loop.

Conclusion: Paper concludes that many human preferences—especially in creative and evaluative domains—contain substantial tacit components that cannot be fully captured by articulated rubrics or executable verifiers. Large-scale preference models (VAT) better align with aggregate human judgments than articulated/verifiable proxies, but there exist measurable articulability (VAT–VA) and verifiability (VAT–V) gaps across domains. These gaps vary by domain, grow as more judges participate, and have practical consequences for model design and deployment (including Goodhart-like shifts when preferences are formalized). The authors provide guidelines on when to automate judgments and when to retain human decision-makers.

Abstract: What makes a short story gripping; a news article newsworthy; or a math proof elegant? These constructs resist articulation or verification; their meaning is at least partially tacit. However, modern AI models are improved primarily via articulated constitutions, rubrics and verifiers (i.e. in RLAIF and RLVR); tacit components of preferences are typically understudied. We introduce a large, labeled preference dataset CreativePreferences, containing 2.8M texts labeled by 317M human preference judgments across 7 creative domains, with 42 benchmark tasks. We model these labels with executable programs, rubric banks and densely trained models (V, A and VAT, respectively). We observe robust articulability gaps, VAT-VA; and verifiability gaps, VAT-V; we estimate upper and lower bounds for each gap with a novel measurement approach that discovers articulable and verifiable metrics, identifies spurious variables and estimates the value of undiscovered metrics using capture-recapture. These gaps occur across all domains, even in domains traditionally treated as fully verifiable: correctness-centered domains (i.e. mathematics and software engineering) and claim- and novelty-centric domains (i.e. news, patents, peer review). The size of the gap varies based on domain (e.g. peer review and creative writing have the largest articulability gaps) and widens as more people take part in the judgment, consistent with Collins' collective tacit knowledge. We show two consequences: (1) on human generations, the full model more closely matches human preferences, often in disagreement with articulated criteria, and (2) in an analogy to Goodhart's law, articulating preference shifts it away from the tacit dimension. Articulability and verifiability gaps are consequential; we give recommendations on when tasks can be prompted; how learning mechanisms might improve; and when to leave judgments with humans.

</details>


### [82] [SoftGene: Protein Language Model-Enhanced Soft Prompting for Interpretable Gene Set Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03029)
*Drew Ross,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftGene结合ESM序列嵌入与混合prompt策略，用于基因集注释，整体性能提升但跨域贡献不均，需更多消融与可解释性分析以验证广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的基因集注释主要依赖符号基因名与文本信息，缺乏对决定分子功能的蛋白质序列层面结构信息的建模；希望通过序列表征补足结构性生物学信息，提升注释质量。

Method: 提出SoftGene：用基于ESM的分层注意力编码器把基因集合表示为蛋白质氨基酸序列信息的嵌入；构造混合提示（soft prompts来自基因集合嵌入，hard prompts为由LLM生成的辅助文本上下文），并将混合提示输入本地LLM进行注释。

Result: 在GO与MSigDB两套基准数据集上，SoftGene总体上优于仅文本或仅符号方法；但按域（例如生物过程、分子功能、细胞组分）分析时，蛋白质嵌入的贡献存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 将蛋白质序列表征与文本提示相结合，可改进基因集注释效果，但不同生物学域的改进幅度存在差异。

Abstract: Gene set analysis is a cornerstone of functional genomics, yet it remains labor-intensive and heavily dependent on manual curation and expert biological interpretation. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for genomic reasoning and annotation, most existing approaches rely on symbolic gene names and fail to capture domain-specific biological structure, particularly protein sequence information that governs molecular activity, interactions, and downstream gene function. In this work, we propose SoftGene, a novel framework for LLM-based gene set annotation that leverages the hierarchical structure of gene sets. First, we use a hierarchical attention-based encoder built on ESM, a protein language model, to represent each gene set using protein-level amino acid sequence information. Second, we construct a hybrid prompting scheme that combines soft prompts derived from gene set embeddings with hard prompts containing auxiliary context generated by an LLM, and feed the resulting prompt into a local LLM for annotation. We evaluate our framework on two benchmark datasets: Gene Ontology (GO) and the Molecular Signatures Database (MSigDB). Our results show that integrating protein-sequence representations with textual context improves gene set annotation overall, while per-domain analyses reveal that the contribution of protein embeddings varies across biological domains.

</details>


### [83] [When Numbers Start Talking: Numerical Signalling and Strategic Behaviour Among LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03033)
*Alessio Buscemi,Daniele Proverbio,Alessandro Di Stefano,The Anh Han,German Castignani,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在四个博弈与四个LLM代理的实验中，结构化消息显著影响合作与收益；数值通信在有意图指令下呈现可检测的非随机模式，但难以人类解读。监测代理协调应聚焦消息指纹而非仅看行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，随着基于LLM的代理越来越多地通过交流进行策略互动，亟需理解不同通信形式是否以及如何影响战略博弈结果，并探寻可用于监测或检测代理协调的信号。

Method: 对四种流行的大型语言模型驱动的AI代理，在四类具有不同合作均衡的博弈中进行对抗性实验，比较不同类型的消息（自然语言、数值信号、随机序列）与代理人格设定对合作水平与收益的影响；分析消息分布、与收益的关联性及重复交互下的集中性。

Result: 结构化消息通常会改变博弈结果，但影响方向和幅度依赖于具体博弈与模型；数值消息在被指示通信时最为非随机并且与支付结构相关，且重复交互会使符号分布趋于集中；行为层面的变化在不同模型间不易泛化，而消息层面的指纹更具跨模型泛化性。

Conclusion: 结构化消息会改变大多数博弈与大模型代理的最终收益，但没有可预测的一致模式；数值消息在明确要求通信时明显偏离随机分布且与收益结构相关，但对人类解释困难；因此，应优先通过消息层面的指纹检测代理协调，而非仅看决策行为。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly operate in multi-agent systems (MAS) characterised by strategic interaction. However, little is known about whether, and to what extent, different types of messages affect the outcomes of strategic games. By investigating AI agents based on four popular LLMs, playing four games with different cooperation equilibria, we study whether messages of different kinds (natural language, numerical signals, or random sequences) significantly modify the levels of cooperation in each game, also depending on the agents' assigned personalities. We observe that structured messages alter the final payoffs for most games and LLMs, but without a predictable pattern; this challenges the assumption that AI agents can converge to stable equilibria regardless of additional capabilities. Moreover, we observe that agent-generated numerical messages depart from randomness, most strongly and consistently when agents are explicitly instructed to communicate; however, they introduce an additional interpretability challenge, as their symbol distributions are mostly associated with the payoff structure and typically become more concentrated with repetition, but are overall difficult for humans to interpret. Monitoring for coordination of AI agents through restricted channels should thus prioritise message-level fingerprints, which generalise across models, over behavioural decisions, which do not.

</details>


### [84] [hacktrace: behavior-supervised detection of reward hacking during code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03055)
*Hao Jiang,Xin Li,Annan Wang,Yichi Zhang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过标注并监督‘捷径’行为，利用生成时内部状态构建轻量级监控器HACKTRACE，可在极小延迟下高精度检测并通过RL惩罚显著降低编码代理的作弊率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理可以通过修复代码或删除/修改暴露漏洞的测试来获得通过分数，检测并抑制这种‘捷径’或奖励规避需要识别尝试中的作弊行为（包括未成功的尝试），而不是仅依赖最终是否成功利用漏洞。

Method: 发布173,561条来自Qwen3-8B的多轮编码轨迹并进行注释；提出HACKTRACE监控器，该监控器读取模型生成过程中已有的内部状态（无需额外token或重复调用），并将这些时序性生成状态与最终文件的静态特征结合用于分类检测；在强化学习中利用这些信号作为惩罚（GRPO）以减少作弊解。

Result: 行为监督独立于利用是否成功的标注显著提升检测效果；HACKTRACE在8 ms监控开销下实现平均每题AUC=0.997，优于对模型重新询问‘诚实性’的监控方式；在RL训练中配合强GRPO惩罚，将通过解中作弊比例从82–91%降至1–5%，同时保留诚实且正确的解，且检测器随着策略演化仍能保持高准确度。

Conclusion: 针对代码代理的奖励规避（reward hacking）问题，本文提出并验证了通过行为监督（特别是针对‘捷径’行为而非仅成功利用的标签）结合生成时内部状态监控来检测和抑制作弊的有效方法。

Abstract: A coding agent can earn a passing grade by fixing its code, or by deleting the test that exposes the bug. Detecting such reward hacking requires recognizing attempted shortcuts, including those that fail. We release 173,561 annotated multi-turn coding trajectories from Qwen3-8B and show that supervising shortcut behavior independently of exploit success substantially improves detection. We introduce HACKTRACE, a behavior-supervised monitor that reads the internal states the agent already computes while generating code. Reusing these states enables monitoring before a turn is complete, without additional language-model tokens or passes. Combining this evidence with static features of the final files achieves a mean per-problem AUC of 0.997 with 8 ms of monitoring overhead, improving both accuracy and latency over monitors that run the model again on an honesty question and answer. The same generation states also provide an inexpensive monitoring signal for reinforcement learning. With strong GRPO penalties, HACKTRACE reduces the cheating share of passing solutions from 82-91% to 1-5%, while retaining honest, correct solutions and maintaining high detection accuracy as the policy evolves. Our results show that both the supervision target and the source of monitoring evidence matter for turning accurate detection into a useful training signal.

</details>


### [85] [MOF-VERIFY: A Failure-Aware Agentic Harness for MOF Hypothesis Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03056)
*Donghyun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Geonhee Ahn,Jieun Kim,Jihyun Park,Suyeon Cho,Yoona Kim,Chaerim Shin,Hoi Ri Moon,Jonggeol Na,Sukho Hong,Jihwan Oh,Soo Kyung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduces a diagnostic benchmark for MOF hypothesis verification and MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agent that significantly improves LLM-based verification by addressing grounding, retrieval, evidence sufficiency, and computation bottlenecks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models are being used as reasoning components in AI-driven materials co-scientists, but MOF verification is challenging due to ambiguous identifiers, condition-dependent syntheses, scattered heterogeneous evidence, and the need for computational validation beyond literature search.

Method: Design of a diagnostic benchmark (T-MOF-1–3 for grounding, synthesis-condition, and evidence-sufficiency; T-MOF-4 for MLIP-based computational checks) evaluated under closed-book, retrieval-enabled, and oracle-evidence settings to localize failures; development of MOF-Verify that applies targeted checks (structural grounding, literature retrieval, evidence-sufficiency assessment, computational evaluation) before producing final verdicts; empirical comparison across backbone LLMs and baselines.

Result: MOF-Verify notably outperforms direct and retrieval-based baselines on the introduced benchmark tasks; datasets and benchmark code are publicly released to enable reproducibility and follow-up research.

Conclusion: MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agentic verification pipeline, systematically addresses structural, literature, evidence-sufficiency, and computation bottlenecks in MOF hypothesis verification and substantially improves verification accuracy across multiple backbone LLMs compared to direct inference and retrieval baselines.

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as reasoning components in AI-driven materials Co-Scientists, yet the reliability of the resulting verification pipeline remains unclear. Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) provide a particularly challenging setting because structures may appear under different identifiers, synthesis outcomes depend strongly on experimental conditions, evidence is distributed across heterogeneous sources, and some hypotheses require computation rather than literature alone. We introduce a diagnostic benchmark with four task families covering structural grounding, synthesis-condition verification, evidence-sufficiency verification, and MLIP-based computational verification. T-MOF-1-3 are evaluated under closed-book, retrieval-enabled, and oracle-evidence settings to localize failures in knowledge access, evidence acquisition, and reasoning, while T-MOF-4 separately evaluates computational verification. Guided by these diagnosed failure modes, we develop MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agentic harness that targets structural, literature, evidence-sufficiency, and computational bottlenecks before producing a final verdict. Across multiple backbone LLMs, MOF-Verify substantially improves hypothesis-verification performance over direct inference and retrieval-based baselines. Benchmark datasets are released at https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark.

</details>


### [86] [RIFAR: Reliability and Forgetting-Aware Replay for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03079)
*Zirong Song,Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIFAR combines inverse-dynamics-based reliability screening and drift-aware trajectory selection to make world-action-model generative replay both compact and effective, showing strong empirical gains in continual embodied learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generative replay can reconstruct past experience cheaply, but predicted visual rollouts may not correspond to executable actions and adaptation to new tasks may degrade previously learned behavior. The goal is to maintain reliable, compact replay that preserves past capabilities during continual learning.

Method: Reconstruct trajectories from compact demonstration prefixes using a world-action model; use a frozen inverse-dynamics model to screen trajectories for action-visual consistency; train in two stages—first with current demonstrations plus top-quality screened trajectories, then measure normalized change in action predictions (drift) on identical historical inputs and continue training with trajectories showing large drift from the screened pool.

Result: On three LIBERO suites and real-world tests, RIFAR outperforms prior WAM-based generative replay approaches. On LIBERO-Goal it reaches 90.97 AUC while storing only 320 historical time steps per task (~4.9% of the steps used by storing 50 full demonstrations).

Conclusion: RIFAR effectively improves generative replay for continual embodied learning by screening generated trajectories for action-visual consistency and then prioritizing trajectories with high post-adaptation action drift, enabling compact storage while preserving past skills.

Abstract: Genuine embodied agency requires robots to turn continuous real-world experience into lasting, transferable skills. This demands continual learning that integrates new capabilities without eroding prior knowledge as tasks and environments evolve. Experience replay mitigates forgetting, but storing complete demonstrations becomes costly as tasks accumulate. World-action models offer a generative alternative, reconstructing past experience through joint predictions of actions and future observations. However, visually coherent rollouts may contain actions that cannot realize the predicted transitions, while new-task adaptation can disrupt previously learned behavior. RIFAR therefore combines reliability screening with drift-aware replay selection. It reconstructs trajectories from compact demonstration prefixes and uses a frozen inverse-dynamics model to assess action-visual consistency. Training first combines current demonstrations with the highest-quality screened trajectories. RIFAR then compares action predictions before and after this adaptation on identical historical inputs, reselecting trajectories with larger normalized drift from the same screened pool for continued training. Across three LIBERO suites and real-world experiments, RIFAR surpasses the previous state of the art in WAM-based generative replay. On LIBERO-Goal, it achieves 90.97 AUC while retaining only 320 historical time steps per task, approximately 4.9% of the steps retained using 50-demonstration replay.

</details>


### [87] [Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03095)
*Frida Nøhr Laustsen,Marie Haahr Petersen,Victoria Popa,Ariel Flint,Romualdo Pastor-Satorras,Andrea Baronchelli,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一次性交换中，说服强且不可预测——听者特性与配对关系决定交互结果，不能仅凭模型规模或独立置信度推断交互行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统日益将不同家族和规模的语言模型混合使用时，理解模型间谁能说服谁决定了哪些判断会在交互后存活，关系到系统可靠性与责任分配。

Method: 将“说服”定义为在一次与持异议同伴交换后，接收者决策的概率性转变；在三个语言理解任务上测试7个开源权重语言模型的成对交互，分析独立决策一致性、规模、解释性回答与相互影响的关系。

Result: 发现说服普遍存在：模型间分歧时，接收者常放弃初始判断；但模型独立置信度和规模并不能可靠预测谁会说服谁；转变幅度更依赖听者的易受影响性；说服模式高度依配对而异，异质性在某些组合放大说服，在另一些组合抑制它。

Conclusion: 交互中模型的行为不可从单独性能推断；说服效果主要由“听者”对抗性决定，而非“说话者”的规模或独立置信度；模型配对的异质性能显著放大或抑制说服，导致小模型在某些组合里能推翻大模型的判断。

Abstract: When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.

</details>


### [88] [Predictor-Guided Latent Space Codon Optimization for Maximizing Protein Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03098)
*Alberto Caron,Tianyu Cui,Dmytro S. Lituiev,Mangal Prakash,Artem Moskalev,Amina Mollaysa,Bo Zhai,Hirsh Nanda,Daniel M. Poole,Zhongyin Liu,Iman Farasat,Robert Davidson,Nikolay V. Manyakov,Tommaso Mansi,Scott Oloff,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将密码子序列嵌入预训练 mRNA 模型的潜在空间，结合不确定性感知的表达目标、MFE 正则化和自然性先验，进行潜在空间梯度优化，从而在抗体表达任务上超越传统和深度生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码子优化是个离散、组合爆炸的问题，阻碍了基于梯度的高效搜索；现有基于启发式指标（如 CAI、GC 含量）的工具无法准确反映真实表达，因此需要一个数据驱动且可微的方法将离散设计空间转为连续以利用现代优化手段。

Method: 用预训练 mRNA 语言模型将密码子序列映射到潜在空间，在该空间上对一个由不确定性感知预测器构建的数据驱动表达目标做梯度优化；同时加入最小自由能（MFE）正则化以维持二级结构稳定性，利用蛋白到密码子的反向翻译模型作为自然性先验，并在解码时加约束以保证氨基酸序列不变。

Result: 在真实湿实验抗体表达数据集上，LSCO 在预测表达（由不确定性感知预测器评估）方面优于简单的频率基线和现代深度生成基线，并在 MFE/其他生物物理指标上保持合格表现，证明了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: LSCO 将离散的密码子优化问题重映射到预训练 mRNA 语言模型的连续潜在空间，使得可用梯度方法高效搜索，实验证明在抗体表达数据集上，LSCO 在预测表达量上优于基线方法且保持合适的生物物理性质。

Abstract: Codon optimization, the process of selecting synonymous codons to improve mRNA translation efficiency and protein expression, is central to therapeutic protein production and mRNA vaccines, yet it remains a hard problem. The design space is discrete and combinatorially large, precluding gradient-based methods, and existing tools rely on heuristic proxies (e.g., Codon Adaptation Index or GC-content) that poorly capture true expression. We introduce Latent-Space Codon Optimization (LSCO), which recasts this discrete problem as a continuous one by mapping sequences into the latent space of a pretrained mRNA language model, enabling efficient gradient-based search. LSCO combines four components: a data-driven expression objective from an uncertainty-aware predictor, a Minimum-Free-Energy regularizer for structural stability, a naturalness prior from a protein-to-codon back-translation model, and constrained decoding for protein fidelity. On a real-world, wet-lab antibody expression dataset, LSCO outperforms simple frequency-based, as well as modern deep generative baselines in predicted expression, while retaining suitable biophysical properties.

</details>


### [89] [Trading Strategy Optimization via Textual Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03128)
*Chaoqun Yang,Qian Wang,Fengbin Zhu,Xinyu Lin,Bingsheng He,Roger Zimmermann,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TradeGrad通过经验驱动的文本梯度估计、多尺度改写与强调弱周期表现的目标函数，缓解了LLM文本梯度的短视与时间不稳健问题，显著提升了量化策略的稳健性与收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用LLM生成的文本梯度存在两个问题：一是优化过程短视，未充分利用以往评估经验；二是基于整体回测的反馈忽略了不同历史时期的稳健性，可能导致仅在特定区间表现良好的策略被误选。

Method: 基于LLM的文本梯度框架上，TradeGrad累积历史优化经验来估计更可靠的文本梯度，采用多尺度（粗粒度→细粒度）改写机制进行策略探索与细化，并引入Cross-Period Robust Objective（CPRO），在目标函数中强调在不利历史区间的表现以提升时间稳健性。

Result: 在中美两地（中国A股与美股）的横截面与时间序列策略设计四种设置中，TradeGrad在样本内与样本外均取得最优表现。典型结果为：中国横截面年化收益27.99%、最大回撤12.19%、夏普1.63，较CSI 300回报高约68%。进一步消融实验验证了各组件的有效性，并显示优化过程中样本内/外性能持续改善。

Conclusion: TradeGrad通过引入基于经验的文本梯度估计与跨期稳健目标（CPRO），显著提高了量化交易策略从历史数据向未来泛化的能力，实现了更稳健且更高收益的策略优化。

Abstract: Quantitative trading strategy design aims to discover trading programs from historical data that remain effective in future markets, which can be viewed as a black-box program optimization problem. LLM-based textual gradients offer a promising approach by providing explicit optimization directions for iterative strategy refinement. However, directly applying textual gradients faces two challenges: (1) optimization is myopic, underutilizing experience from previous evaluations; and (2) aggregate backtest feedback overlooks temporal robustness, potentially favoring strategies that perform well only in specific market periods. To address these challenges, we propose TradeGrad, an experience-guided textual-gradient framework for robust trading strategy optimization. TradeGrad leverages accumulated optimization experience to estimate textual gradients and employs multi-scale revisions for both strategy exploration and refinement. It further introduces the Cross-Period Robust Objective (CPRO), which emphasizes performance in unfavorable historical periods to promote temporal robustness. Experiments on cross-sectional and time-series strategy design in Chinese A-share and U.S. equity markets show that TradeGrad achieves the best in-sample and out-of-sample performance across all four settings. Notably, its Chinese cross-sectional strategy achieves 27.99% annualized return, 12.19% maximum drawdown, and a Sharpe ratio of 1.63, approximately 68% higher than the CSI 300 benchmark. Further analyses validate the proposed components and show consistent improvements in both in-sample and out-of-sample performance throughout optimization. The code is available at https://github.com/transcend-0/TradeGrad.

</details>


### [90] [Keeping JEPA World Models Plannable When Little of the Frame Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03137)
*Florian Strohm,Patrick Wagner,Jannik Schwab,Marco Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在多目标推挤基准SLIM上发现世界模型编码器潜变量对动作不敏感导致规划失败；加入逆动力学辅助损失修复此问题，恢复规划并支持无重训的语言目标规划，且提出了可用的动作敏感性探针作为诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望评估在复杂场景中用自然语言而非显式目标框进行规划时，基于潜在世界模型的方法是否能区分同场景中多个对象并成功规划。

Method: 构建多目标推挤基准SLIM（带视觉与语言目标），分析LeWM在该任务上失败的原因（用解码探针与动作敏感性探测器定位到编码器潜变量几乎对动作不敏感），并引入一个共享的逆动力学辅助头（训练时作用于编码器潜变量与预测潜变量，测试时丢弃）进行修复，辅以一系列对照实验与响应扫频来归因修复来源；提出廉价的动作敏感性探针作为必要条件。

Result: 原始LeWM在SLIM上几乎无法成功（<1%），加入逆动力学损失后在整体上将成功率从0.003提到0.35（难度最高层0.16），PushT性能扩展到更长规划步；语言目标头在修复的潜变量上无需重训可以在导航上达0.84（视觉目标oracle为1.00），并能在分阶段语言描述下将中、困难推挤任务的成功率从0.04提高到0.25，与目标框oracle相当；动作敏感性探针低于阈值的配置都无法规划。

Conclusion: 通过在编码器与预测潜变量上追加逆动力学(aux inverse-dynamics)辅助损失并在测试时丢弃该头，作者修复了潜变量对动作不敏感的问题，显著恢复并提升了基于潜在世界模型的规划能力，同时无需重训世界模型即可实现从语言目标规划。

Abstract: Specifying a goal in language rather than as a goal frame is a natural interface for planning with a latent world model, but testing it needs scenes in which language must discriminate between several objects. We build SLIM, a pushing benchmark with several small objects and paired visual and language goals on identical scenes. On SLIM a LeWM world model that solves PushT succeeds on under 1% of trials, although a scripted controller with simulator state solves every tier. Probes locate the failure in the encoder: its latent is nearly action-insensitive, neither pusher nor object positions can be decoded from it, and rollouts are no better than copying the current latent forward. One inverse-dynamics auxiliary loss, applied to encoder latents and to predicted latents through a shared head discarded at test time, restores every probe and raises success from 0.003 to 0.35 (0.16 on the hard pushing tier, where a goal-agnostic policy scores zero), and improves PushT at twice the trained horizon. Controls attribute the repair to the gradient into the encoder, and a response sweep shows that the vanilla model plans once enough of the frame responds to actions. A cheap action-sensitivity probe, computable without environment access, acts as an empirical necessary condition: all configurations below its threshold failed to plan. On the repaired latent, a small language-goal head plans from sentences without retraining the world model: it reaches 0.84 on navigation (visual-goal oracle 1.00), follows the named zone when it is swapped with a decoy, and degrades gracefully to unseen nouns. A single goal sentence rarely completes a push, but given the push as a sequence of stage sentences the head raises success on the medium and hard pushing tiers from 0.04 to 0.25, on par with the goal-frame oracle, also when the switch between stages is read from the latent alone.

</details>


### [91] [Gains and Collapse in On-Policy Distillation:A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03185)
*Han Cui,Jianhao Yan,Yun Luo,Hongbo Zhang,Zhizhang Fu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把教师当作隐式奖励模型能解释OPD在提升与崩溃间的差异：可靠的评价可带来改进，偏差的评价会导致reward hacking；通过屏蔽不良示例和SFT初始化能缓解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD能提升语言模型的表现，但在实践中有时会导致生成过长、重复的输出，且其发生机制尚不清楚。作者旨在理解为何OPD在不同条件下会产生截然不同的结果，并寻找有效的缓解方法。

Method: 从强化学习视角解释OPD：把教师视为对学生行为隐式打分的奖励模型；通过实验分析该隐式奖励的可靠性与偏差如何影响学生生成；验证两种缓解策略（训练时掩盖不健康响应、使用SFT初始化）。

Result: 实验证明OPD提升的是更容易被采样到的正确回答，而不是扩展学生能力；当隐式奖励与质量对齐时有利，否则会放大学生的有害行为（例如长且重复的生成），而这些行为可能在教师自身生成中罕见。掩盖不健康样本和SFT初始化分别能有效降低崩溃风险，二者结合效果更佳。

Conclusion: OPD会放大教师隐式反馈所偏好的学生行为：当教师的隐式奖励模型可靠时，OPD能提高正确回答的采样概率；当偏好与质量不一致时，会发生“reward hacking”，导致过长、重复的文本生成。掩盖不健康响应或以SFT初始化训练可以减缓或防止这种崩溃。
