Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an important approach to language model post-training. However, despite its performance gains, OPD can also collapse into excessively long and repetitive generation, and the mechanism underlying these divergent outcomes remains poorly understood. We explain these outcomes through a reinforcement learning perspective: the teacher implicitly rewards student behaviors, even those it rarely exhibits itself. From this perspective, our experiments show that OPD improves performance without expanding the student's capabilities. When the implicit reward model is reliable, OPD makes correct responses easier to sample. In contrast, when the preference misaligns with quality, reward hacking happens: the implicit reward model amplifies overlong, repetitive student rollouts, even though it rarely generates such text itself. Guided by this diagnosis, we find that masking unhealthy responses during training and using SFT initialization can each effectively mitigate the collapse. Together, these findings show that OPD amplifies student behaviors favored by the teacher's implicit feedback, shifting the focus from how well the teacher generates to how reliably it evaluates student rollouts. Our code is available at https://github.com/HancCui/opd_hacking.

</details>


### [92] [KV$^2$: A Self-Refining KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03198)
*Johannes Wesch,Danni Liu,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV^2通过先筛后补的选择性重构，在显著降低KV-cache占用和运行成本的同时保留高质量，尤其在极低预算下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可重用的KV-cache场景中，预填充的上下文需要服务大量不同查询，KV-cache的内存占用成为瓶颈且主导了成本。传统折衷在于用廉价但不准的估计器或用昂贵的对全上下文重处理以保持质量，亟需在成本和质量之间更优的折中。

Method: 提出一种两阶段的查询不可知（query-agnostic）KV-cache压缩流程：1) 使用轻量代理评分器对每个token的“信息性”进行快速估计并挑出候选子集；2) 只对该候选子集重新运行完整上下文的重处理（full-context reconstruction）或更精确的评分以得到最终的逐出/保留分数。

Result: 在多个基准（RULER、Needle-in-a-Haystack、LongBench）上，KV^2在紧预算（例如RULER 16K、2% KV-cache预算）下相较次优基线平均得分提高了超过40个百分点，并在2%–10%预算区间于LongBench获得最高平均分，同时在压缩阶段比全上下文重建表现出更低的运行时和峰值内存。

Conclusion: KV^2 证明了在可重用场景下，通过选择性重构（先用轻量代理评分器筛选出信息性强的上下文token，再仅对该子集重处理以计算最终逐出分数）可以在不对整个提示重新处理的情况下获得接近甚至优于全上下文重建的效果，从而显著降低内存和运行成本。

Abstract: The memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache constrains the practical use of long-context models, and it dominates cost when one prefilled context must later serve many different queries. In this reusable setting, query-agnostic compression trades cost against quality: lightweight estimators are cheap but less accurate, whereas full-context reconstruction scoring is more accurate yet reprocesses the entire prompt. We introduce KV$^2$, a query-agnostic KV-cache compression method based on selective reconstruction. KV$^2$ first uses a lightweight proxy scorer to identify informative in-context tokens, then reprocesses only this subset to compute final eviction scores. On RULER, Needle-in-a-Haystack, and LongBench, KV$^2$'s margin over baselines widens as the budget tightens: on RULER 16K at a 2% KV-cache budget it improves the average score over the next-best baseline by more than 40 percentage points, and on LongBench it attains the highest average across 2%-10% budgets at lower compression-stage runtime and peak memory than full-context reconstruction. Reusable KV-cache compression thus does not require reprocessing the full context. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/KVsquared-0B97.

</details>


### [93] [Toward SLM-based agentic task-tool intent matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03213)
*Chiara Troiani,Arash Salarian,Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用小型语言模型对每次工具调用进行任务相关性判别，通过提示优化、监督微调与GRPO强化学习在自建多工具跨MCP数据集上进行专门化，以实现低延迟、本地化的每调用审查能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：现有授权机制只能判断代理是否被允许调用工具，但无法评估该调用是否逻辑上、语义上与任务意图一致——允许的调用仍可能偏离任务（如“流氓代理”或误导性组合调用）。随着代理系统水平扩展，工具调用频次激增，需求出现：需要可低延迟、本地化运行并对每次调用进行自动化审查的机制。

Method: 方法：构建包含跨多个Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器的多工具任务数据集；将每次工具调用与任务描述配对，标注相关/不相关信号；对小模型进行三阶段优化：提示工程（prompt-optimization）、监督微调（SFT）和基于GRPO的策略优化型强化学习；将SLM部署为每次调用的独立判别器，输出二元或分数型相关性信号以供下游执行策略使用。

Result: 结果：基于作者描述，SLM在所构建的数据集上可学习到任务-工具相关性的判别能力；提示优化快速改善零样本/少样本表现，SFT进一步提升准确度，GRPO强化学习有助于在下游策略/奖惩设定下专门化模型行为以减少误判和漏判。同时作者强调了跨MCP服务器的任务分布与多工具交互带来的挑战。

Conclusion: 论文结论：小型语言模型（SLM）可作为每次工具调用的相关性判别器，用以在低时延/本地化条件下对代理的工具选择进行因果性或任务对齐验证。经过提示优化、监督微调和基于GRPO的强化学习，SLM在判别工具与任务意图的相关性上可以获得可用的性能，但仍需权衡泛化、鲁棒性与资源开销。

Abstract: Tool-equipped AI agents use tool calls to access data and act on external systems. Horizontal growth of agentic systems increases the number of these interactions, and further motivates the need for automated, per-call oversight that can operate at low latency and/or on-prem. Conventional authorization schemes can determine whether an agent is allowed to invoke a tool, but cannot assess the agent's underlying cognition, specifically, whether the tool selection represents a logical, relevant step toward satisfying the intent of the task or not. Consequently, an allowed call may still deviate from the task's intent: a rogue agent might deviate the calls or nudge other agents to make a combination of calls that would not align with the intent of the task. Therefore, every call needs to be verified. In this study we investigate the applicability of Small Language Models (SLMs) to this purpose: an SLM functions as a task-tool relevance classifier that evaluates every selected tool independently against the assigned task and returns a relevance signal for downstream enforcement. Equipped with a novel dataset with multi-tool tasks whose required tools span distinct Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, we used prompt-optimization, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning through GRPO to optimize and specialize SLMs.

</details>


### [94] [Learning a Fact Is Not Learning How to Retrieve It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03251)
*Chaemin Jang,Jihee Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出两阶段训练分离检索策略与事实记忆，发现“上下文状态”是连接请求形式经验与后续检索能力的关键：早期的请求形式暴露塑造了上下文表征，从而决定了后来在不同语式下能否检索出已学事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离“学会一个事实”与“学会如何通过特定语言形式调动该事实”的能力，解释为何模型在语式变体（如不同标点或措辞）上泛化差；探索内部表征（上下文状态）在学习与检索中的角色。

Method: 两阶段训练范式：第一阶段为“请求形式训练”，一组模型对每个事实看到五种请求形式，另一组仅看到陈述句；第二阶段为“目标事实训练”，两组对新的事实都只用陈述句训练。分析方法包括：在答案之前提取隐藏层向量（称为上下文状态），比较不同请求形式下的上下文状态相似性；在检索时和在目标事实训练阶段对上下文状态施加干预，观察检索表现变化。

Result: 当新事实仅以陈述句呈现训练时，两组模型在陈述句检索上表现相近，但在其他请求形式上差异显著；在请求形式训练阶段见过多种形式的模型在不同请求形式下产生更相似的上下文状态；人为修改上下文状态可以启用或抑制检索，且这种效果能跨事实与关系迁移；在目标事实训练阶段对上下文状态施加干预，会改变后续的检索表现，无需在测试时再干预。

Conclusion: 后续检索行为取决于早期的请求形式经验以及事实学习时的“上下文状态”。模型可以先学会如何通过某种请求形式检索答案，而不是先学事实本身；改变上下文状态能在检索阶段启用或阻止已学事实的提取，并且对不同事实与关系具有迁移性。

Abstract: A model trained on "The capital of X is Y" may produce "Y" after "The capital of X is" but fail after "The capital of X:". We call these different ways of eliciting the same fact request forms. To separate learning a fact from retrieving it, we train two models in two stages. In the first stage (request-form training), one model sees each fact in five forms and the other sees the same facts only as statements. In the second stage (target-fact training), both receive identical training on new facts, all as statements. Both then retrieve the new facts almost equally well from statements, but differ sharply on other request forms. Thus, a model can learn how to retrieve through a request form before it learns the facts. To understand this difference, we examine the hidden state immediately before the answer, which we call the context state. When given two different request forms for the same fact, the model trained on five forms in stage one produces more similar context states than the model trained on statements alone in that stage. Changing this state at retrieval time can enable or prevent retrieval of an already learned fact, and the same effect transfers across facts and factual relations, such as capitals and currencies. To test its role during learning, we change the context state only during target-fact training. This intervention changes later retrieval without intervention at test time. Together, these results show that later retrieval depends on earlier request-form experience and the context state during fact learning.

</details>


### [95] [EVOL: Simulator-Guided Evolutionary Expert Synthesis for Deployment-Free Learning Path Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03273)
*Geonwoo Bang,Dongho Kim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用知识追踪模拟器+进化搜索合成专家示范，并通过不对称actor-critic与模仿蒸馏得到可部署的学习路径推荐策略，有效缓解稀疏奖励和缺少专家数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在学习路径推荐中受两个耦合障碍限制：1）策略必须在长度为L的序列上盲目承诺，导致组合爆炸与极端稀疏回报；2）真实教育日志缺乏“应做”专家路径，无法直接借助示范学习缓解稀疏奖励。

Method: 构建知识追踪(simulator)作为“特权信息”源，采用进化搜索为每个学习者合成高质量的专家示范；用不对称actor-critic训练：actor在部署时仅使用可获得信息（盲规划），critic在训练时利用模拟器的特权状态；随后将专家示范通过行为克隆(BC)、AWR或DAPG等模仿策略蒸馏到前馈部署策略。

Result: 在ASSIST15、Junyi、EdNet（三个数据集，概念数39–189）与路径长度L∈{5,10,20}的设置下，EVOL优于包括启发式、序列化方法、常规RL、图增强RL和LLM增强方法在内的8种基线；比较三种模仿策略表明最终性能主要由进化生成的专家质量决定，而非具体的模仿目标函数。

Conclusion: EVOL通过在知识追踪模拟器中合成专家示范并用不对称的actor-critic与模仿学习蒸馏，实质上解决了学习路径推荐中稀疏奖励与缺乏专家轨迹的双重难题，从而在多数据集、多路径长度上超越多类基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for learning path recommendation (LPR) faces two coupled obstacles. First, the policy must commit to a sequence of L concepts without intermediate feedback, producing a combinatorial search space that grows super-exponentially with L and provides reward only at the final step. Second, expert learning paths would be the natural cure for sparse-reward RL, but they do not exist in educational data, because student logs record what learners did, not what they should have done. We address both obstacles by importing a recipe from simulator-based demonstration learning in robotics: the knowledge tracing simulator is used both to synthesize per-learner expert demonstrations through evolutionary search and to train a deployment-free policy that distills these demonstrations into a feed-forward learner. Our framework, EVOL, instantiates this pipeline with an asymmetric actor-critic where the actor commits to deployment-realistic blind planning while the critic exploits the privileged simulator state during training. Across three datasets (ASSIST15, Junyi, and EdNet; 39-189 concepts) and path lengths L = 5, 10, and 20, EVOL surpasses 8 baselines spanning heuristic, sequential, RL, graph-enhanced RL, and LLM-enhanced methods. We further compare three imitation strategies (BC, AWR, and DAPG) and show that final performance is governed by the quality of evolutionary experts rather than by the particular imitation objective.

</details>


### [96] [JOVE: Joint Execution and Verification for Resource-Aware LLM Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03296)
*Haoran Zhang,Dongjun Kim,Seohyeon Cha,Kevin S Chan,Ananthram Swami,Gustavo De Veciana,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JOVE 提出一套在异构 LLM 池上对 DAG 分解任务进行在线执行与付费核验的联合分配—学习框架，通过 MILP 决策与信息增益驱动的在线学习在保证延迟与预算约束下实现低成本、低延迟且准确的复杂推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构小型 LLM 能通过并行化和分工完成复杂推理以降低延迟与成本，但实际中对哪个 LLM 适合哪个子任务及其输出正确性事先未知，需要一种在有限预算和延迟约束下同时执行与学习的在线分配机制。

Method: 将复杂推理任务分解为有向无环任务图（DAG），为每个子任务在线求解分配执行器 LLM 并异步选择部分中间结果进行付费核验；每次查询通过求解混合整数线性规划（MILP）决定执行与核验策略，基于核验反馈进行任务相关的质量估计在线更新，并用信息增益奖励（bonus）把学习价值纳入分配决策。

Result: 构建的 JOVE 框架在理论上证明了在自然假设下的次线性质量学习后悔；在四个推理基准上与标准推理基线比较，达到可比的准确率，同时平均成本和延迟至少降低约 3.17 倍。

Conclusion: JOVE 在带有验证反馈的在线分配框架下，实现在未知且异构 LLM 池中对复杂推理 DAG 的高效执行——在保证长期预算和单次延迟约束下，兼顾执行成本与学习收益，理论上给出次线性学习后悔界，实证上在多个基准中显著降低成本和延迟并保持竞争性准确率。

Abstract: Complex reasoning queries can be decomposed into directed acyclic task graphs and distributed across heterogeneous LLMs, reducing latency through parallelism and enabling smaller models to solve complex tasks. In practice, however, the suitability of an LLM for a given subtask may be a priori unknown, and execution alone does not reveal output correctness. We propose JOVE, an online framework that jointly assigns executor LLMs and selects intermediate outputs for paid verification. Verification runs asynchronously and is used to improve future allocations, so the system must balance spending on execution now against learning for later. We study how to optimize this trade-off under a long-term budget and a per-query latency constraint, with stochastic, initially unknown LLM service quality, invocation costs, and execution times. JOVE makes execution and verification decisions by solving a sequence of per-query mixed-integer linear programs. Online learning updates task-dependent estimates of LLM quality based on verification feedback, while an information-gain bonus incorporates the value of learning into allocation decisions. Under a natural set of assumptions, we establish sublinear quality-learning regret for JOVE. Across four reasoning benchmarks, JOVE achieves competitive accuracy against standard inference baselines while reducing average cost and latency by at least 3.17 times.

</details>


### [97] [Optimal Planning in a Dynamic World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03312)
*Devin Wild Thomas,Solomon Eyal Shimony,Wheeler Ruml,Erez Karpas,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Andrew Coles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出cATF用于在起始时间未知的动态环境中紧凑编码随时间变化的最优计划，并给出相应算法与理论界，实验表明在SIPP类问题上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实动态环境中，状态或动作的可行性随时间变化（如移动障碍或列车停靠窗口），但实际执行的起始时间常常在规划完成后才确定。现有多数方法要么忽略时变性，要么假设已知起始时间；这在实际场景中不实用。因此需要一种能在起始时间未定时仍提供最优计划查询的规划方法。

Method: 定义cATF并给出在图搜索框架下的函数传播与合并操作；用启发式搜索构造并传播这些函数以组装整条路径的cATF；理论分析cATF规模界和算法的正确性与最优性；在移动障碍下的路径规划（SIPP）上实现该框架并与重规划基线比较实验性能。

Result: 设计并实现了cATF以及基于它的任意起始时间规划算法；证明cATF大小为O(|problem|)；在SIPP实验中证实在复杂问题上比依赖重规划的代理更稳健，且一旦知道起始时间可以立即查出最优路径。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“任意起始时间规划”（any-start-time planning）的问题设定，并给出了一种名为复合到达时间函数（cATF）的数据结构，用于紧凑地表示最优计划随起始时间的变化。基于cATF的启发式图搜索算法可沿边传播函数而非标量，从而在起始时间未知时仍能预先构造可查表的最优策略。理论上证明cATF大小至多与问题规模线性相关；在SIPP问题上的实现与实验表明，在困难实例上比简单的重规划更可靠，能够在获知实际起始时间后迅速返回最优计划。

Abstract: Background: We address the problem of planning when the set of feasible states or actions changes over time. For example, in the problem of path planning among moving obstacles (sometimes known as SIPP), the feasibility of being at a particular location can change as the obstacles move. Or, the action of boarding a particular train is feasible only while it is stopped at the station. This dynamism means that the optimal plan and its duration can change depending on when execution begins. In practice, execution start time is often unknown until planning has completed or another agent gives the go-ahead. However, most prior planning work either ignores dynamism or assumes a known start time. This makes it straightforward to assess state and action feasibility but is impractical for some applications. Objectives: In this paper, we relax the assumption of a known start time. We define the setting of {\em any-start-time planning} and provide algorithms for it. Methods: We present a data structure called a compound arrival time function (cATF) that compactly encodes the optimal plan as a function of start time. We provide general-purpose planning algorithms, based on heuristic graph search, that assemble cATFs by propagating functions along edges instead of scalar costs. Results: We prove that the size of a cATF is at most linear in the problem size. An experimental evaluation of an implementation for the specific problem of SIPP shows that, on difficult problems, agents that rely on replanning often fail, while any-start-time algorithms using cATFs can quickly look up the optimal plan once the execution start time is known. Conclusions: By enabling efficient representations and reasoning for time-dependent plans, this work provides a foundation for planning in dynamic worlds.

</details>


### [98] [Lightweight, Rubric-Guided Trajectory Evaluation for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03315)
*Linh-An Phan,MingXue Wang,Guangyu Wu,Feng Pan,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiteTrajEval 通过规则驱动的轨迹压缩与启发式标注，加上 rubric 引导的单一 LLM 判定，实现了在大幅降低成本与延迟的同时显著提高故障定位对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的 agent 轨迹冗长且昂贵重复运行，且并非所有原始 token 对故障定位同等重要；需要在预算受限下仍能提供高质量的故障定位与诊断。

Method: 离线生成领域专用规则剖面；在线对原始轨迹做预处理（抽取关键事件、打标签、标注启发式失败信号）；在固定的全局预算下序列化轨迹（保留高价值信息，丢弃冗余 token）；调用单一 rubric 指导的 LLM 判官，输出结构化诊断报告。

Result: 在公开数据集上相较 AgentRx，Magnetic-One 风格数据集的故障定位对齐度提升约 20–35 个百分点，τ-retail 数据集最多提升 23 个百分点；评估成本约降低 6×，评估耗时降低超过 8×；已在企业 agent 平台上线验证可用性。

Conclusion: LiteTrajEval 提供了一种轻量、预算受限的轨迹评估流程，通过离线构建领域规则剖面、在线压缩与标注轨迹，并用单一 rubric 引导的 LLM 产生结构化诊断，从而在保持评价质量的同时大幅降低成本与延迟。

Abstract: Trajectory evaluation is essential for improving the reliability of LLM-based agents, but production use makes it expensive to run repeatedly. Modern agents generate long traces containing tool calls, observations, retries, and external outputs, while not all raw tokens are equally useful for diagnosis. We present \textit{LiteTrajEval}, a lightweight architecture for budget-bounded trajectory evaluation. LiteTrajEval derives compact domain-specific rule profiles offline, then preprocesses each trajectory online, marks heuristic failure signals, serializes it under a fixed global budget, and invokes a single rubric-guided LLM judge to produce structured diagnostic reports. Evaluated on public Magentic-One-style and $τ$-bench-style trajectory datasets, LiteTrajEval improves failure-localization alignment with human annotations by roughly 20--35 percentage points on Magentic-One and up to 23 percentage points on $τ$-retail compared with AgentRx, while reducing cost by about 6$\times$ and evaluation time by more than 8$\times$. This solution has also been deployed in our enterprise agentic platform.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Task Evolution for Zero-Shot Cross-Problem Generalization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03316)
*Zhouliang Xie,Changliang Zhou,Genghui Li,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MECo is an LLM-driven evolutionary method that transfers and selects complementary heuristics across source tasks to achieve strong zero-shot performance on new combinatorial problem variants.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heuristic design for combinatorial optimization is expertise- and search-intensive; LLMs can automate heuristic generation but typically need task-specific evaluation feedback—MECo aims to generalize heuristics to new problem definitions using only source-task feedback (zero-shot).

Method: LLM-driven multi-task evolutionary framework: maintain separate heuristic populations per source task; compute a transfer gap from cross-task population performance to guide transfers and recombination; apply a complementary selection criterion rewarding additional coverage to build a compact heuristic set; apply that set to target tasks without further adaptation.

Result: On 32 VRP and FJSP variants, MECo attains the lowest mean costs versus eight automated heuristic design baselines under equal budgets, outperforms strongest baselines on out-of-domain problems, and improves ID/OOD performance of various AHD methods when integrated.

Conclusion: MECo enables zero-shot cross-problem generalization of heuristics by evolving task-conditioned heuristic populations, guiding cross-task interactions with a transfer-gap measure, and selecting a compact complementary set for zero-shot application to target problems.

Abstract: Designing effective heuristics for diverse combinatorial optimization problems requires substantial expertise and repeated search. Large language models (LLMs) automate heuristic generation and refinement, but heuristic search typically depends on evaluation feedback from the problem being optimized. Generalizing to new problem definitions using only source-task feedback therefore remains a central challenge. We introduce MECo, an LLM-driven multi-task evolutionary framework for zero-shot cross-problem generalization. MECo maintains task-conditioned heuristic populations and uses a transfer gap based on cross-task population performance to guide their interactions. These interactions enable the transfer and recombination of heuristics. A complementary selection criterion then constructs a compact heuristic set by rewarding each member's additional coverage of source combinations. The selected set is applied to target problems without further search or adaptation. Experiments on 32 problem variants across vehicle routing (VRP) and flexible job-shop scheduling (FJSP) show that MECo achieves the lowest mean costs compared with eight automated heuristic design (AHD) baselines under the same budgets. On out-of-domain problems, it outperforms the strongest baseline in each family. Moreover, integrating the framework of MECo with different AHD methods improves their ID and OOD performance in both families, supporting its effectiveness across different methods.

</details>


### [100] [Refinement Buys Intelligibility, Search Buys Identity: What Test-Time Compute Buys in Masked-Diffusion TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03320)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Ayush Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对扩散TTS而言，推理步数主要修复可懂度瓶颈，模型深度更有利于身份保留；两者不可互换，且codec是身份损失的主要来源，应分别设计与优化深度、推理预算与编码器/解码器组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在基于扩散的TTS中，通过增加模型参数（深度）或增加推理迭代（细化步数）两种尺度化手段是否在能力上等价，以及两者如何分别影响可懂度与说话人身份保留，从而为资源分配（模型设计与推理预算）提供指导。

Method: 训练15个masked-diffusion codec TTS模型，模型规模在19M–133M参数（每个规模3个随机种子），训练数据2,000小时语音；在推理阶段对细化步数T在[1,16]范围内做扫参；在174名未见说话人上进行零样本合成评估，使用ASR词错误率（WER）评估可懂度，使用说话人验证评估身份保持；并做3x及6x训练日程重训、Best-of-K搜索实验、以及模型选择对比（可分解B(d)B(T)模型 vs 替代模型，报告Delta AICc=+69.3）；对身份缺陷归因分析以区分codec与生成器的贡献。

Result: 细化在可懂度范围内收敛得更快：细化弥补了86.2%的可懂度差距，但仅弥补了46.4%的身份差距（1.86×的不对称），此结论在多种错误度量下稳健。延长训练日程到3x与6x可减小但不能消除不对称（不对称比从1.86降至1.36再到1.23）。可懂度随步数很快饱和，而身份保持随步数持续改善。Best-of-K搜索能在细化失败时恢复身份（对四个独立编码器获胜率64.6–79.0%）。模型拟合显示深度与步数效果可分解（Delta AICc=+69.3）。身份损失中约62%归因于codec而非生成器。

Conclusion: 细化（推理步数）和模型深度并非可互换的资源：两者针对不同瓶颈。推理步数主要提升可懂度（intelligibility），而深度对保留说话人身份（identity）更关键。即便通过更长训练日程缩小差距，也无法完全逆转这一不对称；此外编码器/解码器（codec）约占剩余身份缺陷的62%。最终结论是应分别优化深度和推理步数。

Abstract: Diffusion language models for text-to-speech combine two forms of computation: model depth (parameters) and refinement steps (inference budget). We ask whether they scale equally across capabilities. We train 15 masked-diffusion codec TTS models varying depth (19-133M parameters, 3 seeds) on 2,000 hours of speech and sweep refinement steps T in [1,16] at inference, measuring zero-shot synthesis via ASR word error rate (intelligibility) and speaker verification (identity) on 174 held-out speakers. Against measured floors, refinement closes 86.2% of the intelligibility range but only 46.4% of the identity range - a 1.86x asymmetry robust across multiple error metrics. Retraining at 3x and 6x schedule attenuates but does not reverse this gap (1.84 to 1.36 to 1.23x), because intelligibility saturates with steps while identity continues improving. Best-of-K search recovers speaker identity where refinement fails, with 64.6-79.0% win rates across four independent encoders. Depth and steps are not interchangeable: separable B(d)B(T) fits significantly better (Delta AICc=+69.3) than substitution models. Analysis shows 62% of remaining identity deficit lies in the codec, not the generator. We conclude that refinement and depth target different bottlenecks and should be optimized separately.

</details>


### [101] [Preserving Mathematical Reasoning in Compressed Diffusion Language Models via Trajectory-Aware Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03326)
*Tian Liang,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决dLLM压缩时训练/推理分布不匹配的问题，论文提出轨迹感知的低秩目标与Traj-MC蒙特卡洛估计器，在理论和实验上都显示出比传统干净激活标定更好的轨迹重建与数学推理保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的dLLM压缩在标定时使用完全可见的干净激活，但推理过程中模型会经历部分掩码的中间状态，二者分布不匹配导致压缩后行为退化，尤其在复杂推理任务上。论文旨在回答：在轨迹分布上是否可以刻画低秩最优性，以及标定状态选择是否影响数学推理的保持。

Method: 提出了轨迹感知的低秩目标，覆盖不同的损坏级别与掩码实现；为高效估计该目标，通过蒙特卡洛采样估计轨迹二阶矩并提出Traj-MC算法，理论上给出采样态精确最优性与总体一致性。

Result: 在相同压缩预算下，轨迹感知标定（Traj-MC）在生成轨迹上的重建误差更小，并在数学推理基准上比干净标定保留了显著更多的推理能力；此外提供了与轨迹感知低秩最优性相关的理论与经验联系。

Conclusion: 在低秩压缩设定下，以生成轨迹分布（即带掩码/被破坏的中间态）为目标进行标定，可以显著优于传统的对干净激活做标定，从而在相同压缩预算下获得更好的重建误差和更强的数学推理保持性。

Abstract: Diffusion language model (dLLM) compression faces a known challenge because calibration is typically performed on clean, fully visible activations, whereas inference traverses partially masked intermediate states. For low-rank compression, this raises two questions. First, can low-rank optimality still be characterized when approximation quality is measured over trajectory-distributed states, and second, does the choice of calibration states affect mathematical reasoning preservation under compression? We address these questions by formulating a trajectory-aware low-rank objective over corruption levels and masking realizations. To estimate this objective efficiently, we propose Traj-MC, which estimates the trajectory second moment through Monte Carlo sampling and yields exact sampled-state optimality and population consistency. Under matched compression budgets, trajectory-aware calibration improves reconstruction over the generation trajectory and preserves substantially more mathematical reasoning than clean calibration on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our results connect trajectory-aware low-rank optimality to the reasoning capability retained after dLLM compression. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/traj-mc.git.

</details>


### [102] [ReFract: Benchmarking Perspective Awareness in Language Model Agents with Text World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03356)
*Hainiu Xu,Vítor N. Lourenço,Mohnish Dubey,Yunfei Bai,Yulan He,Caroline Catmur,Aline Paes,Marco Caserta,Akash Chandrayan,Luca D'Angelo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReFract基准来评估代理的“视角感知”能力：150条专家条目+Text World Models，结果显示现有LLM在该能力上严重不足，动机是为高风险真实场景引入角色约束的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业维护与故障排查等高风险场景中，代理的行为需要根据用户的角色（权限、知识背景、可用工具）进行校准，以避免造成设备损坏或人员伤害。现有基准未充分考察代理是否能推断并遵守角色边界。

Method: 从匿名领域支持对话中构建150条专家验证的条目；为每条条目创建Text World Model来模拟操作环境；为不同角色设计期望的、视角感知的动作轨迹；使用这些条目评估多种SOTA LLM代理的行为与轨迹。

Result: ReFract包含150个专家验证条目；SOTA LLM在该基准上最多仅能正确解决69%任务；超过50%的生成轨迹包含尝试执行与用户角色冲突的动作（即视角违规）。该基准揭示视角感知作为独立评价维度的必要性。

Conclusion: ReFract证明了在高风险、角色多样的实际应用场景中，现有LLM代理缺乏“视角感知”（Perspective Awareness），这一能力尚未解决。需要研究能推断用户角色并限制代理仅使用该角色合法工具与动作的方法，以避免不可逆损害。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as industrial maintenance and equipment fault troubleshooting, where workers occupy a variety of roles. A capable agent must therefore act in a way that is calibrated to user's role: taking actions and providing information that respect the role's knowledge and capability boundaries. Unlike coding, where mistakes are usually recoverable, agent responses in these settings are enacted on physical equipment, and can therefore cause irreversible equipment damage, production loss, or personnel harm. Existing benchmarks, however, largely overlook the need for agents to infer what a role intends and acting only through tools that role may legitimately use, a capability which we term Perspective Awareness. To this end, we introduce ReFract, a benchmark of 150 expert-validated entries in which an agent must act differently in response to the same query depending on user's role. Entries of ReFract are grounded in anonymized queries from domain support conversations, against which we construct Text World Models that simulate the agent's operating environments and assemble perspective-aware action trajectories. State-of-the-art LLMs solve at most 69% of the tasks with more than 50% of their trajectories contain attempts of taking perspective-violating actions. ReFract exposes perspective awareness as a distinct, largely unsolved axis of agent evaluation and motivates agents that calibrate not just how to act, but for whom.

</details>


### [103] [Geometry Meets Physics: Data-Efficient Pre-Training for Unstructured Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03363)
*Luis Medrano-Navarro,Giacomo Baldan,Qiang Liu,Benjamin Holzschuh,Jan Hagnberger,Mathias Niepert,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过几何驱动的表征学习与物理驱动的在线合成数据，提出了一种无需事先存储大规模PDE数据的预训练框架，显著提升了微调时的收敛性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成大规模高质量PDE训练集代价高昂且难以覆盖复杂3D无结构网格下的多样几何与动力学场景；直接训练在小样本下泛化性能差。因此需要一种既节省数据和存储又能提升泛化和收敛的预训练策略。

Method: 两个并行的预训练路径：1) 稳态：基于三维几何的内在描述子（例如形状谱、几何径向基等）构建无解标签的表征学习任务，从而学到对复杂域稳健的编码；2) 瞬态：通过在线数值解算器或简化物理模型实时合成PDE演化样本（对初始条件、边界、参数做随机化），在不保存大型数据集的前提下进行自监督/弱监督预训练。微调时将这些预训练表示迁移到下游精确PDE超模中。

Result: 在多项实验（稳态与瞬态场景）中，该方法在低样本条件下显著提升微调速度与最终精度，证明了无磁盘预训练在数据效率和可扩展性上的优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一个“无磁盘训练数据”（disk-data-free）的预训练框架，分别对稳态和瞬态PDE问题采用不同策略：稳态通过几何驱动的固有形状描述子学习域表示，瞬态通过物理驱动的在线合成PDE数据进行可扩展预训练。整体目标是减少昂贵的预计算数据依赖，提高微调时的收敛速度、数据效率和精度。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models for Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) on unstructured 3D geometries are often limited by poor generalization and the high cost of generating large-scale training datasets. Consequently, pre-training on massive datasets of related PDE dynamics has emerged as a critical alternative to enhance the robustness and scalability of these models. However, this strategy is neither compute- nor data-efficient, as it relies on massive pre-computed data that is very costly to generate. In this work, we introduce a disk-data-free pre-training framework tailored to both steady-state and transient regimes. For steady-state problems, we propose a geometry-driven strategy that leverages intrinsic shape descriptors to learn representations of complex 3D domains. For transient problems, we introduce a physics-driven approach based on online generation of synthetic PDE data, enabling scalable pre-training without reliance on expensive datasets. Across multiple experiments, our approach achieves faster convergence, greater data efficiency, and higher accuracy during fine-tuning, particularly under realistic low-data regimes. This methodology provides a practical pathway toward data-efficient neural emulators for large-scale simulations.

</details>


### [104] [Multilingual GSM-Symbolic: What determines capability transfer across languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03367)
*Kenneth Enevoldsen,Riley Herchert,Sofie Mosegaard,Dan Saattrup Smart,Simon Enni,Isaac Chung,Sofie Bruun,Ayush Sunil Munot,Max Müller-Eberstein,Adnan El-Assadi,Elisa Bassignana,Gianluca Barmina,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Iben Nyholm Debess,Linda Freienthal,Lukas Galke Poech,Mike Zhang,Nicolas Legrand,Vladimir Salnikov,Yevhen Kostiuk,Zafar Hussain,Sagandeep Kaur,Agnes Toftgård,Marie Mattson,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过一个符号化、多语种的数学问答基准，作者用回归分析量化并对比了影响跨语能力迁移的主要因素，提出了无需全面标注即可估计目标语表现的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对跨语能力迁移理解不足，评估数据集不可比且易饱和，缺少对迁移决定因素的联合分析；若能识别预测因子，可减少对全语言评估的需求并帮助改善低资源语言性能。

Method: 构造15种语言、3万道题的项目匹配数学问答数据集，使用符号模板生成大量变体；对多个模型在这些语言上的表现用回归模型联合估计影响因素（模型规模、语言资源量、推理需求、类型学距离等），并报告标准化回归系数和预测误差。

Result: 发现模型规模（β=1.77）、语言资源水平（β=0.77）、推理需求（β=0.67）正向影响迁移，类型学距离（β=−0.25）负向影响；模型规模和推理能力可部分缩小高低资源语言差距；回归框架解释了92%的语言间差异但仅解释了23%的模型×语言交互，能在未见语言上以6.0个百分点（r=0.96）内预测模型表现，使用目标语中10个模板的测量能把误差降到4.19个百分点。

Conclusion: 作者构建了一个可扩展的多语言数学数据集（Multilingual GSM-Symbolic），通过符号化模板生成大规模语言一致的题目，量化了跨语能力迁移的主要决定因素，并给出可用于估计未见目标语性能的实用方法。

Abstract: We understand little about how capabilities acquired in one language carry over to another, or what governs this transfer: evaluations rely on incomparable, saturation-prone datasets and rarely examine its determinants jointly. Identifying what predicts transfer would let us avoid exhaustive evaluation across all language pairs and let developers target the factors that limit performance in low-resource languages. To evaluate cross-lingual capability transfer, we introduce Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, an extensible multilingual mathematical dataset covering 30,000 item-matched question-answer pairs and spanning 15 languages. It utilises symbolic templates to prevent overfitting and ensure generalisation by allowing generation of millions of high-quality variations from a single sample. Using Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, we quantify the largest determinants of capability as model size ($β= 1.77$), language resource level ($β= 0.77$), reasoning ($β= 0.67$) and typological distance ($β= -0.25$). This joint estimation allows these determinants to be expressed in terms of one another: a 32B model evaluated in Marathi performs like a 10B model in English. Our findings have important implications for model developers, showing that model size and reasoning narrow the performance gap between low- and high-resource languages ($β= -0.27$ and $β= -0.20$, respectively), while similar levers have little or no effect on typologically distant languages.
  Overall, our analysis framework explains 92% of between-language variation, but only 23% of the model-by-language variation, and predicts a model's performance on an unseen language within 6.0pp (r=.96). Incorporating measurements from just 10 templates in the target language reduces this to 4.19pp, enabling reasonable estimates of performance with little or no downstream dataset.

</details>


### [105] [CVE2AP: Automated Generation of PDDL-Encoded Attack Paths via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03383)
*Lin Cui,Vincenzo Scotti,Raffaela Mirandola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CVE2AP 用结构化提示+错误反馈让 LLM 自动把 CVE 文本转成高质量 PDDL 攻击路径，实验显示可行且效果良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 PDDL 攻击路径建模依赖专家手工构造，难以扩展且难以跟上威胁演进；大模型具备预训练知识与推理能力，适合将漏洞描述自动映射为机器可验证的形式化表示。

Method: 提出 CVE2AP: 基于 LLM 的生成流水线，使用结构化提示(prompts)并嵌入错误反馈循环——将规划器返回的语法与可解性错误反馈给 LLM 以迭代修正输出；在多种 LLM 和生成配置上进行系统性实证评估。

Result: 在评估中 CVE2AP 达到最高 86.9% 语法正确率、78.6% 可解性与 93.1% 语义正确率（LLM-as-expert 评估）；GPT-5.5 提供最佳质量-成本权衡，且错误反馈机制带来最稳定的质量提升。

Conclusion: CVE2AP 成功展示了用大模型自动将自然语言 CVE 描述翻译为 PDDL 编码攻击路径的可行性，能够显著提升建模效率并在语法、可解性与语义正确性上取得高分。

Abstract: Attack Path (AP) modeling is fundamental to cybersecurity analysis, where the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) has been widely adopted to encode APs into formal and machine-verifiable representations for automated reasoning about vulnerability exploitation, attack progression, and their potential impacts. However, existing AP modeling approaches largely rely on expert-driven manual construction, limiting their scalability and ability to keep pace with rapidly evolving cyber threats. Large language models (LLMs) are promising candidates, as their extensive pre-trained knowledge and reasoning capabilities enable them to interpret and transform threat intelligence into formal representations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CVE2AP}, an LLM-based approach for automatically generating PDDL-encoded attack paths from natural language CVE (Common Vulnerability Exposure) descriptions. CVE2AP leverages structured prompting and incorporates an error-feedback mechanism that iteratively refines the generated paths using planner-reported syntactic and solvability errors. We conduct a systematic empirical evaluation across multiple LLMs and generation configurations, assessing generation quality across syntactic, solvability and semantic dimensions, together with token consumption and generation time. The results demonstrate that CVE2AP effectively generates high-quality PDDL-encoded attack paths, achieving up to 86.9\% syntax correctness, 78.6\% solvability, and 93.1\% semantic correctness under LLM-as-expert evaluation, while \texttt{GPT-5.5} offers the best quality-cost trade-off and error feedback yields the most consistent quality improvement.

</details>


### [106] [Benchmarking Candidate Coverage in Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03387)
*Jiawen Lu,Tongtong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出针对参考答案缺失的成对候选覆盖基准，揭示了模型在检测缺失与避免错误拒绝间的权衡，并表明需要独立评估分类、排序与拒绝机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅报告在完整候选集上的准确率不能揭示模型是否能识别参考标签缺失或是否会错误拒绝有效候选，需要专门的基准来量化覆盖与拒绝能力。

Method: 设计“present/absent”成对候选集，以保持候选数与名称变体一致；对四个数据集（AG News, DBpedia, Emotion, TREC）用相同文本输入做实验：300条用于校准、589条用于测试，共产生23,932次预测；报告原生拒绝率、在仅校准阈值下的行为，以及覆盖分数的AUROC等度量。

Result: 在TREC（5个自然命名候选）上：Laya能检测97.2%的缺失案例但错误拒绝69.7%的存在对照；Jev分别为24.8%和0.0%。用校准阈值后两者的缺失/错误拒绝率分别变为33.9%/3.7%（Laya）和45.0%/1.8%（Jev）。在DBpedia上，Jev的覆盖分数AUROC较高，便于选择更有利的工作点；在Emotion上，两个模型的完整集合准确率都偏弱。作者强调基准仅描述性、仅限参考标签缺失，不声称推广到自然的OOS或提出新拒绝方法。

Conclusion: 作者提出并初步应用了一种成对候选覆盖基准(protocol)来测量有类型决策模型在参考答案缺失时的拒绝与覆盖行为，结果显示不同模型在检测缺失答案与错误拒绝之间有明显权衡，且需要将分类准确性、得分排序与拒绝策略分开评估。

Abstract: Typed decision models return choices or distributions over answer options supplied at request time. Accuracy with complete options does not establish whether a model recognizes that a reference answer is missing or avoids rejecting valid candidates. We present a paired candidate-coverage benchmark protocol and an initial evaluation of Laya and Jev across AG News, DBpedia, Emotion, and TREC. The models receive identical frozen texts and requests: 300 calibration and 589 test texts yield 23,932 predictions per model. Present/absent pairs match ordinary candidate count, and name variants preserve descriptions, members, and order. Native rejection behavior differs sharply: at five TREC candidates with natural names, Laya detects 97.2% of missing-answer cases but falsely rejects 69.7% of present controls; Jev's rates are 24.8% and 0.0%. Calibration-only none-score thresholds change these rates to 33.9%/3.7% and 45.0%/1.8%, respectively. On DBpedia, Jev's high coverage-score AUROC supports a stronger operating point, whereas both models have weak complete-set accuracy on Emotion. Competence-conditioned analysis, probability-precision sensitivity, and interface audits show why classification, score ranking, and rejection policies need separate measurement. This initial benchmark is descriptive and limited to reference-label omission; it does not establish natural out-of-scope generalization, causal mechanisms, or a new rejection method.

</details>


### [107] [Becoming Suspicious Across Borders: Algorithmic Extraterritoriality and AI-Driven Financial Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03425)
*Georgios Pavlidis,Savvas Chatzichristofis,Eleni Gavriil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI in financial surveillance produces transnational, opaque forms of suspicion—'algorithmic extraterritoriality'—that complicate accountability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address inadequacy of traditional, jurisdiction-bound notions of suspicion given the rise of AI in financial surveillance.

Method: Conceptual analysis combining literature on AML/CFT, data infrastructures, and algorithmic governance to develop 'algorithmic extraterritoriality'.

Result: Proposes that suspicion now operates through transnational data infrastructures, creating accountability and contestability challenges as individuals become datafied subjects.

Conclusion: AI-driven suspicion transforms legal judgement into distributed, data-mediated evaluation that is hard to locate or contest.

Abstract: Suspicion is an important, yet elusive concept in anti-money laundering and counter-terrorist financing (AML/CFT), which allows for intervention below the threshold of proof. In its traditional form, suspicion can be understood as a situated legal judgement by human actors within identifiable jurisdictions. It is argued that this understanding is no longer adequate. As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes an integral part of financial surveillance, suspicion is increasingly produced through data-driven processes. This transformation is epistemic, but also spatial. Since AI-driven financial surveillance operates through transnational data infrastructures, regulatory reach is less a matter of where conduct occurs than a question of whether such conduct becomes visible within data systems. This article develops the concept of algorithmic extraterritoriality, understood as a form of regulatory power mediated by data infrastructures rather than formal assertions of jurisdiction. Moreover, since individuals are increasingly constituted as datafied subjects of suspicion, they are rendered governable through dispersed and opaque processes of evaluation. This constitutes a challenge for accountability and contestability because suspicion becomes more difficult to locate, explain or contest.

</details>


### [108] [Jumping the Line: Exploiting Length Predictions in LLM Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03430)
*Yuyang Dai,Rana Shahout,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper presents JIL, an adversarial suffix attack that biases output-length probes to subvert size-based LLM scheduling, achieves large reductions in predicted lengths and notable speedups, and shows coarse-grained prediction grouping is an effective mitigation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Size-based scheduling (e.g., SJF) reduces completion time but requires output-length estimates; attackers can manipulate these estimates to obtain higher priority and faster completion.

Method: Construct an adversarial suffix optimized to make a lightweight output-length probe (e.g., TRAIL) underestimate request length, evaluate across multiple LLMs, datasets and deployment settings, and test simple scheduler-side defenses (coarse interval grouping).

Result: Adversarial suffixes reduce predicted output lengths by up to 83.4%, produce average end-to-end speedups up to 1.53x, and induce a substantial mismatch between predicted and actual output sizes; grouping predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's benefit and lessens harm to benign requests.

Conclusion: JIL demonstrates that prediction-based size-aware LLM schedulers can be manipulated to gain scheduling priority; coarse-grained grouping of predicted lengths mitigates the attack.

Abstract: Efficient request scheduling is increasingly important for reducing completion time in large language model (LLM) serving. Size-based policies such as Shortest Job First prioritize shorter requests, but output lengths are unknown before generation, so practical schedulers rely on predicted lengths. We introduce JIL, an attack on prediction-based LLM schedulers that manipulates the scheduling signal to obtain higher priority and reduce completion time. Using TRAIL as a case study, JIL optimizes an adversarial suffix that causes a lightweight output-length probe to underestimate a request's length. We evaluate JIL on two datasets and four LLMs across varied request profiles and deployment configurations. JIL reduces predicted output lengths by up to 83.4 percent, and adversarial requests complete up to 1.53 times faster on average in end-to-end serving experiments. The reduction in predicted length is substantially larger than the change in actual output length, revealing a mismatch between the scheduler's estimate and the request's realized size. Response utility varies across models and tasks, exposing a trade-off between scheduling advantage and response quality. We also evaluate scheduler-side defenses and find that grouping length predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's scheduling advantage and mitigates delays to benign requests.

</details>


### [109] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 低监视器读数并不意味着已控制行为——监视器读数可能因位置不匹配或延迟承诺而掩盖报酬破解，必须用离带（out-of-band）的行为检测验证对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Post-training monitors and verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking; integrating monitors into the training objective is proposed but it is unclear whether low monitor scores indicate actual behavioral control.

Method: In a code-generation environment, train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties tied to how early the policy commits to its final answer. Analyze final readouts, seed variability, and text outputs to identify failure modes.

Result: Probe scores hit numerical floor early and trained-score medians are zero at endpoints, yet behavior ranged from low hacking share to near-pure hacking across seeds. The probe floor reflects a validation/reading-position mismatch; delayed commitment (prefix failure mode) can hide exploits past monitored cut points. Therefore offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of control.

Conclusion: Low monitor readouts do not prove behavioral control; offline probe/penalty scores can be low while policies still engage in reward hacking or delay committing to exploits. An out-of-band behavioral check is required.

Abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对CoT施加长度压力能提高推理效率，但通常以牺牲faithfulness为代价；monitorability更耐受这种压缩。不同的压缩策略和任务会产生不同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-thought提升可解释性但增加推理时长和成本；为节约tokens需训练模型用更短的CoT完成任务，但担心这会破坏CoT作为真实决策过程的可靠性。研究旨在弄清不同长度压缩方法何时以及如何影响CoT的真实度和可监测性。

Method: 作者对多种模型进行微调，使用三种不同施加长度压力的方法（固定生成预算、按示例的长度目标、以及群体相对长度奖励），并通过干预输入和相似输入测试两种CoT性质：faithfulness（CoT是否反映模型在相关输入上的决策过程）与monitorability（CoT是否揭示当输入被修改时输出如何变化）。

Result: 在大多数设置下，压缩CoT会降低faithfulness，主要由于减少了一致性；但monitorability在多种方法下保持相对稳健，模型仍会在短CoT中表明输入变更对答案的影响。不同施压方法和任务对影响大小有差异。

Conclusion: 在对生成长度施加压力以提高推理效率的设置下，模型的CoT解释在可解释性两个方面表现不同：可信度（faithfulness）通常下降，主要因为模型变得不一致；可监测性（monitorability）更为鲁棒，即使CoT大幅缩短，模型仍能在一定程度上承认输入变化对答案的影响。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [111] [Reasoning Models Are Accurate but Unsound on Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03519)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CERTID 提供了一个基于 ID 算法与精确 SCM 验证的形式化识别与评分流水线，用以生成和认证可识别/非可识别查询并严格评估大模型的因果识别可靠性，实验证明简单准确率难以反映错误主张风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前因果识别模型可能拒绝可识别查询或更严重地错误断言非可识别查询为可识别。现有评测缺乏经形式证明的非可识别查询与对等形式判定的评分机制，导致无法量化模型在“错误主张”上的风险。CERTID旨在提供可追溯、形式化与可验证的基准与评分流程。

Method: 使用图与查询生成受控实例；用ID算法判断可识别性并生成标准化的因果表达式；构造结构因果模型，使其干预分布已知以便精确验证候选公式；发展理论工具以减少结构泄露、修复非可识别查询并为评分建立保证；在1,200个经认证的实例（4–50个顶点）上评估三种前沿大模型（Gemini Flash/Pro, GPT5.5）。

Result: 构建了CERTID并公开了实例与验证器。评估显示：在相同实例上，不同模型在非可识别查询上的虚假主张率最高相差17倍；模型在训练后快照生成的图上做可识别性判定时准确率为97–100%。

Conclusion: CERTID 构建了一个形式化的可证识别 (identification) 流水线，能够用完全且正确的因果识别算法（ID）证明某个因果效应是否可从给定的观测数据和图中识别，并通过在结构因果模型（SCM）上精确已知的干预分布来验证模型输出的公式，从而解决以往评估中缺乏可证非可识别实例与基于字符串匹配评分的局限。实验表明准确率并不能反映方法的“无错主张”能力：在非可识别查询上不同模型的错误主张率相差甚远。

Abstract: A reasoning model asked whether a causal effect is recoverable from observational data can fail in two ways: it refuses an identifiable query or answers a nonidentifiable one. The latter is more consequential, as no observational data can validate the claimed formula. Measuring this failure requires queries that are provably non-identifiable, which prior evaluations lack, and grading that accepts correct formulas in any equivalent form, which string matching cannot provide. We build CERTID, a formal identification pipeline that addresses both limitations. CERTID uses the sound and complete causal identification algorithm ID to certify whether an effect is identifiable from a given graph and query, and verifies returned formulas against structural causal models whose interventional distributions are known exactly. CERTID further develops theoretical results to mitigate structural leakage, repair non-identifiable queries, and establish grading guarantees. We evaluate three frontier reasoning models (Gemini Flash, Gemini Pro, and GPT5.5) on 1,200 certified instances spanning 4 to 50 vertices. Accuracy proves a poor proxy for soundness: on identical instances, the false-claim rate on non-identifiable queries varies by seventeen-fold across models. We also find that models decide identifiability with 97-100% accuracy on graphs generated after the strongest model's training snapshot. Instances, the certification procedure, the verifier, and per-instance records are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/certid-D718.

</details>


### [112] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对金融生产数据库提出结合动态概念查找、模板检索与外键路径剪枝的 Text-to-SQL 系统（FLINT），显著提升在不透明键与深层模式下的执行准确率，并已投入生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 Text-to-SQL 在学术基准（如 Spider、BIRD）表现良好，但在金融生产库中失败，原因是概念用不透明的整型键存储、模式更深、查询需多次 join 与针对 ID 的过滤谓词。需要域专化机制来映射自然语言到这些不透明标识并缩小搜索空间。

Method: 三大核心模块：1) 动态“查找代理”将自然语言概念解析为针对性参考表约束；2) 基于嵌入的相似结构查询模板检索，从紧凑的专家编写模板库中复用模板；3) 基于外键链的模式关联（schema linking）通过遍历外键关系剪枝大模式到相关子集，而非仅用名称相似度。

Result: 在两个数据集共 359 个问题上评测，FLINT 在同样 LLM 的条件下优于多种最先进基线，并已在一个金融数据检索服务中投产。

Conclusion: FLINT 有效弥合了通用 Text-to-SQL 模型在生产金融数据库上的性能差距，通过领域专化组件提高查询正确率并已部署于生产环境。

Abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [113] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce task-harness co-evolution (RSI) that jointly updates generation harness and tasks via online and post-task self-improvement to produce progressively harder reasoning datasets that improve downstream model performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but keeps the construction harness unchanged, which limits the ability to produce progressively harder problems as task distribution evolves. The paper aims to adapt the harness alongside tasks to escalate difficulty and produce valuable training data.

Method: Task-harness co-evolution framework called Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RSI). Two update schedules: (1) Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation; (2) Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria are held fixed.

Result: Over fourteen evolution rounds across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy dropped from 100.0% to 54.8%, indicating increased task difficulty. Ablation experiments show the combination of online and post-task updates yields harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. Downstream benefits include improved SFT and GRPO; specifically, a 27B model fine-tuned on 10K synthesized math examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references.

Conclusion: Adapting both task generation and harness (prompts, skills, workflows) via recursive self-improvement yields progressively harder reasoning problems useful for downstream training; combining online and post-task updates outperforms a fixed harness or either update alone.

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [114] [Knowledge or Calculator? Decomposing the Skill Premium in Verifiable Financial Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03564)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinSkillBench显示：为金融代理提供预制技能包能显著提升性能；工具贡献大于文档；技能溢价是模型—资源—挂钩系统的共同性质，而非模型单独特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI不能仅靠事实检索：投资工作流要求精确的数量计算、可靠的程序性资源使用与可审计的结构化输出，需要系统性评估模型在这些技能上的实际能力。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench基准：2,603个时间点情景、12个细分子任务（组合构建、风险管理、基本面分析），使用可再生的隐藏标准答案与确定性验证器；在9个模型、3种资源条件下执行17,820次情景，采用配对分析（主要比较8个模型）并用簇引导法与第二实现的评测挂钩做复现。

Result: 经策划的技能包平均将得分从0.366提高到0.528（+16.2点）；在单次对话内生成技能仅增+0.5点且消耗更多token/回合；单独提供文档增+5.6点，单独提供可执行工具增+19.5点，两者叠加呈次可加性；工具在数值密集型任务中主导，文档在程序性或输出格式受限时更重要，两者对解释性任务都有增益；效果在10种评分变体和簇引导分析中符号稳定，第二个挂钩实现重现了方向性但幅度依赖工具/数据暴露方式。

Conclusion: 在金融代理中，经过策划的“技能包”显著提升了模型执行复杂投资工作流的能力，但这种“技能溢价”是模型、资源（文档/工具）与评测挂钩系统共同决定的属性，而不是模型本身的单一特征。

Abstract: Financial AI agents must do more than retrieve facts: investment workflows require correct quantitative execution, reliable use of procedural resources, and auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite of 2,603 point in time episodes across 12 subtasks in portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, with hidden regenerable ground truth and task specific deterministic verifiers. Executing 17,820 episodes across 9 models and 3 resource conditions, the paired analysis across 8 models shows that curated skill packages raise mean scores by +16.2 points (0.366 to 0.528), whereas skills generated within a single episode add only +0.5 points while consuming more tokens and turns. We then decompose the curated premium by granting human authored procedural documents and executable domain tools separately: documents alone add +5.6 points, tools alone add +19.5 points, and their combination is subadditive. The premium is strongly workflow dependent: executable tools dominate numerically intensive workflows, documentation matters more when procedural or output schema guidance is the bottleneck, and interpretive tasks benefit from both. The effects are sign stable across 10 scoring variants and cluster bootstrap analyses, and an independently implemented second harness reproduces the directional pattern while showing that effect magnitudes depend on how tools and data are exposed. Overall, a measured "skill premium" is a property of the full model, resource, and harness system rather than of the underlying model alone.

</details>


### [115] [Learning to Assess Heartbeat Observability for mmWave Heart-Rate Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03570)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Jianfei Yang,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HEAR使用可控FMCW仿真自动标注心跳可观测性，训练双任务Transformer实现可观测性评分与心率估计，仿真训练零样本迁移至实测数据后显著提升选择性心率预测准确性并保证边缘实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mmWave无接触心率感知中，由于多个散射体相干叠加同一宏观几何下心跳分量可能被部分或完全抑制，导致心率估计不可靠。需要在测量层直接评估“可观测性”以实现选择性估计、降低错误和资源浪费。

Method: 构建可控多散射体FMCW仿真器，通过检测谱中主心跳带峰与已知心率的一致性自动生成可观测性标签；设计紧凑双任务Transformer，输入为谱幅值与相对于呼吸基频的频率（用于提供呼吸谐波上下文），输出可观测性分数与心率估计；模型仅用仿真数据训练并零样本迁移到60/120 GHz真实数据集。

Result: 在两套公开数据（60 GHz与120 GHz，共134名受试者）上实现无监督迁移；用学得的可观测性分数做选择性预测能显著提升精度：在120 GHz数据上，按分数筛选至50%覆盖率时HEAR的MAE由17.9 BPM降至1.6 BPM；该分数也能提高其他估计器的表现；端到端在边缘设备上的处理延迟为50.8 ms。

Conclusion: 本文提出基于仿真训练的可观测性评估方法（HEAR），能从mmWave相位谱预测心跳可读性并与心率估计联合进行选择性预测；在零样本迁移到真实数据上显著提高选择性心率估计精度并满足边缘实时性要求。

Abstract: Contactless heart-rate sensing with millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires assessing whether individual measurements support reliable estimation. We study learning to assess heartbeat observability, defined as the readability of the heartbeat component in an acquired phase spectrum, for selective heart-rate estimation. Coherent superposition of scatterer returns can suppress this component even under similar macroscopic observation geometry, motivating assessment directly from acquired measurements. To obtain training supervision across different observability conditions, we develop a controllable multi-scatterer frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) simulator. Agreement between the dominant heartbeat-band peak and the known heart rate provides an automatic observability label for each simulated measurement. We propose HEAR (Heartbeat Estimation with Assessed Reliability), a compact dual-task Transformer that jointly predicts an observability score and heart rate. Its input combines spectral magnitudes with frequencies relative to the respiration fundamental, providing context for respiratory harmonics. Trained solely on simulated observations, HEAR transfers zero-shot to two public real-world datasets collected at 60 and 120 GHz from 134 subjects. The same learned score supports selective prediction with both HEAR's own heart-rate head and multiple existing estimators. On the 120 GHz dataset, score-based selection reduces the HR head's mean absolute error from 17.9 BPM at full coverage to 1.6 BPM at 50% coverage. The complete pipeline achieves an end-to-end processing latency of 50.8 ms on an edge device. Project page: https://yuxuanhu9.github.io/HEAR/.

</details>


### [116] [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574)
*Alham Fikri Aji,Faiz Rizki Ramadhan,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Seung Hun Eddie Han,Ryandito Diandaru,Qinrong Cui,Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Badrinath Chandana,Peerawat Chomphooyod,Ahmed Attia,Jonibek Mansurov,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Canh Duong Nguyen,Imran Turganov,Minghao Wu,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Irina Nikishina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperBrowseComp 是一个含423道题、覆盖13种语言且多模态的高难度网页浏览基准，问题被专门设计以要求在开放网络上发现并连接稀疏或异构证据；使用多种检索设置进行评估并辅以人工评估，旨在推动持久信息检索能力的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个更具挑战性的基准来评估模型在真实开放网络、多语言和多模态证据链发现与连结方面的能力，避免仅依赖模型内部参数知识解题。

Method: 构建包含423道由母语或高水平使用者手工撰写并人工验证的问题，覆盖13种语言；问题设计为要求定位难以获得的证据、跨步骤线索推理或检查视频、扫描文档、图像、地图等异构来源；通过让离线模型筛去容易题目以降低参数化知识可解性；使用供应商原生搜索与共享外部检索套件在统一代理协议下评估多种模型，并对部分题目进行人工评估以背景化模型表现与搜索代价。

Result: 提出并发布了 HyperBrowseComp 基准，展示了该基准在多语种与多模态证据发现上的挑战性；通过自动化评测与人工评估，表明当前模型在此类持久检索任务上仍存在明显困难（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: HyperBrowseComp 提供了一个高难度、多语种、多模态的网页浏览基准，用于检验模型在开放网络上发现并连接非显性证据的能力，揭示了当前基于检索的代理在持久信息检索任务中的不足。

Abstract: We introduce HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual and multimodal browsing benchmark comprising 423 manually authored and human-validated questions across 13 languages, written by native or highly proficient speakers. Questions are designed to be extremely challenging. Each question targets a concise, publicly verifiable answer whose discovery requires locating obscure evidence, following multi-step clue chains, or inspecting heterogeneous sources such as videos, scanned documents, images, or maps. Easier questions are filtered out by evaluating them with models without internet access to reduce the likelihood that they can be answered with parametric knowledge alone. We evaluate several models using provider-native search and a shared external retrieval harness under a common agent protocol. To contextualize model performance and effort, we also conduct a human evaluation on a sample of the questions. HyperBrowseComp provides a challenging testbed for persistent information seeking across languages and evidence modalities, with difficulty arising from discovering and connecting evidence on the open web.

</details>


### [117] [HazardWeaver: Scientific Route Selection for Hazard Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03591)
*Wangshu Zhu,Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HazardWeaver 通过将方法适用性（证据驱动）与能力可执行性（输入/输出兼容）分别建模，并由代理基于两者选择与修正分析路径，显著提升了自动化危害分析在多路线场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害风险分析需要将科学数据、模型与工具自动整合为可执行流程，但真实场景下哪些方法既适用又可执行会随可得证据与执行结果而变化，现有代理缺乏对方法适用性与可执行性进行连贯判定与动态修正的机制。

Method: 将问题形式化为状态依赖的科学路径选择；构建 Hazard Knowledge Compiler 从文本/证据中提取与方法适用性相关的条件；用 Hazard Capability Graph 表示可执行的科学能力及其输入/输出兼容性；由 Hazard Weaver Agent 基于上述表示挑选可适用且可执行的路径、执行工作流并在获取新证据或执行结果后修正决策。并用专门的基准（141 个实例，覆盖 7 个单一危害域与 4 类多危害交互）评估系统在输出正确性、路径有效性与合理回避上的表现。

Result: 在 HazardWeaver 基准上，HazardWeaver 超越现有代理系统，尤其在存在多个可选科学路径的任务中增益明显；代码公开。基准评估了不仅输出正确性也包括路径选择的有效性和合理放弃行为。

Conclusion: HazardWeaver 提出并实现了一种面向“状态依赖的科学路径选择”的框架，使得 AI 代理能基于证据和可执行性约束在多种科学方法间选择、执行并随状态变化修正决策，从而在多路线情形下显著提升危害分析表现。

Abstract: Understanding and assessing natural hazards is essential for disaster preparedness and risk reduction. Recent advances in large language models have spurred growing interest in AI agents for hazard analysis, particularly their ability to integrate scientific data, models, and tools into automated workflows. However, effective automation requires agents to determine which scientific methods are appropriate for a given event and executable with the available data and tools. As new evidence and execution results become available, these conditions can change, requiring agents to reconsider their choices. We formulate this problem as state-dependent scientific route selection and introduce HazardWeaver. Specifically, HazardWeaver first leverages the Hazard Knowledge Compiler to extract evidence-linked conditions governing scientific applicability, then its Hazard Capability Graph represents executable scientific capabilities and checks compatibility between their inputs and outputs. Using these complementary representations, the Hazard Weaver Agent component selects applicable and executable routes, carries out their workflows, and revises its decisions as the analysis state changes. To evaluate both the scientific outputs and the decisions that produce them, we introduce the Hazard Weaver Benchmark, comprising 141 instances across seven single-hazard domains and four multi-hazard interaction classes. The benchmark accommodates multiple valid scientific routes and evaluates output correctness, route validity, and justified abstention. Extensive experiments on this benchmark show that HazardWeaver outperforms existing agent systems, with the largest gains on tasks with multiple eligible scientific routes. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LabRAI/HazardWeaver.

</details>


### [118] [Low-Cost Video--Time Priors as a Strong Baseline for EEG--fNIRS Emotion Regression on Familiar Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03618)
*Minghao Kong,Jiurun Chen,Ying Gao,Xiangbin Meng,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 视频-时间先验是熟悉视频情感回归的强基线，EEG/fNIRS可作可选的残差校正，但改进有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在观看已知视频时，是否存在一个简单且稳定的视频-时间先验能很好地预测情感轨迹，以及生理信号（EEG+fNIRS）能否显著改进该先验的预测性能。

Method: 采用连续情感回归（对时刻的valence和arousal建模），在熟悉视频部署中使用参与者特定估计并以“先验主导的固定融合(prior-dominating fixed fusion)”来测试生理信号是否提供残差校正；进行了源显式（source-explicit）的消融分析，并在五折受试者留出（subject-held-out）评估及内部/外部评测上报告MAE差异。

Result: 在五折受试者留出评估中（文本中提到的“24”未明确是参与者还是视频），视频时间先验与融合方法的MAE差距在内部评估约0.05，外部评估约0.32。消融结果表明视频身份和视频内时间占据主要贡献，EEG-fNIRS带来的增益较小且随参与者与视频而异。

Conclusion: 本文结论为：在熟悉视频（familiar-video）场景下，基于视频-时间(video-time)的先验是一个强且低成本的基线，而将EEG和fNIRS作为残差校正信号只带来小且对参与者与视频差异较大的改进，因此可作为可选的补充信号而非必要提升手段。

Abstract: Continuous emotion regression estimates moment-to-moment valence and arousal while a viewer watches a video. In familiar-video deployment, responses fron training participant-specific estimate, and prior-dominating fixed fusion tests whether physiology adds residual correction. In five-fold subject-held-out evaluation on 24was within 0.05 and 0.32 MAE of fusion in the internal and external evaluations, respectively. Source-explicit ablations showed that video identity and within-video tine accounted for most of the reduction, while EG-FNIRS gains were smaller and varied across participants and videos. These results identify the video-time prior as a strong, low-cost baseline and position EEG-fNIRS as an optional residual signal for familiar-video emotion regression.

</details>


### [119] [Depth as Time in One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03626)
*Arnold Caleb Asiimwe,William Yang,Sanghyuk Chun,Esin Tureci,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一步生成将扩散的时间维计算重新映射到网络深度（“depth as time”），这使得某些流图（尤其是时间条件化的MeanFlow）在中间层可恢复出去噪轨迹，并因而可高度压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着一步生成模型在图像质量上的进步，研究者欲弄清多步扩散在被压缩为单步前向时，其原有的去噪时间轨迹是否被保留、改变或消失，以及这对模型可压缩性与内部表征意味着什么。

Method: 通过将模型自身的输出头应用于中间层来探测中间表示（layerwise decoding），在不同传输区间下对多步到一步的流图进行实验（包括MeanFlow和非时间索引的drifting模型），并进一步将层间计算视为流来训练一个单一的时间条件化块以验证压缩与去噪能力。

Result: 发现所谓“depth as time”现象：多步扩散的去噪计算在单次前向传播中沿网络深度展开，可由模型输出头在中间层恢复；该现象依赖于训练要解决的传输任务，MeanFlow在短传输区间探测时表现出去噪与再噪；不带时间索引的生成器（如drifting）不具备该深度去噪特性。通过将层间计算明确视为流并训练单个时间条件块，成功将MeanFlow SiT-L/2模型参数压缩约16.6×，同时保留去噪能力。

Conclusion: 一步生成并未消除扩散模型的时间维计算，而是将其重新组织到网络深度中；在具有时间索引的流地图（如MeanFlow）中，层间可恢复出去噪-再噪的轨迹，并因此具有更强的层级压缩性（实验证明可将SiT-L/2的MeanFlow模型在参数上压缩约16.6×）。

Abstract: The recent wave of one-step generative models, which compress the multi-step trajectory of diffusion via either distillation or learned flow maps, has reached an inflection point where they can generate high-quality images. Here, we ask a natural question that follows from these advances: what happens to the denoising trajectory of multi-step diffusion when generation is compressed into a single forward pass? We offer an empirical observation we call \textit{depth as time}: the denoising computation that multi-step diffusion performs across sampling steps appears to unfold across the depth of a single forward pass, and can be recovered by decoding intermediate layers with the model's own output head. Most interestingly, we show that this depthwise computation depends on the transport task a flow map is trained to solve. The most surprising case is MeanFlow, where probing shorter transport intervals reveals both denoising and renoising within a single network evaluation. In contrast, generators trained without a time-indexed transport task, such as drifting models, do not exhibit the same depthwise denoising. Consequently, we show that models that exhibit the depthwise denoising phenomenon are more compressible across the layerwise computation: a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model can be compressed by $16.6\times$ in parameters into a single time-conditioned block. We offer an explanation for this denoise-then-renoise behavior and show that, when we treat the layerwise computation explicitly as a flow, a single time-conditioned block can be trained to denoise across layers, compressing a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model by $16.6\times$ in parameters. Together, these results suggest that the temporal computation of diffusion is not eliminated by one-step generation, but reorganized across network depth.

</details>


### [120] [NeutronGym: Physics-Graded Neutron Instrument Design for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03631)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeutronGym is an executable, well-graded benchmark for neutron instrument design; RL training substantially improves LLM agents on held-out design instances, but frontier models remain superior and careful grading (partial credit) is essential.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To create a benchmark that forces language models to perform algorithmic/physics reasoning in instrument design rather than recall of texts, enabled by an executable simulator and unambiguous, modular grading.

Method: Built NeutronGym: an executable environment where agents propose instrument designs verified by McStas ray-tracing. Grading uses a level-resolved ladder that checks syntax, runtime, structure and science without relying on an LLM judge. Procedural families generate unlimited instances with held-out parameter regimes; McStasBench provides 16 curated tasks from published instruments plus memorization probes. Trained agents via reinforcement learning on environment reward (Qwen3-8B and others), compared against zero-shot baselines, classical optimizers (when given closed-form physics), and frontier models. Ablations include removing ladder partial credit and testing seeds.

Result: Zero-shot models solve up to 7/16 tasks; none retrieve references or meet improvement targets. RL raises Qwen3-8B success on a gated family from 11% to 77% (69% at another seed), outperforming an untrained larger model (Qwen3-32B) on that family. The ladder’s partial-credit grading is critical (removal collapses scores by ~60 points). Against a classical optimizer given closed-form physics, the RL agent matches performance within the agent’s budget (77% vs 81%); best-in-class models still reach 98–99%. Four task designs needed to be failed to obtain trustworthy results, and the authors released those probes.

Conclusion: NeutronGym demonstrates that executable, rigorously graded environments can meaningfully test whether language-model agents perform physics-informed instrument design (not just memorization). With an automated, level-resolved grading ladder and McStas ray-tracing, RL training substantially improves agent performance on held-out design parameters, though top domain models still outperform the trained agents and some tasks remain unsolved.

Abstract: Designing a scientific instrument tests whether language-model agents can do physics rather than recall it, provided the grading cannot be argued with. We introduce NeutronGym, to our knowledge the first executable environment for neutron instrument design: agents build instruments through validating tools, McStas ray-traces what they build, and a level-resolved ladder grades syntax, runtime, structure and science with no LLM judge. Procedural families supply unlimited instances of a fixed layout whose design parameters the agent must set, with held-out parameter regimes; a curated slice, McStasBench, adds 16 tasks from published instruments behind memorization probes and a sandbox. Seven models reproduce at most 7 of the 16, none retrieves a reference, and none meets an improvement target. The environment also trains. Reinforcement learning on its reward takes Qwen3-8B from 11% to 77% of held-out instances of a family whose targets come from a hidden design (69% at a second seed), past an untrained Qwen3-32B, and the recipe holds, at one seed each, on three further gated families. The analysis says what that gain is. Without the ladder's partial credit it collapses by 60 points. From reward alone the trained model reaches what a classical optimizer reaches, at the agent's simulation budget, only when handed the closed-form physics (77% against 81%, a gap that does not separate at this size), while frontier models still solve 98-99%. Getting a trustworthy result meant failing four task designs that no-model baselines could solve, and we release the probes that found them.

</details>


### [121] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于执行读写依赖图的归因方法（DepGPO），通过向后追溯验证器关注资源并对相关读写路径分配信用，进而改善多步终端任务的策略优化，实验证明能提升性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端型多步任务中后续命令依赖于前面命令产生的信息或中间结果，而现有的轨迹级或步级归因方法未显式追踪读写依赖，导致不相关操作也被赋予训练信号，从而削弱了对关键步骤的学习。

Method: 从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图（命令节点、读/写边），从任务验证器检查的资源向后追溯依赖路径，对路径上的写操作及其支持的读操作分配奖励/优势重分配，随后在策略优化过程中使用这些依赖感知的分配来更新策略（即依赖感知的Group Policy Optimization）。

Result: 通过大量比较实验和消融研究，作者证明DepGPO在复杂终端任务上能提高成功率/任务得分并降低训练方差，表现出更好的性能和稳定性。

Conclusion: DepGPO通过显式追踪命令间的读写依赖来改进端终端任务的归因，从而更准确地将训练信号分配到与最终结果相关的步骤，提升了任务性能和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Do Large Language Models Know Colombian Law? A Reliability Benchmark for the Colombian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03639)
*Rubén Manrique,Michelle Castellanos,Jorge Morales,Juan David Gutiérrez,Antonio Barreto Rozo,Joaquín Vélez Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发布了覆盖哥伦比亚法律的专家验证基准并评测 15 个模型，发现尽管多模型在选择题上效果不错，但自由文本事实正确性很低（≤0.45），模型常表现出“可读性高但内容不实”的风险，需要专家监督与基于权威来源的落地方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 LLM 在法律领域应用增多，但对美国以外国家（此处为哥伦比亚）司法体系下的可靠性评估缺失；需要可复现的基准来量化模型在具体国家法域中的表现。

Method: 构建并发布一个专家验证的基准：1,042 道题目，覆盖十个法律领域、三种题型（封闭选择题、半开放题、开放式 IRAC），通过人机循环、多轮专家复核生成；用格式相适配的指标评估 15 个当代专有与开源模型，并用盲测人类专家与基于评估量表的 LLM 评审器做复现验证。

Result: 封闭题准确率在 0.905（Gemini 3.1 Pro）到 0.577 之间；但自由文本答案的事实正确率（0–1）任一模型均不超过 0.45。答案相关性与事实正确性呈显著负相关（Spearman ρ = -0.46），即模型“听起来合理”但常错。封闭题准确率与自由文本正确性排名高度相关（ρ = 0.94），因此选择题能预测模型排序但高估绝对可靠性。基于量表的 LLM 评审器与盲人类专家对自由文本排名复现良好（ρ ≥ 0.88）。模型引用的规范约有一半是错误或不存在。可靠性在法律领域间系统性差异明显，并随问题复杂度呈倒 U 型变化。

Conclusion: 当前主流大语言模型在哥伦比亚法律任务上仍不足以独立使用，需要法律专家监督；以权威来源为基础（grounding）是提升可靠性的有希望方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support legal practice, education, and research, yet their reliability in national legal systems outside the United States remains largely undocumented. We introduce an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLM reliability on the Colombian legal system. The benchmark comprises 1,042 items spanning ten areas of law and three question formats (closed multiple-choice, semi-open, and open-ended IRAC), built through a human-in-the-loop pipeline with multi-stage expert review. We evaluate 15 contemporary proprietary and open-weight models with format-appropriate metrics. Accuracy on closed questions ranges widely, from 0.905 (Gemini 3.1 Pro) to 0.577, but on free-text legal answers factual correctness never exceeds 0.45 (on a 0-1 scale) for any model. We find a dissociation between answer relevancy and correctness (Spearman rho = -0.46): models reliably sound responsive while frequently being wrong, a pattern of particular concern for non-expert users. Closed-question accuracy and free-text correctness are strongly rank-correlated (rho = 0.94), so cheap multiple-choice screening predicts model ranking but overstates absolute reliability. An independent rubric-based LLM judge and blind human expert scoring both reproduce the free-text ranking (rho >= 0.88). The judge further reveals that only about half of the norms models cite are correct; the rest are wrong or non-existent. Reliability varies systematically by legal area and follows an inverted-U across question complexity.
